# Results ledger — Cubic closed bosonic SFT (Hubbard–Stratonovich)

Established results of the project and the hypotheses they rest on, one `###` entry each, so that they are reused rather than re-derived.
Converted from a table on 2026-09-26 and completed against the sources the same day (`agent/state/ledger-completion-2026-09-26.md`).

## Hypotheses

Motivated hypotheses tracked as such, rather than carried as open problems on individual rows (rigor audit
2026-09-21, `knowledge/assessments/rigor-audit-2026-09-21.md`). Statements, motivations and falsifiers are
taken from the batch reports `agent/reports/planner-rigor-{A,B,C,C2}-*.md` as corrected by
`agent/reports/critic-rigor-audit-20260921T140000.md`.

### PH1 (PH-a) — $m_2$ invertible on the truncated quotient by $\ker V_4$, kernels stable [supported – R53, level 0]

- **Scope**: the support covers level 0 only: the tachyon-pair block under the Euclidean prescription (PH25) and PH29, PH30, where $\ker K_P(s)=\{0\}$ at every real $s$ (the sufficient stand-in, form (β) of R51, for clause (ii)); the on-shell point is covered because R53 holds at every real $s$. Open: levels $>0$ (where $G_2$ is indefinite and the level-0 positivity mechanism does not carry over), the stability clause in $L$, complex $s$ (PH18).
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-09-25 – node check – critic on 050 §18c.4, node C3 (`agent/reports/critic-050-c3-20260925T115500.md`; orchestrator re-run `codes/verify-kp-real-s.py --only c4` 15/15 PASS) – all nodes hold; R53: $\operatorname{sign}(C)\,K_P(s)$ strictly positive definite at every real $s$, hence no null vector at level 0 (Conditional(PH25, PH29, PH30) – Proven)
  - 2026-09-26 – node check – critic on 050 Addendum C5–C5.3 (`agent/reports/critic-050-onshell-node-20260926T052500.md`) – all four nodes hold; bears on R29's on-shell-limit clause and on PH3; for PH1 only the wording 'on a real neighbourhood' (050 Addendum (4)), level-0 support unchanged
- **Motivation**: algebraic and true at every finite level; the level-0 block is invertible (R15); level truncation is the working definition of closed- and open-SFT computations and has converged in every case where it has been tested (Moeller–Taylor, hep-th/0002237); $\ker V_4$ is a zero-action decoupled spectator (R9/R13, the 2026-07-01 annotation in R13), so quotienting it changes no amplitude at any level. For the on-shell clause: PH5 — no selection rule forces a kernel, an accidental one is codimension one in the vertex family — with its 'genericity over families is a remedy, not evidence' caveat and its off-shell, generic-family scope.
- **Would be falsified by**: a kernel that does not stabilise: new null vectors of $V_4^{(L)}$ appearing at arbitrarily high level and not lying in $\ker V_4$, so that the level-by-level quotients have no limit; or a zero of $\det V_4^{(L)}(P^2)$ on the four-tachyon shell persisting as $L$ grows (PH18). **Not a falsifier:** the drift to zero of the smallest nonzero singular value of the level-truncated $m_2=G_2^{-1}V_4$ — basis- and frame-dependent, retained only as a numerical conditioning diagnostic (023 §1).
- **Used by**: R10, R14, R17, R18, R19, R20, R21, R22, R24, R25, R26, R27, R29, R34, R35, R41, R45, R46, R57, R60

$m_2$ descends to an injective map on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0/\ker V_4$, and at each truncation level the descended map is invertible there, so that $m_2^{-1}$ is defined on the truncated quotient and $m_2^{-1}m_2=\mathrm{id}$; the kernels are **stable**: $\ker V_4^{(L)}=\ker V_4\cap\{\text{level}\le L\}$ for $L$ large enough at the external data considered (same for $\tilde M_k$ on $\operatorname{Sym}^k$), so that the quotient, the $\Sigma$-propagator and the homotopy $h$ defined level by level have a limit; and on a real neighbourhood of the external momenta used, the on-shell locus included, the truncated determinant on the quotient has no zero persisting as the level grows. The $\Sigma$ Gaussian integral on the quotient is understood at the formal level of any SFT path integral. **No auxiliary inner product, no closed range, no bounded inverse** (restated 2026-09-22 on Harold's decision; the earlier wording fixed the positive-definite Fock-space inner product of the oscillator basis — that form is neither Lorentz- nor BRST-invariant, and the four-point argument never used a bounded inverse).

**Annotation (2026-09-26).** Clause labels, as the records use them (049 l. 34, l. 114; 050 §18c.4; R51, R53, PH25): **(i)** the kernels are stable as the level grows; **(ii)** on a real neighbourhood of the external momenta, the on-shell point included, the truncated determinant on the quotient has no zero persisting as the level grows (at level 0 read in the sufficient form $\ker K_P(s)=0$, R51). The base clause – $m_2$ descends to an injective map on the quotient, invertible there at each truncation level – carries no label.

**Annotation (2026-10-01).** Supported at level (1,1), zero momentum, by R68: $A=V_4\vert_{\mathrm{Sym}^2\{T,D\}}$ invertible with inertia $(2,1)$ at both $\beta$ (record 064; a data point for the base clause, not a proof).

**Annotation (2026-10-20).** Scope line: add "the range clause: 'invertible on the quotient' as the paper uses it from the quintic order on is $v_p\in\mathrm{ran}\,K_p$ (record 104 C1.5), which an empty kernel does not give; open"; stabilisation clause in kernel form: record 104 (6.2). (synthesis, closing assessment of 2026-10-18) From 106 C5: norm convergence of the truncations gives nothing on stabilization in either reading. Harold, 2026-10-20: the paper assumes the weak reading of the stabilization – the quotient and the propagator have a limit as the level grows, at generic momentum.

### PH2 (b) (PH-a′, replaces PH2; part (a) established by R70) — A presentation in which every null vector of $\tilde M_k$, $k\ge3$, is a joint null vector, and the $T_6$ clause holds [open]

- **Motivation**: Off the Veronese diagonal, $\tilde M_k$ ($k\ge3$) and the source blocks are presentation freedom (R26 (4); 019 §4 point 3). An accidental kernel of $\tilde M_k$ is a codimension-one condition on that freedom and is expected to be removable by a Veronese-null change of parent (061 §7.4: expected, not shown) – the mechanism of PH5's stub deformation, acting on a presentation choice rather than on the vertices; the $T_6$ clause is presentation freedom only off $\operatorname{Sym}^6\mathcal H_0\cap(\mathcal S_3\otimes\ker V_4\otimes\mathcal H_0)$ (vacuous at level 0, R53). At level 0, $\ker V_4=0$ (R53), so (3.8) forces nothing upward from $k=2$. Failing (b), 034's remedy (a) – multiplier pairs on $\ker\tilde M_k/N_{k,\infty}$ (hybrid Route C: cotangent pairs, exact Gaussians with no inverse) – is the known fallback, expected to preserve R26's Schur structure (034 §5.2, not verified).
- **Would be falsified by**: A level-0 null vector of $\tilde M_3$ on which $T_7(\cdot,\cdot,\Psi)$, $L_4(\Psi)$ or $L_3(\Psi)^\top$ does not vanish, in every admissible presentation; or $\ker V_4\ne0$ with the forced diagonal non-zero on $\operatorname{Sym}^6\mathcal H_0\cap(\mathcal S_3\otimes\ker V_4\otimes\mathcal H_0)$. Signature: a non-polynomial term in an effective vertex, at $n=6,7$ for block-symmetric parents and $n=4,5$ otherwise (061 C1.7, C1.10).
- **Used by**: R21, R25, R26, R35 (R21 and R25 in their named presentations, see the qualifier in the statement), R62 ($k=4$ only: $\tilde M$ at level 3, $g=0$, invertible on $\operatorname{Sym}^3$ modulo its kernel; the source condition $\tilde u\in\operatorname{im}\tilde M$ is R62's scope condition)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-01 – part (a), the reduction of PH2 to PH1 (at $k=2$), PH5's $V_5$ clause, the $T_6$ clause and this statement at $k\ge3$, established by R70 (record 061, critic iteration 2: every node holds); the existing PH2 entry carried no verification lines
  - the statement itself is not established: expected, not shown (061 §7.4)

In the presentation used (R21: the block composite $\tilde M=M-\tfrac65\Phi^\top V_4^{-1}\Phi$; R25: scheme B's canonical kernels; R26, R35: at least one presentation of R26's shared-parent class), for every $k\ge3$, every null vector of $\tilde M_k=T_{2k}\rvert_{(k|k)}$ is annihilated by every Jacobi block in which $\Sigma_k$ appears ($\ker\tilde M_k=N_{k,\infty}$). If $\ker V_4\ne0$ and $K\ge3$, the same presentation must also satisfy $T_6(\cdot_3;\ker V_4,\Psi)=0$. This is a condition on the $(3|2,1)$ block of the parent, satisfiable only if the forced diagonal vanishes on $\operatorname{Sym}^6\mathcal H_0\cap(\mathcal S_3\otimes\ker V_4\otimes\mathcal H_0)$, a condition on $V_6$ there given R38 (e.g. $V_6(\chi^6)=0$ for $\chi\otimes\chi\in\ker V_4$), which 034's (C$^{(6)}$) implies. For block-symmetric parents $\operatorname{Sym}(N_{k-1,\infty}\otimes\mathcal H_0)\subseteq\ker\tilde M_k$ is automatic, and the hypothesis requires it to lie in $N_{k,\infty}$. First instance: $\tilde M_3$ at level 0 (momenta fixed or discretised). For a block-symmetric parent, a kernel with a simple pole enters the value first at $n=7$, and at $n=6$ if $T_7(n,n,\cdot)\equiv0$; for other parents already at $n=5$ or $n=4$; in each case generically as a term rational in $\Psi$ (061 C1.7, C1.10).

Part (a), the reduction of the former PH2 to PH1, PH5 and the statement above, is R70. The neighbourhood clause of the former PH2 is automatic at fixed $(L,K)$ and unused by R26 (R70, C1.5). Former PH2 text, for the record: "the nested kernels $\hat H_k(g,\Psi)$ of the dressed tower are invertible on the quotient level by level with a stable kernel (PH1 as restated 2026-09-22) in a neighbourhood of $g=0$, one condition per level (019 l.299–301: strictly extends PH1)".

### PH3 (PH-b) — Completeness and convergence of the $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ two-string state sum on vectors $m_2P$ [supported – R55, level 0]

- **Scope**: the support covers the momentum part of the sum on the level-0 tachyon-pair block, in the eigenbasis form (iii) of R51. This form is sufficient for the momentum-basis statement on $m_2P$, not equivalent to it. It holds at every real external pair and uniformly on compacts of the Euclidean on-shell region (050 Addendum (A.6)), which gives the truncation interchange. It is conditional on PH25 and PH29, PH30. Open: the sum over strand levels, levels $>0$, complex $s$.
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-09-26 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-050-full-20260926T003000.md` on 050 §18c.8, node C4 – REVISE mechanical (Dini/continuity order, missing PH3 interpretation paragraph, scope of the trace check); C1, C2 ACCEPT
  - 2026-09-26 – numerical check – `codes/verify-kp-real-s.py --only c8` 32/32 PASS after the fix pass `agent/reports/computator-050-fix-20260926T020500.md`, orchestrator re-run identical – R55 promoted: by Mercer, the eigen-expansion of $K_P(s)$ converges absolutely at every real $s$ (Conditional(PH25, PH29, PH30) – Proven)
  - 2026-09-26 – node check – `agent/reports/critic-050-onshell-node-20260926T052500.md` on 050 Addendum C5–C5.3 – all four nodes hold; convergence uniform on compacts of the Euclidean on-shell region
- **Motivation**: the same resolution of the identity is standard sewing completeness on an ordinary neck, the colour-2 edge differing only by the missing $e^{-sL_0^+}$ damping of the frozen neck, so the burden is convergence and not existence; the level part is a finite sum at each truncation. **A level truncation does not truncate the momentum integral**, so convergence remains a genuine analytic condition in the truncated theory — the old support "finite sums at each level" is retracted
- **Would be falsified by**: saturation drift of the partial level sums (amplitude plan Phase B3); or, for the momentum part, a divergence of $\int d^Dk_a$ at fixed external momenta, visible as a pair-channel threshold singularity absent from the standard amplitude (031 §4.3)
- **Used by**: R27, R29

Completeness and convergence, on the vectors $m_2P$, of the $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ two-string state sum over the two strands' oscillator levels **and** the pair's relative momentum $k_a$ (R33 annotation on R27; 031 §4.3, §7) — R29 H2, in the form that permits the sum to be interchanged with $m_2^{-1}$ at the collapse: the scalar partial sums $s_N(P,Q)=\sum_{I\le N}V_4(P,m_2^{-1}\lambda_I)\,\langle\lambda_I,m_2Q\rangle$ converge to $V_4(P,Q)$ for the external data used, at every point of a real neighbourhood of the on-shell locus — convergence of numbers, no norm on the string field space (the only analytic input of the telescoping step; moved here from H1 on 2026-09-22; scalar form 2026-09-22).

### PH4 (PH-c) — Feynman regions of the vertex family cover $\mathcal M_{0,4}$ exactly once [supported – 062 C1.5 (declared section, on shell); R2]

- **Scope**: the support is one numerical check in the SL(2,C) declared section (062 C1.5: on shell, three points, two $\beta$, the level-0 tachyon integrand; levels $\ge1$ of the exchange rest on the book's sewing identity, 062 C1.3) plus one boundary check (R2). Not covered: other families; a gap or overlap on which the on-shell integrand integrates to zero. PH4 is a covering statement only: agreement of the local coordinates of $\mathcal V_{0,4}$ with plumbing is not part of it.
- **Verification**:
  - n.d. – literature comparison – Zwiebach 1993 §5.1, [Erbin 2023] – covering exactly once is the defining property of a vertex family (geometric master equation); a definition, not evidence that a given family satisfies it
  - 2026-06-23 – exact check – `codes/verify-cubic-sft-overview.py` C2, C2b, C2c (R2; record 001) – twist-only $\lvert q\rvert=1$ gluing of two $\mathcal V_3'$ reproduces $\partial\mathcal V_{0,4}$
  - 2026-09-26 – numerical check – `codes/verify-ph24-vs.py` (record 062 C1.5; `agent/reports/computator-ph24-20260926T104000.md`) – contact region plus the three plumbing channels reproduce $I_{\rm VS}$ to $6\times10^{-15}$ at three on-shell points, $\beta\in\{\tfrac32,2\}$, $\beta$-independently; node check 2026-10-01: holds (`agent/reports/critic-062-confirm-20261001T020500.md`)
- **Motivation**: the defining property of a string vertex family (geometric master equation); sister project
- **Would be falsified by**: a numerical gap or overlap in the region boundaries of the sister project's $\mathcal V_{0,4}$
- **Used by**: R29

The Feynman regions of the vertex family in use (SL(2,C) family included) cover $\mathcal M_{0,4}$ exactly once.

**Annotation (2026-09-30, critic-062).** The 062 support integrates a strictly positive integrand $|Z|^{2c-2}|1-Z|^{2b-2}$, so any pure gap or pure overlap of positive area fails it; only a gap and an overlap with equal weighted areas at all three points would pass. At $\beta=\tfrac32$ the level-$\ge1$ part of each exchange is $2.4\times10^{-6}$ of $E_{\rm ch}$ at P1 (level 2 only; level 1 vanishes at $b=c$) and up to $2.9\times10^{-3}$ at P3 ($s$ channel); it is resolved at $6\times10^{-15}$. Unchecked: $\beta\notin\{\tfrac32,2\}$; off-shell integrands (local coordinates are outside PH4 anyway).

### PH5 (PH-d) — $\ker V_4=0$ off shell at generic momentum; failing that, $V_5$ and $V_6$ annihilate the kernel [supported – R53, level 0]

- **Scope**: the support covers the first clause at level 0: no kernel of $V_4$ on the tachyon-pair block at any real pair momentum, for every $\beta>\sqrt2$, under PH25 and PH29, PH30. The second clause ($V_5$ annihilates the kernel) is vacuous there and untested elsewhere. At zero momentum, level $(1,1)$, only $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathrm{span}\{T,D\}$ has been checked (see the annotation). Higher levels are open. The 2026-09-22 annotation's "the status stays open" is superseded (2026-09-25: supported at level 0 by R53).
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-09-22 – numerical check – sister project, SL: R49 – the zero-momentum ghost-dilaton block is non-degenerate on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathrm{span}\{T,D\}$ on the sampled sections (companion computation, not yet public); matter states absent, one non-generic momentum point
  - 2026-09-25 – node check – critic on 050 §18c.4, node C3 (`agent/reports/critic-050-c3-20260925T115500.md`) – all nodes hold; R53: $\ker K_P(s)=\{0\}$ at every real $s$, every $\beta>\sqrt2$ (Conditional(PH25, PH29, PH30) – Proven)
- **Motivation**: 034 §4: no selection rule forces a kernel; an accidental kernel is codimension one in the family and removed by a stub deformation; a forced kernel comes with a factorisation map and is inherited. Genericity over families is a remedy, not evidence (critic F9); the zero-momentum ghost-dilaton block (034 §4 (vii)) is why the statement is at generic momentum.
- **Would be falsified by**: $N=\ker V_{4S}\neq0$ stable under variation of the stub parameter with $N\not\subseteq N_5$ (034 l.261 test)
- **Used by**: R17, R19, R20, R21, R22, R24, R25, R26, R34, R35, R45, R46, R57, R60

At generic momentum, $\ker V_4=0$ on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0$ off shell for a generic member of the vertex family (stub parameter); failing that, $V_5$ annihilates the kernel. Third clause (registered 2026-10-01 on Harold's decision, from 034's (C$^{(6)}$)): failing the first clause, $V_6$ annihilates $\ker V_4$ in the sense needed by the quotient presentation — its restriction to $\operatorname{Sym}^6\mathcal H_0\cap(\mathcal S_3\otimes\ker V_4\otimes\mathcal H_0)$ vanishes (the vertex-side half of PH2 (b)'s $T_6$ condition; R19, R21). The second and third clauses condition the quotient presentation only; the hybrid presentation (R75) needs neither.

**Annotation (2026-09-22).** The zero-momentum ghost-dilaton block feared in 034 §4 (vii) is non-degenerate on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathrm{span}\{T,D\}$ on the sampled sections (companion computation, not yet public; SL: R49). That block is not the full zero-momentum level-$(1,1)$ block (the matter states are absent), and a single non-generic momentum point is not evidence of genericity in momentum, so the status stayed open (superseded 2026-09-25: supported at level 0 by R53).

**Annotation (2026-10-01).** The $T_6$ half of the consistency of the $\ker V_4$ quotient sits in PH2 (b); its vertex-side part (the forced diagonal of $T_6$ on $\operatorname{Sym}^6\mathcal H_0\cap(\mathcal S_3\otimes\ker V_4\otimes\mathcal H_0)$) is implied by 034's (C$^{(6)}$), which PH5 does not carry (see the R19/R21 annotations); a third clause was registered on 2026-10-01 (body, last sentences).

**PH5 annotation addendum (2026-10-01, R75).** The second clause and the proposed third clause are requirements of the quotient presentation of $\Sigma$; the hybrid presentation (R75) needs neither, at the price of a complement choice carried by the constraint and the sextic vertex. They condition how the construction is *presented* when $\ker V_4\ne0$, not whether it exists.

**Annotation (2026-10-20).** First clause at fixed level $L$: a theorem under PH35 plus one reference momentum per level (record 104 C4.5–C4.6); (synthesis, closing assessment of 2026-10-18) and under PH35 and PH36 without a reference momentum (106 (2.3), first-horn part assumed through (R′)); for the full operator, countability only (C6.3). Under the paper's second kernel clause, one zero of $\mathcal K_2$ (of $\mathcal K_2^{(L)}$ under the paper's level-stabilisation assumption) gives every $\mathcal K_{k'}$, $k'\ge3$, a plane-wave null vector at every total momentum, in the paper's Fock-space sense (not an $L^2$ null vector of the fibre operator, so not the first horn of (4.6)); the clause plus the paper's Fock-space "no kernel at generic momentum" at $k=3$ excludes zeros at $k=2$; in the $L^2$ sense D1 at $k=3$ and D2 at $k=2$ stay independent (record 104 C5.4). (synthesis, closing assessment of 2026-10-18) Annotation from 107 (*Scope of the exclusion*): the motivation "no selection rule forces a kernel" is supported within the five candidates (none null), not in general. (synthesis, closing assessment of 2026-10-18) Annotation from 106 ((2.2a), (2.4)(ii)): under PH36(S), PH5's falsifier in the form "a null vector fixed in fixed local coordinates along the one-sided lengthening of the stub" is excluded; a real zero moving with the stub on a curve is the generic behavior and is not excluded. Harold, 2026-10-20: a null vector at every total momentum is excluded on physical grounds, with record 106's heuristic in support; 'no kernel at generic momentum' for the higher fields is meant in the square-integrable sense, and the plane-wave null vectors generated by a zero of $p_{2,2}$ are not counted.

### PH6 (PH-f) — Every geometric $V_n$ is $\ddagger$-equivariant [supported – Zwiebach 1993 (general $n$); R56 ($n=4$, SL(2,C) family)]

- **Scope**: the support has two parts. (a) The general-$(g,n)$ implication, that a mirror condition on the vertex regions with antiholomorphically related local coordinates makes the vertex real, is cited from Zwiebach 1993 §5.1 condition (c) and §7.4 eq. (7.98); Zwiebach proves (c) for the minimal-area construction only (§6.4). (b) For the SL(2,C) family, conjugation invariance holds at $n=4$ for $\beta\ge\beta_{04}$ (R56): region, tiling, first-order section data and plumbing maps. At level 0 the check is real tile by tile, hence blind. Sonoda–Zwiebach H5 is a cited CFT input, not re-derived here (R56 part 3 rests on it, 054 A4). Open: descendant-level frame data, the $b$-ghost measure (R56, `Argued` part), $n\ge5$ for the family.
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-09-26 – numerical check – `codes/verify-ph6-conjugation-n4.py` 45/45 PASS incl. 14 break legs, seed 20260926 (record 054), orchestrator re-run – conj-invariance of the $n=4$ contact region, its tiling, the section data and the plumbing maps for the SL(2,C) family
  - 2026-09-26 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-054-20260926T022000.md` – ACCEPT, five minors applied by `agent/reports/computator-054-fix-20260926T023000.md`; R56 promoted
  - 2026-09-26 – literature comparison – Zwiebach 1993 (hep-th/9206084) §5.1 condition (c), §7.4 eq. (7.98), extraction `agent/context/zwiebach1993-vertex-reality-2026-09-26.md` (`agent/reports/searcher-ph6a-zwiebach-20260926T054500.md`) – general-$(g,n)$ reality of $\{\Psi^n\}_g$ given (c) and the field reality condition (3.6)
- **Motivation**: reality of the nonpolynomial action (028 l.70–81); derived at $n=4$ in 028 from the two inputs (a), (b) of the annotation, which were then assumed for every $n$; (a) has since been located in Zwiebach 1993 (see Scope)
- **Would be falsified by**: a vertex family whose regions or measures are not conjugation invariant
- **Used by**: R24, R35, R46

Every geometric $V_n$ is $\ddagger$-equivariant (H$_R$): Sonoda–Zwiebach H5 together with conjugation invariance of the moduli regions and measures of the family in use.

**Annotation (2026-09-26).** Clause labels, as the records use them (R56, record 054): **(a)** conjugation-invariant regions and measures with antiholomorphically related local coordinates make every vertex real – Zwiebach 1993 (hep-th/9206084) §5.1 (c), §7.4 (7.98), stated for general $(g,n)$; **(b)** the vertex family in use satisfies that condition – for the SL(2,C) family, R56 at $n=4$. Sonoda–Zwiebach H5 is the CFT input to both.

**Annotation (2026-10-01, node check `agent/reports/critic-r11-r13-c3-20261001T093500.md`).** Component form with the Koszul sign: $\ddagger$-equivariance of $V_n$ reads $V_n(A_1^\ddagger,\dots,A_n^\ddagger)=(-1)^{k_o(k_o-1)/2}\,V_n(A_1,\dots,A_n)^*$ with $k_o$ the number of Grassmann-odd arguments. Read without this sign, PH6 contradicts conventions C3 at $n=2$ on the $(1,3)$ and $(3,1)$ ghost-number pairs; with it, three routes agree (Zwiebach's total-field reality as read in 028 P.6; C3 with the $Q$ rule at $n=2$; the $L_\infty$ relations at $n=2,3$). R46 uses even arguments only; R24 and R35 were not rechecked against the signed form.

### PH7 (PH-h2) — $\ddagger^2=1$ on Grassmann-odd states [discharged – Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/028-reality-tower.md` addendum 2026-09-26, P.3–P.6
- **Scope**: every state, even or odd, for either sign $\eta$, either inversion, any normalisation and either product-ordering convention; with Grassmann-odd coefficients under the involutive conjugation convention (conventions C3); matter sector argued, not machine-checked
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-09-26 – exact check – `codes/verify-ph7-ddagger.py` 35/35 PASS, seed 20260926 (ghost truncation $n\in\{-1,0,1\}$; orchestrator re-run identical)
  - 2026-09-30 – node check – `agent/reports/critic-028-ph7-node-20260930T233800.md` – P.3, P.5, P.6 hold (re-derived in general: $\ddagger$ diagonal in the mode-monomial basis with real entries $\pm\eta$); mechanical fixes `agent/reports/computator-028-ph7-fix-20261001T010700.md`
- **Status**: discharged 2026-09-26 – Proven (028 addendum P.3–P.5; node check `agent/reports/critic-028-ph7-node-20260930T233800.md`: P.3, P.5, P.6 hold) – Verified on the ghost truncation $n\in\{-1,0,1\}$ (`codes/verify-ph7-ddagger.py`, 35/35, seed 20260926); matter sector argued, not machine-checked
- **Motivation**: the addendum's reading of the live book at 1dbb7217 (not verified by the node check): the book states the reality condition only for the open string (`bsft_free_brst.tex` l. 237–241); for the closed string it has a TODO citing Zwiebach (3.6) (l. 1637); the closed-string source is Zwiebach (3.6)/(3.9). (The earlier Motivation line attributed a closed-string reality condition to [Erbin 2023] at 028 l. 309–311; misattribution found by the ledger completion, K1.)
- **Would be falsified by**: an explicit sign check on one odd state failing (then $\lambda_k^\ddagger=\pm i\lambda_k$ and there are no $\ddagger$-real odd states)
- **Used by**: R35

$\ddagger^2=1$ on Grassmann-odd states.

**Discharge (2026-09-26, node check 2026-09-30).** $\ddagger=\eta\,\mathrm{bpz}^{-1}\circ\mathrm{hc}$ maps each mode monomial to a real multiple of itself ($\phi_n^\star=(-1)^h(\pm1)^n\phi_n$), so $\ddagger^2=+1$ on every state, even or odd, for either sign $\eta$, either inversion, any normalisation and either ordering convention for products. With Grassmann-odd coefficients $\ddagger^2$ is the square of c-number conjugation: $+1$ in the standard involutive convention for conjugating odd coefficients (Zwiebach writes it as a dagger; no input states a rule for its square), $-1$ on odd coefficients only under the graded convention $\theta^{**}=-\theta$. For commuting coefficients PH7 is unconditional.

Refined at $O(g_s^5)$ by derivation 038 (2026-09-21): the couplings are $V_5(\chi,\chi,\Psi)$ (supplying $S_1$) and $V_6(\chi,\Sigma,\Sigma)$ (supplying $S_2$; $V_6(\chi,\Sigma_3,\Psi)$ is a redundant partner), together with a right-sign $\Sigma_3$ carrying its even HS pair, which is forced – not a wrong-sign partner of $\Sigma_3$. $V_6(\chi,\chi,\Sigma)$ enters only at $O(g_s^6)$. Conditions at this order: $b'_\chi=0$ (no $V_5(\chi,\Sigma_3)$ mixing) and a stated sextic split $c_6$, which moves the $S_2$ component of the deficit, $\beta(c_6)=\tfrac{7(2+c_6)}5$.

**Annotation (2026-09-22).** **Supported at $O(g_s^5)$ in the model (048, 2026-09-22): the coupling closing the $S_1$ direction is $V_5(\chi,\chi,\Psi)$ with $c_\chi=1$, and the forced $\Sigma_3$ partner is the right-sign one with its even HS pair (its removal is the C3 break leg); the run tests the consequence, not the hypothesis.**

**Annotation (2026-10-01, after 051 Block 13).** What is established at level 0, $\beta=2$: an off-lattice zero of the determinants of the Nyström matrices at $\alpha's_{**}\approx3.2231131+9.4645234\,i$, stable under the discretisation. Five discretisations place it within a disc of diameter $2.8\times10^{-7}$ (a spread, not a convergence error): radial nodes $n_r=44,56$ at cutoff $\Lambda=8$; $\Lambda=10$ at the same node density; vertex-integral rules $n_{2d}=60,90,120$ (051 C7.6.4, C7.6.6). Reading (B) of 051 C7.6.4 (a cutoff artefact) and the quadrature-rule concern are refuted. Whether $K_P(s_{**})$ has an $L^2$ null function – the level-0 stand-in of R51 – is open. Readings (A) (an $L^2$ null function whose eigen-coefficients lie below the double-double floor) and (C) (none) remain, and the matrices' null vector is not resolved (it follows the edge of the radial range). PH18 at level 0 is neither realised nor excluded. Decided by: the eigen-coefficient profile of the null vector from entries accurate beyond $10^{-50}$ relative to the kernel. That needs dps $\ge50$, a 2D rule converged at that level (the present rule converges non-monotonically at $10^{-13}$–$10^{-12}$, 051 (13.2)–(13.3)) and an analytic (Taylor-in-$s$) derivative $M'$ in place of finite differences. Estimated cost (not measured; basis to be recorded in 051 `## Next`): 20–60 min per matrix per core in quad-double, 2–4 h in mpmath. Not run.

For $k\ge3$ under the second clause, a single zero at $k=2$ (of $\mathcal K_2$, or of $\mathcal K_2^{(L)}$ under the paper's level-stabilisation assumption) gives a plane-wave null vector of $\mathcal K_{k'}$ at every momentum (not an element of $Z_{L,k'}$) (C5.4).

### PH21 (PH-m5) — $\rho(D^{-1}N)<1$ off $Z_L$ for the level-diagonal split of the quartic form on $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ [supported – one block: zero momentum, $\{T,D\}$]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/020-riemann-m2-geometric.md` §3 (ii), (3.1) (the Neumann chain sum); `agent/derivations/023-a4pt-virasoro-shapiro-chain.md` (5.2) (the split with $G_2$); registered as PH-m5 from `agent/reports/planner-rigor-D-20260921T150000.md` (applied in `agent/reports/ledger-rescope2-20260921T160000.md`)
- **Scope**: the support is one numerical point: the zero-momentum $\{T,D\}$ block of $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ at the declared section, $\beta\in\{\tfrac32,2\}$ (values from the companion SL(2,C) computation, not reproduced here), where $\rho<1$ comes from cancellation, not diagonal dominance. Open: nonzero momenta, the tachyon-pair block, levels beyond $(1,1)$, matter states
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-09-21 – literature comparison – `agent/derivations/023-a4pt-virasoro-shapiro-chain.md` l.1046–1059, l.1496–1500 – the diagonal-dominance motivation rests on open-string level truncation (Moeller–Taylor, hep-th/0002237), transferred to the closed-string quartic Gram by analogy; 023's own $N=2$ model has no level structure and says nothing about PH21
  - 2026-09-22 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-rigor-D-20260922T090000.md` item 4 – partly, [major]: no momentum domain, incompatible with PH18 as stated; domain added in `agent/reports/ledger-rescope4-20260922T100000.md`
  - 2026-09-22 – numerical check – `codes/verify-neumann-chain.py` (`agent/reports/ph21-neumann-script-20260922T150200.md`) – spectral radius below 1 on the zero-momentum $\{T,D\}$ block of $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ at the declared section; the matrix is not diagonally dominant; the note's "about 15 terms" at $\beta=\tfrac32$ does not reproduce (17 terms), exit nonzero on that check only
- **Motivation**: level-diagonal dominance of the vertex Gram matrix, the usual situation in level truncation; without it the only honest surface representation of $m_2^{-1}$ (020 (3.1)) does not exist. Distinct from PH1: PH1 can hold with the chain series divergent.
- **Would be falsified by**: the spectral radius of $D^{-1}N$ at fixed level from the actual $V_4$ — a by-product of the 16b data
- **Used by**: R27

$\rho(D^{-1}N)<1$ on a stated momentum domain (real off-shell momenta away from $Z_L=\{\det V_4^{(L)}=0\}$), for the level-diagonal split of the quartic form on $\operatorname{Sym}^2$: $V_4\rvert_{\operatorname{Sym}^2}=D-N$, $D$ its level-diagonal part in a basis of $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0$, $N$ the off-diagonal remainder, so that $m_2^{-1}=\sum_n(D^{-1}N)^nD^{-1}G_2$ (023 (5.2)). Equivalently, 020 (3.1)'s split $m_2=\tilde D-\tilde N$ with $\tilde D=G_2^{-1}D$: then $\tilde D^{-1}\tilde N=D^{-1}N$, so the spectral radius is the same object under either reading.

**Annotation (2026-09-22).** $\rho(D^{-1}N)<1$ (values from the companion SL(2,C) computation, not reproduced here) on the zero-momentum $\{T,D\}$ block of $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ at the declared section, machine-checked in `codes/verify-neumann-chain.py` (1 % relative error of the truncated series against the exact inverse, spectral 2-norm, first reached at 17 resp. 4 terms). That matrix is however *not* diagonally dominant: $\rho<1$ comes from a cancellation among the off-diagonal products, not from the level-diagonal dominance invoked above, so the data supports the conclusion while undercutting this motivation

**Annotation (2026-10-20).** The Neumann chain as a bounded-operator statement at continuous relative momentum is argued against by record 104 (4.2) (relative unboundedness in the Hermite scale); the frozen-block support is unaffected.

**Discharge (038 Addendum N10, 2026-09-26; node check 2026-09-30).** The $O(g_s^2)$ source doublet of the $\mathrm{Sym}^2$ pair $(\Sigma,\chi)$ is unit-timelike, $\langle\lambda,\lambda\rangle=\lambda^\top Q^{-1}\lambda=-1$. This is the $O(g_s^2)$ matching condition itself, for every real presentation of the $\chi$ scheme (elementary couplings $g^{n-2}V_n/n!$, cubic action, no lower-order sources), so null and spacelike doublets do not reproduce the quartic vertex. The residual $O(1,1)$ therefore always aligns the source with $\Sigma$ and leaves $\chi$ without an $O(g_s^2)$ source. Using that boost is a choice of frame, unique up to $\chi\to-\chi$. The alignment holds at $O(g_s^2)$ only: the $O(g_s^3)$ source covector $\mu$ is null ($\langle\mu,\mu\rangle=0\Leftrightarrow b_\chi^2/a_\chi=4$), so $\chi$ is sourced at $O(g_s^3)$ in every frame with diagonal kinetic form.

Stripping, sphere and coupling factors do not depend on the states at fixed $n$, so R43's other conversions ($V_4$, $G_2$, $\operatorname{spec}(m_2)$) are covered up to basis normalisations of states at level $\ge1$. Relative to each project's declared vacuum normalisation (the two projects' declared vacuum normalisations differ by $\lambda$ (R43); unit matter coefficient; $(2\pi)^D\delta^D$ stripped) the three companion/book ratios all equal $\lambda$, so no stripping, matter, field or coupling factor lies beyond $\lambda$. The constants are off-shell couplings; the statement is not limited to on shell. Not covered: couplings with $n\ge5$ (not used by R43); basis normalisations of states at level $\ge1$ (a basis convention, not a stripping one). A factor common to both projects is a redefinition and unobservable; how a project's declared $\nu$ divides between ghost and matter inside its code is not an observable.

- **Scope**: the vertices $V_3,\dots,V_{k+2}$ with $k\le4$: at $k\le3$ through $V_5$ with $V_6$ as the consistent completion; at $k=4$ the $V_6$ of the action is that completion (the arity-5 relation, $\mathcal J_4=0$) and no $V_7$ clause is used, so the content used at $k=4$ is that of $k=3$ ($\mathcal J_m=0$, $m\le4$); at $n=4$ the covering clause is PH4, and the agreement of the local coordinates on $\partial\mathcal V_{0,4}$ with the plumbing-induced ones is part of PH27, not of PH4
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-09-26 – node check – `agent/reports/critic-058-confirm-20260926T110000.md` – H1 holds as a declared hypothesis (statement, motivation, falsifier); wording fixes applied 2026-09-30 (`agent/reports/computator-058-fix-20260930T233000.md`)
  - 2026-10-06 – cited source – companion-project review: (a) the local coordinates of $\mathcal V_{0,4}$ agree with those of the plumbed cubic vertices on $\partial\mathcal V_{0,4}$ by construction (the sister's paper 1 §4.2; checks: plumbing $\tilde F_3=Z/\tilde F_1$, `SL: R43` audit A4, the $C_4$ sewing datum) – supports the $n=4$ boundary clause; (b) no consistent $\mathcal V_{0,5}$, $\mathcal V_{0,6}$ exists in the sister project or is planned before its paper 2 – PH27 stays open. The sister's $d^5$ ghost-dilaton closure is conditional on the sister's own PH5 (the five-point vertex extends the family consistently, a PH27-type clause) and tests the consistency of its five-point chain, not the family: no status change
- **Motivation**: the HS rewriting of R1/R17 takes the closed-SFT vertices as given and inherits their consistency; consistent genus-zero families exist at all $n$ (Zwiebach 1993 §6.4–6.5: minimal-area / restricted-polyhedra vertices and their stub family)
- **Would be falsified by**: a gap or overlap between $\mathcal V_{0,5}$ or $\mathcal V_{0,6}$ of the family in use and the Feynman regions built from lower vertices, or a nonzero $\mathcal J_m$, $m\le k+1$, computed from the vertices. Discharged by an explicit $\mathcal V_{0,5},\mathcal V_{0,6}$ extending the SL(2,C) $\mathcal V_{0,4}$ with a verified single cover and matching local coordinates on the boundaries, or by fixing the project's $V_n$, by definition, as those of such a family
- **Used by**: R62 ($k\le3$ and $k=4$); R89 (including the normalisation factorisation $C_5=C_4^2/C_3$ in the book normalisation)

The kept vertices $V_3,\dots,V_{k+2}$ of the cubic HS action ($k\le3$) are the truncation of a genus-zero $L_\infty$ family: the vertex family in use satisfies Zwiebach's recursion (5.1) at $g=0$ (the vertex regions and the Feynman graphs built from lower vertices cover $\mathcal M_{0,n}$ once, and the local coordinates of $\mathcal V_{0,n}$ agree with the plumbing-induced ones on the common boundary) for $n\le k+2$, and admits a consistent $V_{k+3}$ ($n\le6$).

### PH28 (057 H1) — One-leg universality selection rule for the declared SL(2,C) section [discharged → R88]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/057-finite-field-truncation.md` §4.1 (H1), claim-tree node H1 (registered 2026-10-01 at the promotion of R63)
- **Scope**: zero-momentum, level-matched, ghost-number-2 sector; $\mathcal H_u$ = matter part in the identity Virasoro module (any ghost part), $\mathcal H_{nu}$ = matter part in another module; nonzero-momentum legs are covered by momentum conservation, not by this hypothesis
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-09-30 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-057-partB-20260930T233100.md` – declared with motivation and falsifier; carried in every dependent node; fix pass `agent/reports/computator-057-partB-fix-20261001T010500.md`
  - 2026-10-06 – cited source – companion-project check: the rule is Yang–Zwiebach hep-th/0506077 §2.1 (closed strings, zero momentum, any $n$-string vertex and the kinetic term; after Sen hep-th/9911116), re-derived for the SL(2,C) family as `SL: R57` (Proven): the cubic vertex and the quartic vertex in any admissible section vanish with exactly one leg outside the matter identity module, any ghost part, on the $p=0$ Fock space; the integrand vanishes pointwise, so the section enters only through local coordinates holomorphic in the puncture coordinate; the falsifier's first test ($V_3(T,T,x)$, $V_4(T^3,x)$, $V_4(T^2D,x)$, $x$ the matter trace) is among the vanishing cases listed. The discharge clause (a written proof for the declared section) is met → R88
  - 2026-10-06 – status change – `discharged → R88` (orchestrator, workflow `agent/state/sister-handoff-004-2026-10-06.md`); R88's scope contains PH28's (zero momentum, any ghost part; $n\ge5$ through the family-independent Ward argument)
- **Motivation**: Sen's universality argument (hep-th/9911116; Yang–Zwiebach hep-th/0506077 for closed strings), which is a Ward-identity statement: every $V_n$ is a moduli integral of a sphere correlator of conformally transformed states with $b$-insertions; conformal maps preserve the matter module of each leg and $b$-insertions act on the ghost factor, so the matter factor is a correlator of identity-module states with one non-identity state, which vanishes; independent of the vertex family. Strictly weaker than a $\mathbb Z_2$ grading (three-non-universal-leg couplings are allowed and generically nonzero)
- **Would be falsified by**: a nonzero sister-project value of $V_3(T,T,x)$, $V_4(T^3,x)$, $V_4(T^2D,x)$ or $V_5(T^4,x)$ with $x\in\mathcal H_{nu}$ (first test $x=\eta_{\mu\nu}\alpha^\mu_{-1}\bar\alpha^\nu_{-1}\lvert0\rangle$); a failure of the argument at zero momentum in the non-compact matter CFT (PH24's $\delta(0)$ factors). Discharged by a written proof for the declared section including the zero-momentum normalisation, or those sister values vanishing
- **Used by**: R63 (C4.3, C4.3.2, C4.5 part 2)

Every elementary vertex $V_n$ of the declared SL(2,C) section vanishes when exactly one leg lies in the non-universal space $\mathcal H_{nu}$ (zero momentum, level-matched, ghost number 2).

- **Scope**: record 080 C3.4, C4, C5 (the "almost everywhere" strength of the failure of the selection rule); the open-set statements C3.3, C4.1 hold without it

  - 2026-10-04 – cited source (provisional) – companion-project statement that the section is real-analytic inside each of the twelve tiles: the declared section is real-analytic inside each of the twelve tiles of the modulus plane and across the real-axis walls and $\Re Z=\tfrac12$ above $|\Im Z|=\tfrac{\sqrt3}2$, but across the six arcs joining $\mathrm e^{\pm\mathrm i\pi/3}$ to $-1,\tfrac12,2$ the $c_j$ are $C^1$ not $C^2$ and $|a_j|$ has a kink; at $\partial\mathcal V_{0,4}$ the $c_j$ join the channel maps $C^1$, not $C^2$. The statement as worded (real-analytic over $Q_V$) is false; the falsifier (a non-analytic locus of positive measure) is not met — the locus is a finite union of arcs. Recommendation (Harold's decision): replace by piecewise real-analyticity on the twelve tiles, supported by this source; R86's a.e. clause then needs $\mu_i\not\equiv0$ on each tile (or the $S_4$ equivariance to carry it from one tile), to be checked when record 080 is next touched
  - 2026-10-04 – status change – author's decision: `refuted` as worded (the section is not real-analytic across six arcs), replaced by PH34 (piecewise real-analytic), `knowledge/decisions.md` (2026-10-04)
- **Motivation**: the SL(2,C) sections of the sister project are algebraic in the moduli; analyticity is the generic expectation for a Möbius section
- **Would be falsified by**: a section with a non-analytic locus of positive measure inside $Q_V$
- **Used by**: R86 (C3.4)

*The map $q\mapsto(f_1(q),f_2(q))$ from $Q_V$ to the local coordinates of the actual $\mathcal V_4$ at the two punctures of the $\Sigma$-half is real-analytic.*

### PH35 — H1: Fredholm form of the fixed-level kinetic operator after dividing out the level-0 Gaussian on both sides [open]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/104-kernel-analytic-structure.md` §4.5
- **Motivation**: exact at $L=0$: the level-0 block $\mathcal T_{00}(p)=K_{00}(p_{\rm ref})^{-1/2}K_{00}(p)K_{00}(p_{\rm ref})^{-1/2}$ is bounded with bounded inverse at every real $P^2$ (two-sided form comparison of the tile weights, Douglas' lemma) and bounded holomorphic at complex $p$, equal to $1$ at $p_{\rm ref}$ (R53, 050 (4.3)); all blocks share the level-0 Gaussian (2.5) and the toy scaling (4.4) leaves a matrix of polynomial-coefficient differential operators in the oscillator basis, Fredholm under Douglis–Nirenberg global ellipticity, symmetric by (1.5) ($U$ commutes with $\mathbb K_0$); index $0$ once $\mathcal T_L(p)U$ is self-adjoint at one point of $\Omega_L$ (part of the discharge), then on all of $\Omega_L$ by local constancy; the failure mode of D1 is a kernel escaping to high modes, which Fredholmness forbids; PH35 fails exactly at the accumulation point of the repaired counterexample (4.1); the UV-finiteness analogy (the stub Gaussian controls the high-momentum behaviour of every vertex)
- **Would be falsified by**: an accumulation point of $Z_L$ at finite $p$ near the real axes; a near-null sequence with escaping momentum content; a degenerate weighted principal-symbol matrix of $\mathcal T_L$; a failure of the range condition $K^{(L)}_p(D)\subseteq\mathrm{ran}\,\mathbb K_0^{1/2}$ (record 104 C3.2, absorbed by PH35); a null vector of $K_{00}(p_{\rm ref})$ on odd functions
- **Discharged by**: the symbolic $\Pi^{(1)}$ of the level-$(1,1)$ block, the Douglis–Nirenberg ellipticity of the weighted symbol matrix of $\mathcal T_1$, and the self-adjointness of $\mathcal T_1(p)U$ at one real $p$

For every $L$, with $\mathbb K_0=\bigl(K_{00}(p_{\rm ref})\,\Pi_{\rm even}+|K_{00}(p_{\rm ref})|\,\Pi_{\rm odd}\bigr)\otimes1_{W_L}$ at a fixed reference momentum of real $p_{\rm ref}^2$ ($\Pi_{\rm even/odd}$ the parity projectors in $q$) and, at $L\ge1$, $K_{00}(p_{\rm ref})$ is injective on $L^2_{\rm odd}(\mathbb R^{26})$ (not given by R53, whose mechanism uses evenness), there is a connected open $\Omega_L\supset\mathbb R\cup i\mathbb R$ on which $\mathcal T_L(p)=\mathbb K_0^{-1/2}K^{(L)}_p\mathbb K_0^{-1/2}$ is a holomorphic family (type (A), common domain $D\subseteq\mathrm{ran}\,\mathbb K_0^{1/2}$ mapped by $K^{(L)}_p$ into $\mathrm{ran}\,\mathbb K_0^{1/2}$) of closed Fredholm operators of index $0$ with $\ker\mathcal T_L(p)\subseteq\mathrm{ran}\,\mathbb K_0^{1/2}$.

### PH36 — H2′: a kernel of $p_{2,2}^{(L)}(P)$ at every momentum is a selection rule of the vertex family, and no pair state is blind to the single-string exchange tower, at any level [open]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/106-kernel-heuristic-mass-sum.md` §1 (C1, C1.1)
- **Motivation**: Harold's decision (2026-10-20, `knowledge/decisions.md`, "Track D closing document" and its addendum): a null vector at every total momentum would be a whole spacetime field of two-string states with no quadratic term, a sector decoupled from the quartic vertex; Harold excludes it on physical grounds, and the mass-sum argument of this record is the supporting heuristic; the stub dependence of the fixed-level operator beyond a Gaussian congruence is the shell of single-string exchanges, a convergent sum over the mass tower (K8U3UFZH §3.7, §8.1) with one finite-rank piece per mass level and distinct exponents $h_n(P)$ in the stub length (106 (0.3s)); a null vector fixed along the stub family must decouple from every mass level separately (Laplace separation, 106 (2.1)); at level 0 the shell is definite by R53's mechanism, so (S) holds there. (R′): every known mechanism producing a null direction at every momentum is a conservation law of the family (ghost number, Lorentz, momentum, $Q_B$-exactness, strand exchange, level matching – record 107's candidate class), whose sector is $\Lambda$-invariant and annihilated at every stub length (lemma 106 (1.0)); analyticity excludes a kernel present at exactly one stub length (a line component of the singular set in the $(p,s)$ plane) but not an accidental kernel whose null vectors rotate with the stub – for that case the only motivation is that no mechanism is known. Logical status: given (S) and (2.1), (R′) is equivalent to "no kernel at every real $P^2$", so D1 under PH36 is assumed through (R′), not derived from an independent premise (106 §1, *Logical status*). Fits Harold's $\Lambda_2$ mechanism (decisions 2026-10-18 10:10): a selection-rule kernel would be a fixed gauge sector, harmless; (S) says none survives the exchange tower
- **Would be falsified by**: (a) a null vector of $p_{2,2}^{(L)}(P)$ at every real $P^2$ for some $L$ at the project's stub (record 107's first-horn type, sense (ii)); (d) for (S): a level-$\le L$ pair state $N$ with $\Pi_L\mathcal V_3\Pi_n\mathcal V_3N=0$ for every mass level $n$ and $(\mathcal E^t+\mathcal E^u)N=0$, testable level by level with the cubic vertex, cheapest at $L=1$ in the ghost sector; (e) for (R′): a kernel at every real $P^2$ containing no selection-rule sector (null vectors rotating with the stub in both descriptions). Not falsified by an isolated real zero at $P^2=0$ or on shell (107's types (b), (c)). Tested against record 107 after its fix pass: no null vector among the five symmetry-sector candidates, the exact-strand family $E(0)\odot\Phi(P)$ included ($V_4(T,T,E,E)=-h_2\ne0$) – consistent with PH36, silent on (d) and (e)
- **Discharged by**: (S) a proof that $N\mapsto(\Pi_L\mathcal V_3\Pi_n\mathcal V_3N)_{n\ge0}$ is injective on the level-$L$ fibre space at each momentum (moment-determinacy route of 050 (4.2) component by component in $W_L$, or a finite $n_0(L)$ that suffices); (R′) a rigidity proof that a kernel at every real $P^2$ of an analytic Hilbert–Schmidt family with the BV stub structure is generated by symmetries, or any proof of D1 at level $L$ (104 C4.6's reference point)

For every level $L$, the project's operator $p_{2,2}^{(L)}(P)$ and the one-sided family obtained from it by lengthening the stub: (R′) if $p_{2,2}^{(L)}(P)$ has a kernel at every real $P^2$, then at every real $P^2$ that kernel contains a non-zero selection-rule sector – a subspace invariant under the per-leg stub factor $\mathrm e^{-\sigma L_0^+/2}$ and annihilated by the operator at every stub length of the family; (S) no non-zero level-$\le L$ pair state at any real $P^2$ is annihilated by the exchange shell $\partial_sp_{2,2}^{(L)}(P)$ for all stub lengths in an interval – equivalently, by every map $\Pi_L\mathcal V_3\Pi_n\mathcal V_3$ to the mass tower and by the crossed-channel shells.

**Annotation (2026-11-09).** On the Motivation's "whole spacetime field of two-string states with no quadratic term": the physical content is a sector of pair states the quartic vertex is blind to at every momentum. The quotient removes these states from $\Sigma_2$ and the quartic source $\mathcal V_4(\Sigma_2, \Psi^2)$ does not reach them ($\mathcal V_4(N, A) = 0$ for all $A$, $\Psi^2$ included), so a kernel at every momentum is not a dynamical sector and costs nothing at quartic order. The cost is the kernel condition (the cubic action descends to the quotient) imposed at every momentum on a whole spacetime field, for $\mathcal V_5$ and every higher vertex; the range condition on the quintic source is independent of the horn (needed even when $p_{2,2}$ has no kernel). The hypothesis itself, (S) and (R′), concerns the vertex and is unaffected. Source: record 116, C1.4 (checked against the quintic section of the paper; no machine check).

The SFT level factor by itself gives no suppression

### PH42 — Operator form of the genus-zero IBL relations on the C6 role-graded symmetric algebra: rule V's single-contraction composition equals the lifted antibracket term by term, and is the square of one operator [open]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/103-ibl-mc-conventions.md`, node H-par
- **Verification**:
  - none (declared 2026-10-18; no check bears on it)
  - 2026-10-22: record 111 (C3.1) tests only the physical-parity form of the (1,1)–(2,1) operator relation on odd-letter words, not this hypothesis's role-parity clause; in the role parity of C6 the zero-contraction part does not cancel on gauge words $\Lambda_2\Psi$ (agent/reports/critic-gu-111-20261022T203016.md, finding 2, a two-line computation not reproduced in code); status unchanged.

On the C6 role-graded symmetric algebra $EC$ at $d=\tfrac12$ (input-role letters in the physical parity, output-role letters in the shifted parity), extend CFL's (2.1) to every word by applying $\phi$ to $k$-subsets of letters – input-role letters directly, output-role letters through rule V – with Koszul signs in the role parities, and compose by rule V: an output letter fed to a later operation is converted back to a vertex leg by $\omega$, i.e. the term is the sewing of the two vertices with the odd $\omega^{-1}$, with the sign of that sewing, R87's sign under the paper's coefficient rule (R87, C3; coefficients moved to the left, passing only basis states), for the `eq:conv:sprod` family – on words with odd letters the two families and the two coefficient rules give different signs, and the hypothesis is stated for this pair; the antibracket on odd inputs is R87's Argued part, so the discharge of H-par on such words also discharges it; the sign of the sewing depends only on the role parities and positions of the letters of the word, so that $\hat\phi$ is one operator on $EC$ and $\hat{\mathfrak p}'\circ\hat{\mathfrak p}'$ is its square. Hypothesis: for every pair of genus-zero operations with $k+\ell\le4$ the single-contraction part of $\hat\phi'\circ\hat\phi$ equals, with sign and coefficient, the lifted term of R87's antibracket $\{S,S\}$, so CFL's operator form of the genus-zero relations holds in this algebra. Motivation: R87's vertex-form proof, in which all legs are physical inputs and outputs are legs raised by the odd $\omega^{-1}$, and C6's shifted output parity as the parity that odd map transports (record 076). Falsified by a finite-model computation with random graded-symmetric vertices (an identity term by term, no $L_\infty$ data needed) implementing C6's role parities – not the script's `eps_out` – and rule V, finding a single-contraction term that differs in sign or coefficient from the lifted antibracket term. Not covered, and failing in general for the genus-zero truncation: the double-contraction (involutivity) relations.

### PH43 — Regularity of the truncated stationary surface of closed SFT at generic momentum [open]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/102-gauge-unfixing-structure.md#H1`
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-18 – none: declared hypothesis of record 102 (fix pass)
- **Motivation**: at $g_s^0$, $Q\Psi = 0$ is linear and $Q$ has no cohomology at generic momentum, a regular system; Koszul–Tate acyclicity is stable under a formal deformation in $g_s$, order by order (Barnich–Brandt–Henneaux 2000, cited from memory, no section number)
- **Would be falsified by**: a polynomial in finitely many components vanishing on the $O(g_s^2)$ shell that is not a combination of $\mathcal E_\Psi$ (for example an equation entering only squared), or a symmetry of $S_{\le 4}$ at generic momentum outside gauge plus trivial; discharged by a proof of perturbative Koszul–Tate acyclicity for the truncated theory
- **Used by**: the L2-SFT entry of record 102 (single-string sector only)

At generic momentum – for a given polynomial, the finitely many momentum combinations it involves are off every mass shell – and order by order in $g_s$ through $O(g_s^2)$, the equations of motion of Zwiebach's truncated action $S_{\le 4}$ form a regular set: every polynomial in finitely many components of $\Psi$ that vanishes on shell is a combination of the components of $\mathcal E_\Psi$, and the Noether identities are generated by those of the gauge symmetry (Koszul–Tate acyclicity at antifield numbers 1 and 2), the premise under which the Henneaux–Teitelboim theorem turns properness into completeness of the gauge generators up to trivial symmetries. Being stated modulo $g_s^3$, it covers the regularity premise in truncated form; the truncated form of the theorem's step "proper ⟹ complete" itself is not covered.

### PH44 — The cubic action is the gauge-fixed form of a parent on an enlarged field content (ghost-$5$ partner $\Pi_2$, enlarged $\Sigma_2$ sectors) with a $2$-string parameter acting on $\Psi$ by $g_s\ell_2(\Lambda_2)$ and on $\Sigma_2$ by $\hat Q_B\Lambda_2$, some $\Lambda_2$-orbit in $\Psi$ meeting a non-trivial class; the cubic gauge represents every class in $\Psi$ [open]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/119-aux-physical-dof-conjecture.md#C3` (H1, block *Verdict*; (3.2), (3.4))
- **Scope**: genus zero, $\Sigma_2$ at quartic order for the statement; the same counting for $\Sigma_n$ with partners of ghost number $3n-1$; the $\hat Q$-based statements of R18 are used because the hypothesis concerns the form with $Q_B$ on each strand (R18's annotation of 2026-10-17 is not reversed)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-11-09 – consistency check – ghost-number arithmetic of record 119 *Checks* (saturation, partner ghost number, the two leading terms of the law; Künneth exactness of $\ell_2(\Lambda_2)$ at generic momentum), by hand, PASS

**Statement.** There exists a parent action, with field content $(\Psi,\Sigma_2,\Pi_2,\dots)$, $N_{\rm gh}(\Pi_2)=5$ (the $\Pi$-route; a dressed $p_{2,2}$ with $\hat Q_B\Lambda_2$ among its null directions, R18 T6-bis, is the alternative not excluded), $\Sigma_2$ enlarged to the full ghost-$4$ part of $\mathrm{Sym}^2\mathcal H$ (R108's criterion 3a, so that the $(3,1)$ part of $\hat Q_B\Lambda_2$ stays in the field space, R110), and a gauge parameter $\Lambda_2\in\Hn{2}$ of ghost number $3$ acting as $\delta\Psi=g_s\ell_2(\Lambda_2)+O(g_s^2)$, $\delta\Sigma_2=\hat Q_B\Lambda_2+O(g_s)$, gauge invariant to the order of the truncation, whose gauge fixing on the $\Sigma$ side ($\Pi_2=0$ or equivalent) is the cubic action; and some orbit of $\Lambda_2$ in $\Psi$ meets a non-trivial class of $H^2(Q_B)$, which a gauge fixed on $\Psi$ represents in $\Sigma_2$; the total spectrum is $H(Q^{(k)}_{\Psi_0})$ in every gauge (R73, if the parent is a proper gauge-unfixing of the cubic action). **Motivation.** The component IBL gauge law has these leading terms and preserves the Maurer–Cartan components through $O(g_s)$ (R110; on a model where the $(2,2)$ part of $\hat Q_B\Lambda_2$ is vacuous); the partner construction of R18 (3) realizes $\hat Q_B\Lambda_2$ with a ghost-$5$ field and recovers the cubic action at $\Pi_2=0$, the anomaly of its $\Lambda_2$ variation being PH13; the ghost-number counting (119 C2) excludes a strand-wise $Q_B$ kinetic term without the partner; R106's class ($\Lambda_2$ not acting on $\Psi$) re-splits nothing, and R108 excludes a $\Lambda_2$ acting on $\Psi$ within the present field content at $O(g_s^2)$, generic momentum, so the enlargement is forced (119 C3). At generic momentum $\ell_2(\Lambda_2)$ is $Q_B$-exact for $\hat Q_B$-closed $\Lambda_2$ (119 (3.4)), so the re-split clause concerns zero momentum on a strand or $\hat Q_B\Lambda_2\in\ker\ell_2$. **Discharge/falsification.** Construction of the parent with its partner at $O(g_s^2)$ with $D=g_s\ell_2$ and a $\Pi_2$ coupling of ghost number $5$; or the proof that no such coupling cancels its gauge variation (an exclusion within $D\in\{g_s\ell_2,g_s^2\ell_3(\Psi,\cdot)\}$ on the $\Pi$-route); or the proof that every $\Lambda_2$-orbit in $\Psi$ is $Q_B$-exact (then the parent, if it exists, re-splits nothing). Not the IBL formulation itself: its multi-string states are Fock states, a different cohomology (119 C4).

## Results

### R1 — HS action; EOM $\Rightarrow\Sigma=\Psi^{\otimes2}$ when $m_2$ invertible (on the quotient, R9) [Verified]

- **Source**: `knowledge/notes/cubic-sft-overview/cubic-sft-overview.tex` §3 (`sec:HS`: `eq:SHSderive`, `eq:SHS`, `eq:Sigeom`, §3.4); `agent/derivations/001-cubic-sft-overview-note.md` C1
- **Scope**: $O(g_s^2)$ (quartic truncation), classical action; $V_4$ a graded-symmetric bilinear form on $\mathcal H^{\otimes2}$, modelled in the check by a symmetric matrix $M=m_2$ (001 C1 Setup); invertibility of $m_2$ is the premise of the implication and is not established here (see R9 for injectivity on $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ and the quotient by $\ker V_4$)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-06-23 – exact check – `codes/verify-cubic-sft-overview.py` C1a–c, PASS (script 17/17); a self-check of algebra asserted in the context document, not an independent re-derivation (001 l.10–14)
  - 2026-06-23 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-note-20260623T205039.md`, REVISE overall (F1, F2 not on this row); F13: completion-of-square algebra and boxed `eq:SHS` correct by inspection
  - 2026-06-23 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-note-iter2-20260623T210619.md`, ACCEPT; "adding" vs "replacing" the quartic term flagged as wording, `eq:SHS` unaffected (the note now reads "Replacing", l.379)

HS action $S_{\mathrm{HS}}=\tfrac12V_2(\Psi^2)+\tfrac{g_s}{3!}V_3(\Psi^3)-\tfrac{g_s^2}{4!}V_4(\Sigma,\Sigma)+\tfrac{2g_s^2}{4!}V_4(\Sigma,\Psi^{\otimes2})$; EOM $m_2(\Sigma)=m_2(\Psi^{\otimes2})\Rightarrow\Sigma=\Psi^{\otimes2}$ when $m_2$ invertible (completion of the square in $\Sigma$). On $\mathcal H^{\otimes2}$ the premise never holds ($\wedge^2\mathcal H\subseteq\ker m_2$); the operative reading is injectivity on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0/\ker V_4$, where the implication holds modulo $\ker V_4$ (R9, PH1).. On $\mathcal H^{\otimes2}$ the premise never holds ($\wedge^2\mathcal H\subseteq\ker m_2$); the operative reading is injectivity on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0/\ker V_4$, where the implication holds modulo $\ker V_4$ (R9, PH1).

**I3 — auxiliarity of $\Sigma$ $\iff$ injectivity of $m_2$ on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H$** at $O(g_s^2)$, $g=0$, level-matched, physical $N_{\rm gh}$. **Conditional reduction:** with $G_2=\omega_1^{\otimes2}$ non-degenerate (R7) and $V_4$ fully graded-symmetric (hypothesis H2), $m_2=\mathfrak p_{2,2,0}=G_2^{-1}\circ V_4$ so $\ker m_2=\ker V_4$; $m_2$ has symmetric outputs $m_2(\operatorname{Sym}^2)\subseteq\operatorname{Sym}^2$ and $\wedge^2\mathcal H\subseteq\ker m_2$ is physically inert by **decoupling** (absent from both the $\Sigma$-kinetic term $V_4(\Sigma,\Sigma)$ and the source term $V_4(\Sigma,\Psi^{\otimes2})$). On the **off-shell** level-matched $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H$: $\ker(m_2\rvert_{\operatorname{Sym}^2})=0\iff V_4\rvert_{\operatorname{Sym}^2}$ non-degenerate $\iff\ell_3$ has no symmetric level-matched null vector; when it holds, the $\Sigma$-EOM $m_2(\Sigma)=m_2(\Psi^{\otimes2})$ (note `eq:Sigeom`, R8) has the **unique** solution $\Sigma=\Psi^{\otimes2}$ $\Rightarrow$ $\Sigma$ **purely auxiliary**. This is **injectivity**; actually integrating $\Sigma$ out (S6) needs no inverse on the saddle route, and the propagator form additionally needs $m_2^{-1}$ on the truncated quotient with stable kernels (R10; PH1: no closed range, no bounded inverse). A counterexample to auxiliarity is **exactly** a symmetric level-matched null vector of $\ell_3$ (sharp). $\ker m_2$ is taken off-shell, not on $Q$-cohomology (the cross-check against R6's $Q$-exactness lapsed with R6's withdrawal, R31). **CFT-level inputs:** (5.1) non-degeneracy of $V_4$ on $\operatorname{Sym}^2(\ker L_0^-\cap\ker b_0^-)$ – PH5, supported at level 0 (R53), open above; H2 (full graded symmetry of $V_4$) – an input from the vertex construction (the 2026-07-01 annotation in R13), as in R7, R8, R10. Pole-freeness / "generic" / measure-zero / YM-$\Box$-at-$p^2=0$ are **heuristic only, NOT evidence** (a normalizable null vector would be an honest flat direction); see PH1 and PH5. (restated 2026-09-22, see PH1)

**S6 — integrating $\Sigma$ out of the HS action reproduces the original $\ell_3$ quartic** at $O(g_s^2)$, $g=0$, level-matched, physical $N_{\mathrm{gh}}$: the homotopy-transferred product $q_{3,1,0}^{\mathrm{transf}}=\ell_3$, equivalently $V_4^{\mathrm{transf}}=V_4$. **Mechanism = completion of the square in $\Sigma$:** substituting the on-shell saddle $\Sigma=\Psi^{\otimes2}$ (unique solution of `eq:Sigeom`, R8/R9) into the boxed HS action `eq:SHS` (R1) gives the $2{:}1$ source-to-kinetic ratio $-1+2=+1$, hence effective quartic $+\tfrac{g_s^2}{4!}V_4(\Psi^{\otimes2},\Psi^{\otimes2})$ with the **correct sign and coefficient and no extra terms** (matches R1/R8 eq. 2.4; sign/coeff hand-re-derived by critic). **Single computation:** routes (A) brute saddle-substitution and (B) propagator / CFL homotopy-transfer are the **same** Gaussian elimination — (B) is the diagrammatic restatement, **not** an independent confirmation; its tree combinatorics ("one tree", $L!K!$ factors) reproduce (A)'s $\tfrac1{4!}$: assumed in 005, computed explicitly in R14 (007 §4). Diagonal saddle value $\to$ full-form $V_4^{\mathrm{transf}}=V_4$ is a polarization step (char 0, uses H2). Route (A) is unconditional at $O(g_s^2)$ on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0/\ker V_4$: it uses no inverse, and the $S_4$ symmetry of $V_4$ comes from the vertex construction (the 2026-07-01 annotation in R13). Only route (B), the propagator form, needs an inverse: $m_2^{-1}$ on the truncated quotient with stable kernels (PH1, which assumes no closed range and no bounded inverse) — strictly stronger than R9 injectivity: Conditional(PH1); see also PH5. Classical-saddle integrate-out ($g=0$; Gaussian determinant is a higher-genus effect outside the truncation). The `eq:SHSderive` prose sign slip in the overview note is confined to intermediate prose — boxed `eq:SHS`/`eq:Sigeom` are correct. **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** The row inherited two residuals that no longer apply to its classical statement: the saddle route uses no inverse, so the inverse (PH1) is a condition on the propagator form only (005 l.309–315 vs l.317–321). (restated 2026-09-22, see PH1)

### R7 — Two-output pairing $\omega_2$ graded symmetric in physical statistics, not an odd symplectic form [Verified]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/002-omega2-construction.md` (Revision-2: R2 steps 3–5, R2.Verdict; step 4′ (D4.1)–(D4.9)); `agent/derivations/055-omega-k-graded-symmetry.md` (B1, B3, B4)
- **Scope**: physical parity $\epsilon=N_{\rm gh}\bmod2$, exchange law on the support of $\omega_1$ (ghost-number saturation), stated on $\mathcal H^{\otimes2}$, not on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0/\ker V_4$; cyclicity of $m_2$, $\det\omega_2$ and non-degeneracy are checked on a finite toy ($\dim\mathcal H=4$); the shift partner is checked only as 'demanding graded antisymmetry forces one shift of the whole $\Sigma$-field, $c_s=1$' (check Dict); the antisymmetric form so obtained is even in every uniform grading (002 step 4′, D4.8–D4.9), and the body's '$\omega_1^{\otimes2}\circ(s\otimes s)$' wording is superseded by 002 step 4′ (node check 2026-10-01, `agent/reports/critic-002-step4-node-20260930T234000.md`); infinite-dimensional non-degeneracy reduces to that of $\omega_1$ on $\ker L_0^-\cap\ker b_0^-$ and is flagged open in 002 (l. 384–387, l. 779–782); the grading statement of the body's 2026-09-14 annotation (029 §1 (ii), check G; "Status of this annotation: Argued") is R36's `Argued` part (R36 Status, fourth line) and is not covered by R7's tag
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-06-24 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-omega2-20260624T123500.md`, REVISE (Revision-0/1, suspended-grading argument)
  - 2026-06-24 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-omega2-iter2-20260624T125500.md`, REVISE (framing); Revision-2 re-derived the sign from physical statistics
  - 2026-06-24 – exact check – `codes/verify-omega2-physical.py` 12/12 PASS (P0–Dict, Rob, Cyc, Det; toy $\dim\mathcal H=4$); P0a models $\omega_1$ with the wrong sign $s=-1$ (055 B3.4), the $k=2$ verdict P1 is unaffected ($s^2=1$)
  - 2026-06-24 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-omega2-physical-20260624T184500.md`, REVISE: verdict right, the written derivation of R2.10 wrong; corrected
  - 2026-06-24 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-omega2-physical-iter2-20260624T193000.md`, ACCEPT with non-fatal revisions
  - 2026-09-26 – exact check – `codes/verify-omega-k.py` 89/89 PASS ($k=1$–$5$, both signs of $s$, seed 20260926): general-$k$ exchange law (055 B1), $s=+1$
  - 2026-09-26 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-055-20260926T032500.md`, ACCEPT with a mechanical fix pass: $s=+1$ confirmed; 002 R2.6 ($\omega_1$ graded antisymmetric) is a sign error, R7's $k=2$ headline survives
  - 2026-09-26 – exact check – `codes/verify-002-dictionary.py` 35/35 PASS (explicit ghost zero-mode Fock representation, three break legs, seed 20260926): suspended-degree dictionary of 002 step 4′; node-checked 2026-09-30 (`agent/reports/computator-002-dictionary-20260926T104500.md`)
  - 2026-09-30 – node check – `agent/reports/critic-002-step4-node-20260930T234000.md` – (D4.1)–(D4.9) and conclusions (i), (ii) hold, re-derived by hand; order-reversal statement restricted to the zero-mode sector

Two-output pairing for $\Sigma\in\mathcal H^{\otimes2}$: in **physical** $N_{\mathrm{gh}}$ statistics ($\epsilon=N_{\mathrm{gh}}\bmod2$) $\omega_2=\omega_1^{\otimes2}=(\cdot,c_0^-\cdot)_{\mathrm{bpz}}^{\otimes2}$ is graded **SYMMETRIC** ($W(Y,X)=(-1)^{\epsilon(X)\epsilon(Y)}W(X,Y)$ on support; $\omega_1$ is itself graded symmetric in physical parity on its support ($\langle A,B\rangle=(-1)^{(1+\lvert A\rvert)(1+\lvert B\rvert)}\langle B,A\rangle$, Erbin 2023 `bos:eq:closed-product-rearrangement`; 055 B3), hence so is $\omega_1^{\otimes k}$ for every $k$ (055 C1, R58); 002's R2.6 ($\omega_1$ graded antisymmetric) is a sign error and P0a of `verify-omega2-physical.py` models that wrong sign – the $k=2$ headline is unaffected since $s^2=1$), hence **not** a symplectic form on $\mathcal H^{\otimes2}$, and not an odd form after any uniform degree shift — so DC6 stands physically. Graded antisymmetry needs one shift of the whole $\Sigma$-field: $\omega_2^{\rm symp}(sX,sY):=\omega_2(X,Y)$ on $(\mathcal H^{\otimes2})[1]$, $\deg_S=\epsilon+1$, satisfies $\omega_2^{\rm symp}(sY,sX)=-(-1)^{\deg_SX\deg_SY}\omega_2^{\rm symp}(sX,sY)$ on support. It is an **even** form, since $\omega_2$ pairs equal total parities and is even in every grading whose parity is a function of $\epsilon$. It is therefore an even symplectic form, not an odd one, and a décalage twist $(-1)^{\epsilon(X)}$ does not change this. The strand-wise suspension $(\mathcal H[1])^{\otimes2}$, under which $\Omega_1(A,B)=(-1)^{\epsilon(A)}\langle A,B\rangle$ is the odd symplectic form of each strand, shifts $\Sigma$ by two: $\Omega_2=\Omega_1^{\otimes2}=(-1)^{\epsilon(X)}\omega_2$ has $\omega_2$'s exchange law and is graded symmetric there. An odd $\Sigma$-pairing needs a grading whose parity is not a function of $\epsilon$, e.g. R36's $(N_{\rm gh}-4)/2$ on the classical complex (Argued there). The even shift $s^{-2}$ (degree $-2$) is parity-irrelevant (002 step 4′, (D4.1)–(D4.9)). $m_2=p_{2,2,0}$ is cyclic w.r.t. it (from full graded symmetry of $V_4$; relabel-invariant); $\det\omega_2=(\det\omega_1)^{2\dim\mathcal H}$. Non-degenerate on the finite toy ($\det\omega_2\ne0$); in infinite dimension non-degeneracy reduces to that of $\omega_1$ on the level-matched space, which 002 leaves open. Invertibility of $m_2$ is not used here; see R9 (quotient by $\ker V_4$), PH1 and PH5 (both stated on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0/\ker V_4$, whereas R7 is stated on $\mathcal H^{\otimes2}$). Full graded symmetry of $V_4$ is an input from the vertex construction, not an open item (the 2026-07-01 annotation in R13). **Annotation 2026-09-14 (026 §7, B′c/026C):** the shift $\omega_2^{\rm symp}$ is the parity part of a cyclic pairing on $E_2$; for the brace *products* of $\hat{\mathfrak p}$ on the multi-string space it is necessary but not sufficient — no degree-diagonal pairing makes them cyclic (026 §5.5, products-level; unaffected by the §5 re-derivation, 026 §5′.4). **Annotation 2026-09-14 (024 §5.1, §5.3(b); R34):** the shift $\omega_2^{\rm symp}$ is the $\ell=2$ case of $\deg\omega_1^{\otimes\ell}=-\ell-2$ in the physical grading, and R7's "not an odd symplectic form" is CMZ's action obstruction 1 (no degree-3 form on the cobar space) in the project's grading. **Annotation 2026-09-14 (029 §1 (ii), check G; R36):** $\tilde\ell_1=Q\oplus(-\tfrac{g^2}{12}m_2)$ is not parity-homogeneous in $N_{\rm gh}\bmod2$; the grading under which every bracket of presentation (b) restricted to the classical complex has degree $+1$ and $\omega_2$ degree $-1$ is $\deg_{\mathcal H_0}=N_{\rm gh}-2$, $\deg_{\mathrm{Sym}^2}=(N_{\rm gh}-4)/2$ — not R7's uniform shift (gh 4 even, gh 6 odd; $\omega_2$ degree $-1$ with $\omega_1$'s exchange sign); it is half-integer on the gauge directions $\Lambda\otimes\Psi$, and no ghost-number-based $\mathbb Z$ or $\mathbb Z_2$ grading makes the brackets homogeneous on the full space. R7's sign content (a BV parity must be declared) is untouched. This grading statement is R36's `Argued` part (R36 Status, fourth line) and is not covered by R7's tag. **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** The three "open" flags were replaced: two had been closed or reduced (graded symmetry of $V_4$ by the 2026-07-01 annotation; non-degeneracy reduced to that of $\omega_1$, 002 step 5, which leaves the infinite-dimensional case open) and invertibility of $m_2$ is not used by this row. *Annotation 2026-09-26 (055 B3 commentary):* the odd symplectic form in the BV presentation is $(-1)^{\lvert A\rvert}\langle A,B\rangle$ per strand, not $\langle A,B\rangle$. *Annotation 2026-09-26 (002 step 4′):* the suspended-degree dictionary has been redone in 002 step 4′ ((D4.1)–(D4.9), `codes/verify-002-dictionary.py` 35/35): $\Omega_1$ is odd symplectic, the $\Sigma$-pairing is even in every uniform grading and antisymmetric only after one shift of the whole $\Sigma$ ($c_s=1$ stands); the replacement wording of the shift sentence above is proposed in `agent/reports/computator-002-dictionary-20260926T104500.md` and was applied 2026-09-30 after the node check. *Repair 2026-09-26:* this row was truncated at commit fdc4452 (a raw vertical bar in an inserted formula ended the cell, dropping the 2026-09-14 annotations, the 2026-09-21 re-scope and the Status/Files columns); restored from commit 7cb7d4b with the two 2026-09-26 changes re-applied.

### R11 — Gauge structure of $S_{\rm HS}$: composite law gives $\delta S_{\rm HS}=\delta S_{\rm eff}$ exactly, off-shell [Proven]

- **Source**: `knowledge/assessments/fable-2026-07-01-assessment-and-results.md` §4 (Claim, Proof, Remarks)
- **Scope**: $O(g_s^2)$ HS action, physical $N_{\rm gh}$; uses only the pair-exchange symmetry of $V_4$; $\delta S_{\rm eff}=O(g_s^3)$ is the standard $L_\infty$ input, not re-derived; uniqueness of the extension modulo kernel shifts and EOM-trivial symmetries is an expectation (body); the BV contractible-pair sentence is cited (Henneaux auxiliary-field theorems), not derived here (derived for the auxiliary sector in R60)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-07-01 – exact check – `codes/verify-hs-gauge-invariance.py` 5/5 PASS (sympy; generic symmetric $M$, commuting symbols, $\delta\Psi$ free): infinitesimal and finite equality, falsification leg with source coefficient 1
  - 2026-07-01 – independent re-derivation – critic, by hand in physical $N_{\rm gh}$ incl. Koszul signs ("sign-airtight")
  - 2026-07-01 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-fable-assessment-20260701T120000.md`, REVISE (0 Fatal): S1 (Serious) "exact off-shell gauge invariance" overclaimed – relabelled "equality exact, invariance to $O(g_s^3)$"; S2 uniqueness downgraded to an expectation; relabelings applied, no second iteration

**Gauge structure of $S_{\rm HS}$ (closes note-Q2/S2/P2, DC2/DC3/DC8; finishes I4)** at $O(g_s^2)$, physical $N_{\rm gh}$: with the standard truncated $L_\infty$ transformation $\delta\Psi=Q_B\Lambda+g_s\ell_2(\Psi,\Lambda)+\tfrac{g_s^2}2\ell_3(\Psi,\Psi,\Lambda)$ and the **composite law** $\delta\Sigma=\delta\Psi\otimes\Psi+\Psi\otimes\delta\Psi$ (finite: $\Sigma'=\Sigma+\Psi'^{\otimes2}-\Psi^{\otimes2}$), one has $\delta X=0$ identically ($X=\Sigma-\Psi^{\otimes2}$), hence the **equality $\delta S_{\rm HS}=\delta S_{\rm eff}$ is exact, off-shell, finite** (no $\Sigma$-EOM, no $\Lambda$-expansion). So $S_{\rm HS}$ is gauge-invariant **to the same order $O(g_s^3)$ as the quartic-truncated theory** — the HS step costs nothing (NOT "exactly invariant": $\delta S_{\rm eff}=O(g_s^3)\neq0$). No independent $\Lambda_2$ exists or is needed ($\Sigma$ has no $Q_B$ kinetic term, unlike the open-stub precedent C9ETB6WE which needs $\Lambda_1,\Lambda_2$); residual freedom = kernel shifts $\delta\Sigma\in\ker V_4$ + EOM-trivial symmetries; uniqueness of the extension modulo these is an **expectation, not proved**. Composite law = the $\mathcal H^{\otimes2}$ component of $\hat{\mathfrak p}(\Lambda,e^\Psi)$ (1905.06785 eq. (5.55)) — I4 done. BV: $(X,X^+)$ contractible pair (Henneaux auxiliary-field theorems; cited, not derived here – derived for the auxiliary sector in R60, under R60's conditions). Uses H2 (bilinear-symmetry part). Falsifiable: equality fails for source coefficient $\neq2$ (re-confirms R1's 2:1 ratio from the gauge side; same completion-of-square, not independent). **Annotation 2026-09-14 (026 §7, B′c/026C):** presentation (b)'s products are the re-weighted $V_4$-slice of the brace *products* of $\hat{\mathfrak p}$ (026 (5.25), read on the doubled symbol algebra as bookkeeping: 026 §5′.5–§5′.7, no single graded-commutative polynomial operator host in the strand variables with even $\hbar$ and slot-uniform parity-homogeneous raising for the $\ell\ge2$ components; untested, not excluded: separate input/output generators, non-polynomial product, $\star$-calculus, odd parameter) and are not the Koszul brace structure of any operator (026 (5′.10), script W4, proven); what Theorem A′ (R31) guarantees for (b) is the $\min(k,\ell)=1$ relations, hence the composite gauge law; the $(2,2,0)$ relation plays no role in the HS theory or in the all-orders tower (026 §5.4, §5′.4). **Annotation 2026-09-14 (029; R36):** the composite gauge law is the $(\Lambda,\Psi)$ component of $\phi_2=\cdot^{\otimes2}$; $\phi_2$ is not an $L_\infty$-morphism component (the two-input identity fails by $\hat Q\Psi^{\otimes2}$); $\tilde\ell_1^2=O(g^4)\ne0$ on the full $\mathrm{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0$, consistent with "to $O(g^3)$".

*Annotation (2026-10-01, node check `agent/reports/critic-r11-r13-c3-20261001T093500.md`; conventions C3):* the equality $\delta S_{\rm HS}=\delta S_{\rm eff}$ is an algebraic identity ($\delta X=0$) and holds for complex $\Lambda$. Reality of the law: under C3, $(Q\Lambda)^\ddagger=-Q\Lambda^\ddagger$ and $\ell_n(\Psi,\dots,\Psi,\Lambda)^\ddagger=-\ell_n(\Psi^\ddagger,\dots,\Psi^\ddagger,\Lambda^\ddagger)$. So for $\Psi^\ddagger=\Psi$ and real $g_s$, $\delta\Psi$ is $\ddagger$-real iff $\Lambda^\ddagger=-\Lambda$ (Zwiebach (3.18)), and $\ddagger$-imaginary for $\Lambda^\ddagger=+\Lambda$. The composite law $\delta\Sigma=\delta\Psi\otimes\Psi+\Psi\otimes\delta\Psi$ and the finite law are then $\ddagger$-real: two even strands, componentwise, no exchange sign. The bracket identity uses PH6 in its component form with the Koszul sign $(-1)^{k_o(k_o-1)/2}$ for $k_o$ Grassmann-odd arguments, at $n=3,4$; at $n=2$ that sign follows from C3. A "real gauge parameter" in this entry means $\Lambda^\ddagger=-\Lambda$.
