  - (3) level-0 anchor – tachyon-diagonal element nonzero, level-0 block invertible, equal-mass momentum dependence nonvanishing: Numerical (imported from the sister project, $I(3/2)$ re-derived)

**A2(i)/(ii) — $V_4^{-1}$: coloured grading + unconditional Schwinger form.** (1) *Two-gradings statement:* a length-$k$ chain of 4-punctured spheres joined by $k-1$ colour-2 (double-line) edges is a **coloured tree** ($E=k-1$, $V=k$, $L_c=0$) while its underlying uncoloured surface has $\chi=-2k$, $n_{\rm ext}=4$, genus $g=k-1$ — the genus is **formal Euler-characteristic bookkeeping of a hypothetical worldsheet resolution** (its $3k-2$ moduli never appear; the colour-2 edge carries no modulus). $g_s$ cross-check (contrapositive form): uncoloured grading predicts $g_s^{2(k-1)}$ suppression, actual is uniform $g_s^2$ ⇒ uncoloured grading inconsistent with the coupling expansion; coloured grading = the physical diagram grading ('t Hooft double-line analogy; sharpens the Phase-6 C3 resolution). (2) *Schwinger:* direct $V_4^{-1}=\int_0^\infty dt\,e^{-tV_4}$ valid **iff** positive definite — an inner-product-dependent statement about $(V_4,G)$, not $V_4$ alone; the **unconditional** form for symmetric invertible $V_4$ is $V_4^{-1}=\int_0^\infty dt\,V_4e^{-tV_4^2}$ (integrand expands in ODD chains $V_4^{2k+1}$ only; NOT a convergent ladder expansion of $V_4^{-1}$ itself); **sandwiched collapse** $\int dt\,V_4(V_4e^{-tV_4^2})V_4=V_4$ ($G$-independent) = §6bis(b)'s consistency demand as an identity; Schwinger $t$ = spectral parameter, NOT a modulus (contrast $1/L_0^+$ where $s$ = cylinder length). (3) *Imported empirical note (`SL: R5`/`SL: R6`):* zero-momentum tachyon-diagonal element $=C\,\beta^8\mathrm{Vol}(\mathcal V_{0,4})\neq0$ (anchor value re-derived) ⇒ level-0 block invertible; equal-mass-squared momentum dependence nonvanishing (positive KN integrand); **sign open** when written ($C$ symbolic, sister O3; sign settled by the annotation below, R47). **Carve-outs:** finite-dim; inf-dim self-adjointness/closed-range + canonical inner product open (see PH1, which since 2026-09-22 assumes no auxiliary inner product, no closed range and no bounded inverse); actual spectrum/rank = A2(iii) (task spec in derivation 008 §4: massless block needs $\mathcal J\neq1$ + all Schiffer vectors; cheap first pass $\{T{\otimes}T, T{\otimes}D\}$). (restated 2026-09-22, see PH1) **Annotation 2026-09-22 (conventions; R43, derivation 041 §4.3).** Clause (3)'s *sign open ($C$ symbolic, sister O3)* is closed by `SL: R37` modulo Q2, the measure-orientation question (041 §4), which was undecided on 2026-09-22 (resolved since by R47: branch A, a convention imported from `SL: R37`): on the Belopolsky–Zwiebach branch $\operatorname{spec}(m_2)$ is all negative, the direct form $\int_0^\infty dt\,e^{-tm_2}$ diverges and clause (2)'s unconditional form $\int_0^\infty dt\,m_2e^{-tm_2^2}$ is the one that applies; on the book branch it is all positive and the direct Laplace form converges, at rate the gap. The row's statement is unchanged either way — clause (2) is an *iff* whose truth does not depend on the branch, and clause (3)'s carve-out is discharged on both. Companion magnitudes imported into clause (3) are converted by the rule of R43, signs unchanged.

### R16 — Augmented $\Delta$-raised products of a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra: CFL relations hold at $(1,2,0)$, not implied at $(2,2,0)$ [mixed]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/026-linfty-to-ibl-repackaging.md` §3.1 ($(1,2,0)\Leftrightarrow[Q,\ell_2]=0$, the $\min(k,\ell)=1$ corollary), §3.2 (Prop. 3.2, obstruction), §3.3 (adjudication of R16); `agent/derivations/010-a4-220-relation.md` (Lemmas A, B1 as raw identities; superseded on the $(2,2,0)$ claim)
- **Scope**: genus zero; augmented family $\{p_{k,\ell}\}_{k,\ell\ge1}$ of a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra on a finite-dimensional graded space; the proven counterexample CE($\mathfrak{sl}_2$) has ghost numbers 1 and 4 only, so the failure on the physical sector (both inputs at ghost 2) is seen on the toy generator (iv) only; not evaluated on the closed-SFT algebra (026 §9.4 (i)); cyclic dg Lie algebras satisfy $(2,2,0)$ iff $\ell_2\circ_1\ell_2=0$ (026 (3.18)); weakest relaxation: the non-augmented family satisfies every genus-zero relation (R31); for $k,\ell\ge2$ the relation is equivalent to $P_{k,\ell}=0$ (Prop. 3.2), shown not implied at every such $(k,\ell)$ by 026 §9 Cor. 9.2 (for the given products; removed at $(k,\ell)\ne(2,2)$ by a cyclic $L_\infty$ isomorphism, 026 §9.4; node check 2026-09-30)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-07-03 – numerical check – `codes/verify-220-relation.py` (numpy, dim-16 generic toy), all checks PASS; check 7b (the "$=0$ by Jacobi" step) is a hard-coded PASS (026 §3.3, critic 026A)
  - 2026-07-09 – numerical check – `codes/verify-220-relation.py` re-run after comment-level hygiene, all PASS
  - 2026-07-09 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-r16-220-20260709T140000.md`, blind, ACCEPT (0 fatal, 0 serious); withdrawn on the $(2,2,0)$ headline on 2026-09-08 (verified the lemmas and signs, not the vanishing step)
  - 2026-09-08 – independent re-derivation – derivation 026 §3.1–§3.3 (sub-task 7b): $(1,2,0)$ proven, $(2,2,0)$ false (Prop. 3.2)
  - 2026-09-08 – numerical check – `codes/verify-linfty-ibl-repackaging.py` checks 3c/E5/E6, 81/81 PASS
  - 2026-09-08 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-026A-r16-reconciliation-20260908T101500.md`, verdict (a): 026's adjudication correct in substance, own hand proof and reading audit of 010's script (no shell)
  - 2026-09-26 – exact check – `codes/verify-r16-scope.py`, 183/183 PASS, seed 20260926 (record 026 §9); orchestrator re-run identical
  - 2026-09-30 – node check – `agent/reports/critic-026-s9-node-20260930T233600.md` – (9.4), Prop. 9.1, Cor. 9.2 hold (re-derived by hand; witness isomorphic to a dg Lie algebra at $(k,\ell)\ne(2,2)$, vanishing on ghost-2 inputs — scope completed accordingly)
- **Status**:
  - $(1,2,0)\Leftrightarrow[Q,\ell_2]=0$, and every $\min(k,\ell)=1$ relation, all sectors: Proven (026 §3.1, (3.10))
  - $(2,2,0)$ not implied by the cyclic $L_\infty$ relations, on the full graded space: Proven (026 §3.2 Prop. 3.2; CE($\mathfrak{sl}_2$), (3.18)–(3.19), check E3)
  - failure on the physical sector at $(2,2)$, and failure at $(2,3)$, $(3,2)$: Numerical (generator (iv), seed 20260908; $\lvert P_{2,2}\rvert=20.3$ on ghost-2 inputs, check E5; 7c table)
  - $(k,\ell,0)$ for every $k,\ell\ge2$ not implied by the cyclic $L_\infty$ relations, for the given products on the full graded space: Proven (026 §9 Prop. 9.1/Cor. 9.2); Verified `codes/verify-r16-scope.py` 183/183, seed 20260926

**A4, as corrected: the augmented relations hold at $(1,2,0)$ and are not implied at $(2,2,0)$; A4's original $(2,2,0)$ claim was wrong.** The positive result is Theorem A$'$ (R31): the $\tilde\Delta$-raised products of a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra satisfy the genus-zero relations in non-augmented (generating-function) form at every $(k,\ell)$, and the augmented $(2,2,0)$ relation plays no role in the HS theory or in the all-orders tower (026 §6 Interpretation, §7). For a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra the $\Delta$-raised products satisfy the genus-zero CFL relations at $\min(k,\ell)=1$ only; at $(2,2,0)$ the CFL relation fails by $\tfrac12M_{2,2}\ne0$, equivalently by the extra condition $P_{k,\ell}=0$ ($P=-\tfrac12M$), which at every $(k,\ell)$ with $k,\ell\ge2$ the cyclic $L_\infty$ relations do not imply on the full graded space: the cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra $\mathrm{CE}(\mathfrak{sl}_2)\oplus\langle e_{(2)},e^*_{(3)}\rangle$ with $S=e^{x}S_{\rm CE}$ has $P_{k,\ell}=-\tfrac12\frac{2^{k-2}+2^{\ell-2}-1}{(k-2)!(\ell-2)!}x^{k-2}y^{\ell-2}M^{\rm CE}_{2,2}\ne0$ at all of them (026 §9, Prop. 9.1/Cor. 9.2, proven; `codes/verify-r16-scope.py` 183/183 at $k,\ell\le5$ and $(2,6)$, $(6,2)$). This witness vanishes identically on ghost-2 inputs, and at $(k,\ell)\ne(2,2)$ it is removed by the cyclic $L_\infty$ isomorphism $(c,c^*,x,x^*)\mapsto(e^{x}c,e^{-x}c^*,x,x^*-c^*_ic^i)$, which maps it to a dg Lie algebra: the non-implication concerns the given products, not their isomorphism class (the $(2,2)$ failure of CE($\mathfrak{sl}_2$) is invariant). On the generator (iv) the failure is also seen at $(2,2)$ on ghost-2 inputs (value 20.3, check E5) and at $(2,3)$, $(3,2)$ (026 §3.2, 7c table). Excl. within cyclic $L_\infty$ algebras (a not-implied statement): $P_{k,\ell}$ is not evaluated on the closed-SFT algebra; cyclic dg Lie algebras satisfy the augmented relation at every $(k,\ell)\ne(2,2)$; the family closes only when extended by $p_{k,0}=V_k$ and $p_{0,\ell}=\tilde\Delta^{\otimes\ell}V_\ell$ (non-augmented, 026 §3.4). What was wrong in 010: the final step "$=0$ by Jacobi" was never computed (script check 7b is a hard-coded PASS) and is false — the tensor $W\Delta\circ([Q,\ell_3]+\ell_2\circ_1\ell_2)$ with plain-$\Delta$ products is not the cyclic $L_\infty$ relation (its $Q$-part is not the symmetric $\delta_QV_4$; 026 check 3c, residual 19.7; critic 026A §B). 010's lemmas A, B1 and 6b are correct raw identities and are non-hollow on generic data in 010's convention; 010's falsification legs tested those raw identities, not the vanishing. The separate "end-to-end on an $L_\infty$ algebra" check of 026's sub-task 7a is hollow (mixed $\Delta\otimes\tilde\Delta$ raising makes every term $\bar\varepsilon$-antisymmetric, $0=0$ after projection; 026 (3.23)–(3.24), E6) and is not evidence either way. Survives: $(1,2,0)\Leftrightarrow[Q,\ell_2]=0$; Lemma A, Lemma B1 as raw identities; byproduct 1 (outputs $\bar\varepsilon$-symmetric, 026 (1.7)). Sub-family reading: the augmented components of $V_{\le4}$ need $p_{1,3}$ as well and still fail $(2,2,0)$ (026 §6, W12). The claims "closes the open core of the restricted HS correspondence at $O(g_s^2)$" and "the full genus-zero chain holds" are struck. **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** "All $k,\ell\ge2$" was narrowed to the three signatures the source checks, and the proven non-augmented theorem was put next to the negative, which the headline alone did not convey. Widened again on 2026-09-26: 026 §9 Cor. 9.2 proves the non-implication at every $k,\ell\ge2$ for the given products (node check 2026-09-30). Widened again on 2026-09-26: 026 §9 Cor. 9.2 proves the non-implication at every $k,\ell\ge2$ for the given products (node check 2026-09-30).

**Annotation (2026-09-26).** The original A4 claim, that the $\Delta$-raised products satisfy the augmented $(2,2,0)$ relation, was withdrawn on 2026-09-08/13 (026 §3.2–§3.3, critics 026A/026B); this entry states the corrected result. The non-augmented relations are R31.

### R17 — B1: O(g_s³) step – V₅(Σ,Σ,Ψ)/5! unique, reproduces the quintic on shell, gauge law extends [Conditional(PH1, PH5) – Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/011-b1-quintic-milestone.md` §1 (uniqueness), §2 (exact rewriting (B.2), coefficient), §3 (diagram enumeration), §4 (gauge law (B.3), (B.8)/(B.9)), §7
- **Scope**: finite-dimensional algebraic layer (generic totally graded-symmetric $V_4$, $V_5$; $V_4$ invertible on $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ or on the quotient by $\ker V_4$); tree level, five points, $O(g_s^3)$ with gauge invariance to $O(g_s^4)$; coupling unique on the minimal field content $(\Psi,\Sigma_2)$; CFT analytics of $V_4^{-1}$, $K_\Psi^{-1}$ (A2(iii)/T4) and region identification not covered; the five-point double collapse needs $V_5$ to vanish on $\ker V_4$ (R38) when $\ker V_4\ne0$
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-07-03 – numerical check – `codes/verify-b1-quintic.py` (numpy, $\operatorname{Sym}^2$-basis toy, generic tensors), 9/9 PASS; check 4's factor 2 is a consistency check, not independent, and check 5's tolerance was loosened (critic)
  - 2026-07-04 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-b1-quintic-20260704T091727.md`, blind, ACCEPT (0 fatal, 0 serious); load-bearing chain hand-re-derived (uniqueness on minimal content, (B.2), feedback $g_s^4/600$, saddle, class-(c) telescoping, (B.3) via (B.8)); script not re-executed
  - 2026-09-21 – numerical check – `codes/verify-covering-m06.py` check `C2.2-raw` (derivation 036): the double collapse fails on a generic $V_5$ when $V_4$ has a kernel; the dependence on R38 made explicit (critic-confirmed, preamble annotation of 2026-09-21)

**B1 — the O(g_s³) quintic milestone of Route B (dynamical closure at the first nontrivial order).** With the O(g_s²) part fixed (R1 `eq:SHS`), the unique cubic completion at O(g_s³) is $S_3=S_{\rm HS}+\tfrac{g_s^3}{5!}V_5(\Sigma,\Sigma,\Psi)$ — coefficient **1/5! forced** by on-shell matching (falsifiable: $c\neq1$ leaves $(c-1)\tfrac{g_s^3}{5!}V_5(\Psi^5)$), coupling unique (5=2+2+1; tadpoles and kernel modifications excluded; ghost saturation $4{+}4{+}2=10=6+\dim_{\mathbb R}\mathcal M_{0,5}$ ✓). **Exact rewriting** (identity in fields, all orders): $S_3=S^{(5)}_{\rm eff}-\tfrac{g_s^2}{4!}K_\Psi(\tilde X,\tilde X)+\tfrac{g_s^4}{600}\,vK_\Psi^{-1}v$ with dressed kernel $K_\Psi=V_4-\tfrac{g_s}{5}V_5(\cdot,\cdot,\Psi)$, $v=V_5(\cdot,\Psi^{\otimes2},\Psi)$, $\tilde X=\Sigma-\Psi^{\otimes2}-\tfrac{g_s}{5}K_\Psi^{-1}v$; Σ-EOM = $\tilde X=0$; the O(g_s⁴) **feedback** term is explicit (= R12 triangularity made exact; the precise target the V₆ step must absorb — spec in derivation 011 §6). **Diagrams:** at O(g_s³) (5-pt tree) exactly three classes — (V₃)³ shared; V₃×[Σ-exch] = R14 collapse; [src–Σprop–V₅–Σprop–src] = 15 assignments × $\tfrac{g_s^3}{15}V_5$ = contact with coefficient **1**; **no unwanted diagrams** (first candidates at O(g_s⁴)/one loop; Σ-tadpole on V₅ = first Σ-loop, quantum tower). **Gauge law extends exactly (R11 analogue):** with $\delta\tilde X=\tfrac{g_s}{10}K_\Psi^{-1}V_5(\cdot,\tilde X,\delta\Psi)$ the dressed quadratic form is exactly invariant and $\delta S_3-\delta S^{(5)}_{\rm eff}=\delta[\text{feedback}]$ **exactly** (off-shell, finite, arbitrary δΨ) ⇒ $S_3$ gauge-invariant to O(g_s⁴), the same order as the truncation; corrected composite law = "Σ transforms as its perturbative saddle + homogeneous rotation of the deviation"; naive composite law falsified at O(g_s³). Moduli inherited region-by-region (R14(b) carve-out language); Σ-props stay moduli-free. **Carve-outs:** finite-dim algebraic layer (CFT analytics = A2(iii)/T4); region identification inherited; BV classification of the homogeneous X̃-term **SETTLED (R22/derivation 016, ST4): auxiliary-field/contractible-pair law, neither HT-trivial (μ graded-symmetric) nor nontrivial (no independent parameter)**; O(g_s⁴) = next milestone — [**note: 011 §6's guess that the master equation is the O(g_s⁴) closure mechanism was FALSIFIED by R19; the feedback-absorption part of the spec is confirmed, the "c₆=1 / master-eq-as-closure" part is not**].

**Annotation (2026-09-21, from derivation 036, critic-confirmed with an independent hand re-derivation and a falsifying check).** R17's five-point double collapse $\mathcal V_4\,p_{2,2}^{-1}\,\mathcal V_5\,p_{2,2}^{-1}\,\mathcal V_4=\mathcal V_5$ **requires the R38 condition** ($\mathcal V_5$ vanishing on $\ker p_{2,2}$), equivalently $\Pi^\top V_5\Pi=V_5$: on a generic $\mathcal V_5$ whose $\mathcal V_4$ has a kernel the collapse fails (`codes/verify-covering-m06.py`, check `C2.2-raw`). Every $O(g_s^{\ge3})$ toy that used a generic invertible $\mathcal V_4$ never saw this. R17 itself is unchanged where $\ker\mathcal V_4=0$ or R38 holds; the dependence is now explicit rather than implicit.

### R18 — T6 at $O(g_s^2)$, split verdict: no independent $\Lambda_1$ needed in $S_{\rm HS}$ (Q-need); whether $S_{\rm HS}$ is a gauge fixing (Q-can) unresolved [mixed]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/012-t6-gauge-tower.md` §1 (no $\hat Q$-raising kinetic term, no flat direction), §2 (kernel shifts), §3 (Stückelberg), §4 ($\Pi$-partner), §5 (Route C), §6 (tower reading), §7 (verdict table); revised 2026-07-05
- **Scope**: $O(g_s^2)$ action $S_{\rm HS}$ on the minimal content $(\Psi,\Sigma_2)$ and Route C's multiplier tower; finite-dimensional algebraic layer for the machine-checked parts; (Q-can) not answered – a same-saturation non-$\hat Q$ enhancement $K=V_4+\Delta K$ is not excluded (T6-bis); (5) refutes one candidate law, not every $\Lambda_1$-symmetry; closure of the $\Pi$-partner construction is PH13 (open); the body's appeal to 'R6 $m_2$ $Q$-exact' rests on the withdrawn R6 and supports no Status line

- **Verification**:

  - 2026-07-04 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-t6-gauge-tower-20260704T091730.md`, blind, REVISE (0 fatal, 2 serious, both on the scope of the negative verdict; saturation arithmetic hand-verified)
  - 2026-07-05 – critic review – REVISE addressed by author revision of 012: §0/§1/§7 narrowed from "no hidden $\Lambda_1$" to "no $\hat Q$-raising diagonal kinetic term / no unfixed flat direction", can-vs-need framing added, §5 rescoped; not re-reviewed
  - 2026-09-21 – critic review – rigor audit (`knowledge/assessments/rigor-audit-2026-09-21.md`; `agent/reports/planner-rigor-B-20260921T132500.md`, `agent/reports/critic-rigor-audit-20260921T140000.md`): two sub-clauses overstated ((4), (5)); narrowed in the entry
- **Status**:
  - (1) no $\hat Q$-raising diagonal kinetic term in $S_{\rm HS}$ (ghost saturation) – Proven; no unfixed flat direction beyond $\ker V_4$ – Conditional(PH1) – Proven
  - (2) kernel shifts $\delta\Sigma\in\ker V_4$ are an exact two-string symmetry of $S_{\rm HS}$; the R13 quotient is their gauge fixing – Proven
  - (3) Stückelberg enlargement exact, gauge $B=0$ leaves the composite law – Proven; $\Pi$-partner: $\delta\tilde S=-\tfrac{2g_s^2}{4!}\mathcal A(\Lambda_1,X)$ at leading order, $\mathcal A$ the master-equation boundary bilinear – Proven (leading order); admissibility of $\Pi=0$ ⟺ $m_2$ surjective onto the ghost-5 $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ sector – Conditional(PH13) – Argued (012 §4 item 2, one line)
  - (4) Route C carries no independent $\hat Q$-acting parameter (unit-triangular Jacobian) – Proven
  - (5) the candidate $\delta\Psi=g_s\ell_2(\Lambda_1)$ is not a symmetry of $S_{\rm eff}$ – Proven; "no independent $\Lambda_1$ needed at all orders of Route B" – not a result of this entry (the content of PH12)

**T6 — gauge tower / gauge-fixing question, split verdict: (Q-need) answered, (Q-can) unresolved.** Distinguish **(Q-can)** *can* $S_{\rm HS}$ be presented as the gauge fixing of a Λ₁-theory (the question as posed) from **(Q-need)** is an independent Λ₁ *needed*. (1) **No *Q̂-raising* diagonal kinetic term / no unfixed flat direction inside $S_{\rm HS}$** : ghost saturation forbids a diagonal Q̂-kinetic term ($\omega_2(\Sigma,\hat Q\Sigma)\equiv0$: $4{+}4{+}1=9\neq10$; the open-stub diagonal term saturates because the open auxiliary sits at $1{+}1{+}1=3$ — the open-string two-parameter mechanism transplants to closed strings **only in off-diagonal/partner form**); and $V_4$ invertible on the R13 quotient (PH1) ⇒ no unfixed flat directions beyond $\ker V_4$. **But invertibility does NOT exclude an already-gauge-fixed reading** (gauge-fixed operators are invertible by construction; R11's contractible pair is *compatible with* $S_{\rm HS}$ being a gauge fixing — the further appeal to "R6 $m_2$ $Q$-exact" misreads R6, which is withdrawn, and supports no Status line), and the count rules out only *Q̂-raising* terms — a **same-saturation non-Q̂ enhancement $K=V_4+\Delta K$ is NOT excluded and is OPEN (T6-bis)**; this is the (Q-can) crux, unresolved. So (1) answers (Q-need) = no, not (Q-can). (2) The **genuine** two-string gauge symmetry of $S_{\rm HS}$ = kernel shifts $\delta\Sigma\in\ker V_4$; **the R13 quotient is precisely their gauge fixing.** (3) Enlargements realizing the scenario: **Stückelberg** $S_{\rm HS}[\Psi,\Sigma-\hat QB]$, $N_{\rm gh}(B)=3$ — exact, trivial, gauge $B{=}0$ leaves exactly the composite law (machine-checked); **nontrivial conditional candidate** = ghost-5 partner $\Pi$ with $\tilde S=S_{\rm HS}+\langle\Pi,\hat QX\rangle_{\omega_2}$: $\delta\Sigma=\hat Q\Lambda_1$, $\delta\Pi=-\tfrac{2g_s^2}{4!}m_2(\Lambda_1)+\dots$ cancels all but a geometric-master-equation boundary anomaly (cure = L∞-covariantized constraint $\langle\Pi,\mathcal Q_\Psi X\rangle$ — **closure OPEN, ST2; PH13**); gauge $\Pi{=}0$ recovers $S_{\rm HS}$ with residual = composite law, and **admissibility of $\Pi{=}0$ ⟺ $m_2$ surjectivity — R9-invertibility in a new role (gauge-fixability)** (Conditional(PH13) – Argued, Status (3)). (4) **Excl. within independent $\hat Q$-acting parameters on the multiplier tower: Route C carries none**: constraint Jacobian unit-triangular ⇒ invertible for every Ψ ⇒ no flat directions (machine-checked); an enlargement of Route C is *not* excluded (012 §5 scope, critic C1); sharpens R13's open gauge item (S_C gauge algebra = pulled-back Ψ-algebra; explicit λ-compensations = ST5). (5) Tower reading: the candidate $\delta\Psi=g_s\ell_2(\Lambda_1)$ is not a symmetry of $S_{\rm eff}$ (one candidate refuted, 012 §6 caveat C2); that the tower is a quantum/loop BV structure is an attribution (Münster–Sachs), not computed here; B1's homogeneous law (R17) supports "no independent Λ₁ ever needed" for Route B at all orders (conjecture; PH12). **Annotation 2026-09-14 (024 §4.3–4.4; R34):** T6-bis answered on the bar–cobar side — an independent $\Lambda_{1,2}\in\mathrm{Sym}^2\mathcal H$, $N_{\rm gh}=3$, exists in CMZ's enlarged theory, is used up in reaching the canonical gauge $\Phi_{f\ge2}=0$, and is not a symmetry of $S_{\rm HS}$ on type-I fields (R18(1) saturation argument; toy G4 weak corroboration only); ST2 (an enlarged *action* with independent $\Lambda_1$) unchanged; ST5 (BV of Route C): obstacle named in 024 §5.3(b). **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** Two sub-clauses were narrowed to what 012 establishes: §5 shows Route C carries no independent $\Lambda_1$, not that it admits no enlargement, and §6 refutes one candidate law, not every $\Lambda_1$-symmetry.

**Annotation (2026-10-17).** The $\hat Q$-based statements (1)–(5) – ghost-number exclusion, $\hat Q$ Stückelberg, $\Pi$-partner – do not address the gauge-unfixing question of 2026-10-13 (a quadratic form $\langle\Sigma_2,q_{2,2}(\Sigma_2)\rangle$ and a law $\delta_{\Lambda,\Lambda_2}\Sigma_2$ whose gauge $B_2\Sigma_2=0$ gives back the present action, with no reason for $Q$ to appear): they are not to be quoted as an answer to it (`knowledge/decisions.md`, 2026-10-13, "Unfixing the gauge of $\Sigma_2$"; workflow `agent/state/gauge-unfixing-2026-10-13.md`).

**B3 — the O(g_s⁴) sextic milestone. Excl. within the field content $(\Psi,\Sigma_2)$, generic tensors (PH14): the geometric $V_6(\Sigma^3)$ alone leaves the two-$V_5$ $\Sigma$-exchange feedback uncancelled, and the forced vertex is dressed, $W_6=V_6-\tfrac{6}{5}\,\mathcal C$; with one more field the pure geometric $V_6$ does suffice (R24). The master equation is NOT what closes the matching.** (1) **Uniqueness:** at O(g_s⁴) only three-Σ couplings are admissible — every other cubic addition (Ψ³, source/coupling/kinetic corrections, ΣΨ-mixing, tadpole) contaminates an n-pt tree at $g_s^4\neq g_s^{n-2}$ (exclusion table). (2) **Failure of V₆ alone:** on-shell mismatch $=\tfrac{g_s^4}{600}vV_4^{-1}v+(c_6-1)\tfrac{g_s^4}{720}V_6(\Psi^6)+O(g_s^5)$; $vV_4^{-1}v$ and $V_6(\Psi^6)$ are independent invariants ⇒ **no $c_6$ cancels the R17 feedback** (falsifies 011 §6's naive expectation). (3) **Forced dressed vertex:** $\tfrac{g_s^4}{6!}W_6(\Sigma^3)$, $W_6=V_6-\tfrac65\mathcal C$, with $\mathcal C(A,B,Z)=\sum_{\rm strands}\langle V_5(\cdot,A,z^{(1)}),V_4^{-1}V_5(\cdot,B,z^{(2)})\rangle$ the **Σ-channel exchange composite** (two V₀,₅ blocks + one Σ-prop, third Σ strand-split; $\mathcal C(P,P,P)=vV_4^{-1}v$); $(c_6,\theta)=(1,6/5)$ forced; kernel unique up to Veronese-diagonal-null forms (presentation freedom); ghost saturation consistent (V₆: 12=4+4+4; in $\mathcal C$: V₅ 10=4+2+4, prop pairing 8). (4) **Exact rewriting** (any $(c_6,\theta)$, identity in fields): $S_4=S^{(6)}_{\rm eff}-\tfrac{g_s^2}{4!}K_6(\tilde X_6^2)+\tfrac{g_s^4}{6!}T(X^3)+\tfrac{g_s^4}{600}v_6K_6^{-1}v_6+\tfrac{g_s^4}{6!}[T(P^3)-V_6(\Psi^6)]$, $K_6=K_\Psi-\tfrac{g_s^2}{10}T(\cdot,\cdot,P)$, $v_6=v+\tfrac{g_s}{4}T(\cdot,P,P)$ — the square no longer completes (cubic deviation term); on-shell split **exact**: feedback $\mathbb F_5=O(g_s^5)$ (analytic $A_5=\tfrac1{600}[\tfrac12T(V_4^{-1}v,P,P)+\tfrac15vV_4^{-1}WV_4^{-1}v]$) + $O(g_s^7)$ cubic + $O(g_s^{10})$ quadratic remainders. (5) **Diagrams:** the new 6-pt class = two-V₅ Σ-exchange, 90 assignments × $\tfrac{g_s^4}{75}$(kernel); sum = polarized feedback = polarized $\theta\mathcal C$-contact (exact polarization) ⇒ exchange class cancelled identically by the $\mathcal C$-part of $W_6$. (6) **Gauge law goes implicit but stays homogeneous:** $\delta\Sigma=\delta[\Sigma_*(\Psi)]+\delta Y$, $\delta Y=\mathcal K^{-1}[-\tfrac12(\delta\mathcal K)(\cdot,Y)+\tfrac{g_s^2}{20}T(\cdot,Y,\delta Y)]$ (Neumann-solvable) ⇒ $\delta S_4-\delta S^{(6)}_{\rm eff}=\delta[\mathbb F_5]$ **exactly** off-shell; $\delta Y(Y{=}0)=0$; naive and 011-(B.8) laws falsified; **still no independent two-string parameter** (R18 pattern at two consecutive orders). (7) **Structural lesson:** all checks pass with generic (non-L∞) tensors — Route-B matching is completion-of-squares + Σ-channel exchange subtraction, **master-equation-agnostic**; the master equation's entire content sits in the target $S^{(6)}_{\rm eff}$ (amplitudes + gauge invariance), which the exact equalities *transfer* to $S_4$. All-orders skeleton conjectured: $W_n$ = target vertex − colour-2-reducible composites, recursive feedback, homogeneous implicit gauge laws (derivation 013 §8 = next-session spec). **Carve-outs:** finite-dim layer (CFT analytics of $V_4^{-1},K_6^{-1},\mathcal C$ = A2(iii)/T4); regions inherited; composite-vs-elementary kernel presentational question (early Σ₃?) open; BV class of the implicit law open (ST4 scope); perturbative-branch statements (toy saddle can fold at large $g_s$). **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** "The pure geometric $V_6$ FAILS" was scoped to the field content $(\Psi,\Sigma_2)$, since R24 closes the same order with the undressed geometric $V_6(\Sigma^3)$ and a third field.

**Annotation (2026-10-01).** Per 034 §5.1 and the §5.2 table, the dressed vertex $W_6=V_6-\tfrac65\mathcal C$ and $K_6^{-1}$ descend to $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0/\ker V_4$ only under R38's (1.2) and, in addition, 034's (C$^{(6)}$): $V_6(\Xi,A,B)=0$ for $\Xi\in\ker V_4$, $A,B\in\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0$. The condition list does not carry (C$^{(6)}$) (PH5's second clause covers $V_5$ only). It is vacuous where $\ker V_4=0$ (level 0, R53). Not registered as a hypothesis; the natural home is a third clause of PH5.

**R19 annotation (2026-10-01, R75).** When $\ker V_4\ne0$ and (1.2) fails, $W_6$ exists in the hybrid presentation with $V_4^{-1}\to G_C$ (R75); the 2026-10-01 annotation's (C$^{(6)}$) is a condition of the quotient presentation only and is moot in the hybrid.

### R20 — excl. within $(\Psi,\Sigma_2,\Sigma_3)$, elementary vertices, generic tensors: $O(g_s^4)$ sextic does not close [Conditional(PH1, PH5, PH14 [$O(g_s^4)$]) – Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/014-b4-sigma3-elementary.md` §1 (admissible couplings, $n=7$ septic obstruction), §2 (Gaussian elimination, on-shell collapse $\mathcal D(P,P)=V_6(\Psi^6)$), §3 (mismatch on generic tensors), §4 ($\mathcal D\ne\mathcal C$), §5 (auxiliarity condition, $L_\infty$-robustness), §7
- **Scope**: excl. within the content $(\Psi,\Sigma_2,\Sigma_3)$ with elementary geometric vertices, any $V_6$-adjustment and either sign of the $\Sigma_3$ kinetic term (014 (2.2), (3.1)), generic finite-dimensional tensors ($V_6\rvert_{\operatorname{Sym}^3}$ invertible, so that $\Sigma_3$ can be integrated out; at the string vertices this is the elementary analogue of PH2, not registered), tree level, $O(g_s^4)$; outside it, a second $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ field $\chi$ with a wrong-sign kinetic term closes the order with geometric vertices only (R24) – the weakest relaxation, a change of field content –, and a $\Sigma_3$ paired with the dressed vertex $\tilde V_6$ closes with a composite kinetic term (annotation below); outcome B at CFT level not decided by the toy; CFT analytics of $V_4^{-1}$ and $V_6^{-1}$ on $\operatorname{Sym}^3$ not covered; gauge law of the non-closing action not derived
- **Verification**:

  - 2026-07-06 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-b4-sigma3-20260706T091500.md`, blind, ACCEPT (0 fatal, 0 serious); every load-bearing claim hand-re-derived; critic added the $n=7$ septic obstruction
  - 2026-07-06 – numerical check – after the author revision F5–F8 (scope narrowed, power counting into §1), check-2 tolerance tightened $3\cdot10^{-3}\to10^{-3}$, still 7/7 PASS

**B4 — the $O(g_s^4)$ sextic in the $\Sigma_2\oplus\Sigma_3$ elementary-vertex presentation does NOT close (Harold's alternative to R19's composite $W_6$; answers R19 §3d/§8's open flag for one enlargement of the field content).** Instead of the composite $W_6=V_6-\tfrac65\mathcal C$ (with $V_4^{-1}$ *inside* the vertex), introduce $\Sigma_3\in\Sym^3\mathcal H_0$ ($N_{\rm gh}=6$) with **elementary** vertices only: bilinear kinetic $V_6(\Sigma_3,\Sigma_3)$ + cubic source $V_6(\Sigma_3,\Sigma_2,\Psi)$. (1) **Exact Gaussian elimination of $\Sigma_3$** gives the $\Sigma_3$-channel exchange $\mathcal D(\Sigma_2,\Sigma_2)=V_6(\cdot,\Sigma_2,\Psi)^\top(V_6\rvert_{\Sym^3})^{-1}V_6(\cdot,\Sigma_2,\Psi)$, **quadratic in $\Sigma_2$**. (2) **On-shell $\mathcal D$ collapses to the CONTACT** $\mathcal D(P,P)=V_6(\Psi^6)$ (Sym³ completeness, generic, no $\Loo$ input) — the $\Sigma_3$ sector supplies only the *target* sextic. (3) **The R17/R19 feedback $\tfrac{g_s^4}{600}vV_4^{-1}v$ survives uncancelled:** it is generated by the *forced* $O(g_s^3)$ coupling $V_5(\Sigma_2,\Sigma_2,\Psi)$, is built from $V_4,V_5$ **only** (exactly $V_6$-independent), and the quadratic-in-$\Sigma_2$ $\mathcal D$ cannot reach the cubic-in-$\Sigma_2$ $\mathcal C=V_5V_4^{-1}V_5$ it must subtract. Joint on-shell mismatch $=\tfrac{g_s^4}{600}vV_4^{-1}v+\tfrac{g_s^4}{720}(c_6+\tfrac{b^2}{4a}-1)V_6(\Psi^6)+O(g_s^5)$; matching the contact leaves the residual $\neq0$ for **generic** tensors (not outcome A). (4) **The $V_4^{-1}\to V_6^{-1}$ "trade" is not an identity:** a $V_6^{-1}$ appears but in a *different-field-degree* composite ($\mathcal D=V_6V_6^{-1}V_6\to$ contact) than $\mathcal C$ ($=V_5V_4^{-1}V_5\to$ feedback); on-shell diagonals $V_6(\Psi^6)$ vs $vV_4^{-1}v$ are independent invariants. (5) **Enumeration closed by the $\Sigma_3$-exchange power counting:** a $\Sigma_3$-source at $O(g_s^k)$ gives an exchange at $O(g_s^{2k-4})$ ⇒ only $k=4$ contributes at $O(g_s^4)$; a cubic-in-$\Sigma_2$ source would need $V_7(\Sigma_3,\Sigma_2,\Sigma_2)$ ($n{=}3{+}2{+}2{=}7$, **septic, $O(g_s^5)$** — forbidden), so the source is necessarily linear in $\Sigma_2$. (6) **$\Loo$-robust in the algebraic layer** (feedback $V_6$-independent; $\Sigma_3$-contact additive for *any* $V_6$ — an identity linear in $V_6$, not a draw accident): **outcome C, not B**. (7) **New auxiliarity condition (R9 analogue):** $\Sigma_3$ genuinely auxiliary $\iff$ $V_6$ non-degenerate on $\Sym^3\mathcal H_0$. **Consequence:** within the content $(\Psi,\Sigma_2,\Sigma_3)$, for either sign of the $\Sigma_3$ kinetic term (014 (2.2), (3.1)), the composite kernel $\mathcal C$ is irreducible there — one must *still* add a composite subtraction; R24 falsifies the strong conjecture with a different third field, a wrong-sign $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ partner $\chi$ (R24 critic C3; PH14, PH15); Route C (R13) is the complementary presentation that avoids the $\Sigma_3$-source (cubic constraint) but not the underlying $\Sigma_2$-feedback. **Scope (critic F5):** proved for the $\Sigma_2\oplus\Sigma_3$ content + any $V_6$-adjustment; the strong form "no elementary $V_n(\Sigma_a,\Sigma_b)$ presentation whatsoever" was falsified by R24 with a wrong-sign $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ partner $\chi$, a change of field content (power counting bars $\Sigma_4$ as an $O(g_s^4)$ auxiliary; R12 makes $\Sigma_3$ the natural next field). **Carve-outs:** finite-dim layer; CFT analytics of $V_4^{-1}$ and now **$V_6^{-1}$ on $\Sym^3$** = A2(iii)/T4; outcome-B at CFT level a carve-out the toy can't decide (R19 §7 gives structural grounds it fails there too); regions inherited; gauge law of the non-closing action deferred. **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** "Irreducible", "resolved in the NEGATIVE" and the strong conjecture were given their class (elementary vertices inside $(\Psi,\Sigma_2,\Sigma_3)$, either sign of the $\Sigma_3$ kinetic term), since R24 contradicts them with a wrong-sign $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ partner $\chi$ outside that content.

**Annotation to R20 (Harold, 2026-07-06 — preferred all-orders framing).** Equivalent, cleaner reading of R20: instead of reproducing the *bare* $V_6(\Psi^6)$ with $\Sigma_3$, pair $\Sigma_3$ with the **dressed effective vertex** $\tilde V_6$, $\tilde V_6(\Psi^{\otimes3},\Psi^{\otimes3})=V_6(\Psi^6)-\tfrac65 vV_4^{-1}v$ (= $W_6$ on the diagonal; the $-\tfrac65$ because the $\Sigma_2$ sector *overshoots* $S^{(6)}_{\rm eff}$ by $+\tfrac{g_s^4}{600}vV_4^{-1}v$, which the $\Sigma_3$-contact must subtract). This **closes** (established in R21: 015 §2, Lemma 2.1, checks 1–2) — but $\tilde V_6$ contains $V_4^{-1}$, so it is composite: R20 with the composite *relocated* from $W_6$ into the $\Sigma_3$-kinetic pairing, not eliminated (R20 answered the *elementary* question). **Uniform all-orders principle** (for the 013 §8 write-up): at each order, pair the new field with the **running effective vertex** $V_{n,\rm eff}=$ bare $V_n$ + all lower-order feedback (a 1PI-like dressing), then HS — vertices grow composite but the rule is uniform and the recursion closes ("more composite, not more ad hoc"). Preferred over the order-by-order dressed-$W_n$ bookkeeping.

**B5 — gauge transformations, EOMs, and IBL$_\infty$ rewriting at $O(g_s^4)$, both presentations (full understanding of the $O(g_s^4)$ order).** Investigates **(P-dressed)** = Harold's R20-Annotation ($\Sigma_3$ paired with the composite dressed effective vertex $\tilde V_6$, closes) as PRIMARY and **(P-elementary)** = R20 ($\Sigma_3$ with bare $V_6$, fails — outcome C) for the structural comparison. **(1) Gauge transformations.** The **$\Phi$-map construction** (§2, the central new object): $\Phi:\Sym^3\Hilb_0\to(\Sym^2\Hilb_0)^*$, $\Phi(X)_I=V_5(\Lambda^2_I,X)$, by which R19's composite $\mathcal C=V_5V_4^{-1}V_5$ **relocates** from the three-$\Sigma_2$ vertex $W_6$ into the $\Sigma_3$-kinetic pairing $\tilde M=M-\tfrac65 N$, $N=\Phi^\top V_4^{-1}\Phi$. Veronese collapses $\Phi(\Psi^{\otimes3})=v$ (Lemma 2.1), $\tilde M(\Psi^{\otimes3},\Psi^{\otimes3})=W_6(\Psi^6)$, $\tilde u(P)=\tilde M\,p_3$ are identities (checks 1-2, $10^{-13}$–$10^{-15}$) — confirms Harold's R20-Annotation in detail: the dressed presentation closes on-shell with $\Sigma_3^*=\Psi^{\otimes3}$ exactly, the composite absorbed into the kinetic. **$\Sigma_3$ gauge law: $\delta Y_3=0$** (trivial homogeneous — Gaussian + $\Psi$-independent kernel $\tilde M$/$M$; $\delta\Sigma_3=\delta\Sigma_3^*(\Psi,\Sigma_2)$; no homogeneous rotation of $Y_3$ at $O(g_s^4)$, first at $O(g_s^5)$ via $V_7=(3,2,2)$, R12; check 3, $\lvert {\rm var}\rvert =10^{-16}$). **$\Sigma_2$ gauge law: R17-type** (quadratic-form invariance — $T_{\rm dr}=0$ at this order since $c_6=0$ and the $\Sigma_3$-exchange $\mathcal E=s^\top K_3^{-1}s$ is quadratic in $\Sigma_2$, contributing to the Hessian $\mathcal K$ only) with $O(g_s^4)$-dressed Hessian; exact off-shell equality $\delta S-\delta S_{\rm eff}^{(6)}=\delta[\mathbb F]$ with $\mathbb F$ $\Sigma$-independent: **dressed closes** ($\mathbb F_5^{\rm dr}=O(g_s^5)$, check 4), **elementary = R20 residual** ($\mathbb F_4^{\rm elem}=\tfrac{g_s^4}{600}vV_4^{-1}v+\dots$, check 4); homogeneity $\delta Y\rvert_{Y=0}=0$ both sectors (check 5). **The structural comparison across presentations: gauge-law STRUCTURE IDENTICAL across presentations** — $\delta Y_3=0$, $\Sigma_2$ implicit/homogeneous, no independent $\Lambda_1$ (R18 pattern at three consecutive orders). The elementary failure is **dynamical** (on-shell contact $V_6(\Psi^6)$ not $W_6(\Psi^6)$), **not gauge-algebraic**; the gauge law does not see composite-vs-elementary; the master equation does not enter gauge invariance, only on-shell matching. Sharpens R19 §7. **(2) EOMs.** $\Sigma_3$ Gaussian (saddle $\Psi^{\otimes3}$ exact for $(a,b)=(1,2)$); $\Sigma_2$ implicit (R19 $\tilde X_6=0$ + $\Sigma_3$ back-reaction). Auxiliarity chain $V_4\rvert_{\Sym^2}$ (R9) + $K_3\rvert_{\Sym^3}$ (R20), tied in the dressed case ($\tilde M$ needs both $V_4$ and $V_6$). **(3) IBL$_\infty$ rewriting (tree level, $g=0$).** Products $\ell_4=p_{4,1,0}$, $\ell_5=p_{5,1,0}$, $p_{3,2,0}=\Delta\circ\ell_4$, $p_{4,2,0}=\Delta\circ\ell_5$ (bare), $\tilde p_{4,2,0}=\Delta\circ\tilde\ell_5$ (dressed), $N_{\rm gh}=3\ell-2k$. Relations $(4,1,0),(5,1,0)$ = $L_\infty$ Jacobi axioms (**$L_\infty$-required** — check 6: $\lvert R_{(4,1,0)}\rvert =5.3$ on generic data); [Withdrawn 2026-09-26, Status (3b) and the annotation below: "$(3,2,0),(4,2,0)$ = consequences (R16 lemma extension, even-parity sector). ### R22 — ST4: homogeneous auxiliary-rotation law neither Henneaux–Teitelboim trivial nor nontrivial [Conditional(PH1, PH5) – Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/016-st4-bv-homogeneous-law.md` §2 (HT graded-antisymmetry condition (16.2)), §3.1–§3.4 ($\mu$ graded-symmetric; compensator), §5 (result), §7
- **Scope**: finite-dimensional algebraic layer (CFT analytics of $K_\Psi^{-1}$ not covered); the laws $\delta\tilde X$ at $O(g_s^3)$ and $\delta Y_2$, $\delta Y_3$ at $O(g_s^4)$; the worldsheet $N_{\rm gh}$ of the antifield $\tilde X^+$ is a convention; the all-orders auxiliary-tower statement is suggested, not proved
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-07-06 – numerical check – `codes/verify-st4-bv-xtilde.py` (numpy $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ toy), 6/6 PASS; decisive check 2: $\mu$ symmetric, distance to antisymmetric $\approx277$
  - 2026-07-08 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-b5-st4-r21-r22-20260708T214500.md`, blind, Part II ACCEPT (0 fatal, 0 serious); (16.2) and the forced $\mu$ re-derived by hand; $\mu$ reproduces the 018/019 kernel-rotation law at $k=2$
  - 2026-07-09 – numerical check – re-run after relabelling (check 6's zero-rotation leg illustrative, 0=0 by construction; "odd-parity probe" reworded), 6/6 PASS

**ST4 — BV taxonomy of the homogeneous auxiliary-rotation law (settles R17 carve-out (c) / 011 §4 "small open point"; grounds B5's "trivial/implicit homogeneous" labels, R21 §4.2 line-509 flag).** The homogeneous term $\delta_h\tilde X=\tfrac{g_s}{10}K_\Psi^{-1}V_5(\cdot,\tilde X,\delta\Psi)$ (011 §4 B.8) is **neither a Henneaux–Teitelboim *trivial* gauge symmetry nor a *nontrivial* one — the dichotomy is the wrong axis.** (1) **HT-trivial FAILS (decisive graded computation):** writing $\delta_h\tilde X=\mu\,E_{\tilde X}$ on the $\tilde X$-EOM $E_{\tilde X}=-\tfrac{g_s^2}{12}K_\Psi\tilde X$, the forced generator $\mu=-\tfrac{6}{5g_s}K_\Psi^{-1}W_{\delta\Psi}K_\Psi^{-1}$ ($(W_{\delta\Psi})_{IJ}=V_5(\Lambda_I,\Lambda_J,\delta\Psi)$) is graded-**symmetric**, whereas HT-triviality for **even** fields requires $\mu$ **anti**symmetric. The condition is derived from first principles: $\mu^{ij}=-(-1)^{(\epsilon_i+\epsilon_j)(1+p)+\epsilon_i\epsilon_j}\mu^{ji}$; for even fields the parameter-parity factor $(\epsilon_i+\epsilon_j)(1+p)$ **drops out** ⇒ plain antisymmetry required regardless of $\delta\Psi$-parity. The graded rescue fails because the two exchanged $\Sym^2$-slots are both even and $\delta\Psi$ is a **spectator** in the $I\!\leftrightarrow\!J$ swap (its parity never enters the sign). (2) **Not even a standalone symmetry:** with $\Psi$ frozen $\delta_hS_3=-\tfrac{g_s^3}{120}V_5(\tilde X,\tilde X,\delta\Psi)\neq0$; it is a **compensator** for the $\delta K_\Psi$-anomaly ($2K_\Psi(\delta_h\tilde X,\tilde X)+(\delta K_\Psi)(\tilde X,\tilde X)=0$ = R17 check-5), glued to $\delta\Psi$ — classifying it in isolation is a category error. (3) **Nontrivial FAILS:** no independent parameter ($R^{\tilde X}_\Lambda\propto\tilde X$ vanishes on-shell; removed by $\tilde X=0$). **Unified home:** the forced transformation law of the **auxiliary field** $\tilde X$ under the single closed-string $\Lambda$-symmetry — the $O(g_s^3)$ descendant of R11's $(\tilde X,\tilde X^+)$ **contractible pair**, the sole new feature being the $\Psi$-dependent dressed kinetic $K_\Psi$ forcing a nonzero (EOM-proportional) rotation. Covers uniformly $\delta\tilde X$ ($O(g_s^3)$), $\delta Y_2$ ($O(g_s^4)$, 015 §4.2), and $\delta Y_3=0$ ($O(g_s^4)$, 015 §4.1 = field-independent-kinetic corner); all three are field-dependence-parametrised contractible-pair auxiliary laws, **none HT-trivial, none nontrivial**. Consistent with R18's "no independent two-string parameter" and B5's "obstruction dynamical not gauge-algebraic". **Carve-outs:** finite-dim algebraic layer (CFT analytics of $K_\Psi^{-1}$ = A2(iii)/T4); worldsheet-$N_{\rm gh}$ label of the antifield $\tilde X^+$ = convention (natural choice stated); all-orders auxiliary-tower statement suggested not proved.

**Annotation (2026-09-26).** (R60) the field-dependence-parametrised contractible pair is, in the hatted chart of 056, a constant-coefficient pair $(\hat Y^+,p\hat Y)$; the forced symmetric $\mu$ untouched; the all-orders tower beyond $k\le3$ not addressed.

### R23 — ST6: physical ghost number of IBL structure maps, $N_{\rm gh}(p_{k,\ell,g})=3\ell-2k+g$ ($g\le1$ derived, $g\ge2$ by ghost-additive gluing) [Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/009-a3-ibl-pedagogical.md` §2.2–§2.5 (corrected, ST6 2026-07-06, with the revision notice); `knowledge/notes/cubic-sft-ibl-pedagogical/cubic-sft-ibl-pedagogical.tex` §3.2 (eq:ngformula)
- **Scope**: genus 0 and genus 1 independently anchored (backbone and $\omega_2/V_4$ anchor at $g=0$, the $(1,1,1)$ relation at $g=1$); for $g\ge2$ the $+g$ is the uniform ghost-additive self-gluing convention, not an independent computation; with the strand degree $3-\Ngh$ on every input and output the CFL degree is matched exactly at $d=\tfrac12$ (026 §1.4 (1.11)–(1.12)), outside CFL's integer-$d$ hypotheses; the $g$-slope of the match rests on the $+g$ convention; slot symmetries need the per-slot parity dictionary (026 §1.2 (iii))
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-07-05 – exact check – original 11-check script (`agent/reports/computator-st6-ngh-formula-20260705T204500.md`), all PASS; later found tautological and convention-blind (its homogeneity checks pass equally for the stale values)
  - 2026-07-09 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-r23-ngh-20260709T121000.md`, blind, REVISE; the formula confirmed by hand re-derivation ($3-2n$ backbone, $+5$ $\Delta$-shift, $N_{\rm gh}(m_2)=+2$ anchor, $g=1$ coefficient, Hohm–Zwiebach citation)
  - 2026-07-09 – exact check – `codes/verify-st6-ngh-formula.py` restructured (support-constrained $V_3,V_4,\Delta$ on a physical-grading toy, ghost shifts measured $\{-1\},\{+2\},\{+4\}$, stale values excluded), 16/16 PASS; `codes/verify-a3-ibl-pedagogical.py` Hq4-sat re-run PASS
  - 2026-10-03 – critic node check – `agent/reports/critic-r23-r31-degree-node-20261003T203000.md` – the disclosure "cannot agree for any $d$" withdrawn (it shifted the output slots only); exact match at $d=\tfrac12$ with the strand degree $3-\Ngh$ on every strand, consistent with R31; formula and status unaffected; Scope, body, disclosure and carve-out replaced by the orchestrator

**ST6 — the two-parameter physical ghost-number formula for IBL structure maps (resolves the A3 §2.5 flag and the pedagogical-note §3.2 draftcomment of 2026-07-03).** The physical ghost number of the general IBL structure map is $\Ngh(\pklg)=3\ell-2k+g$ (boxed eq:ngformula in the note). **Derivation:** every output at $\Ngh=3$, every input at $\Ngh=2$ (out-minus-in), so genus-0 backbone $\Ngh(\pklg_{k,\ell,0})=3\ell-2k$; its $\ell=1$ slice is $\Ngh(\ell_k)=3-2k$; each extra output is a $\Delta$-raising, $+5=(+3\text{ output})-(-2\text{ input})$ (the $\Delta=\omega_1^{-1}$ is NOT metric-index raising: $\omega_1$ is the $\Ngh$-5 pairing (graded symmetric, not a symplectic form: R7, R58), so $\Delta$-raising converts an $\omega_1$-pairing input slot at $\Ngh=2$ into a genuine output slot at $\Ngh=3$, a net $+5$, not $+1$ — this kills the stale derivation-009 values $\Ngh(m_2)=-2$ and $\Ngh(p_{1,2,0})=0$). The genus coefficient $+1$ is fixed by $\Ngh$-homogeneity of the (1,1,1) loop relation: $\Ngh(p_{1,1,1})=+2=3-2+1$; each handle is an operadic self-gluing identifying one output leg ($\Ngh=3$) with one input leg ($\Ngh=2$), ghost excess $+1$. **Decisive independent anchor:** the $\Sigma$ kinetic term $\langle\Sigma,m_2(\Sigma)\rangle_{\omega_2}=V_4(\Sigma,\Sigma)$ with $\Sigma$ at $\Ngh=4$; $\omega_2=\omega_1^{\otimes 2}$ pairs at $\Ngh$ sum 10 (two $c_0^-$; two-sphere saturation); $V_4$ saturates at $\sum\Ngh=8=4+4$; hence $\Ngh(m_2(\Sigma))=10-4=6$, so $\Ngh(m_2)=6-4=+2$ — independent of any $\Delta$-shift convention. **Reconciliation with CFL:** the CFL degree $\lvert \pklg\rvert =-2d(k+g-1)-1$ is $\ell$-independent, while $\Ngh(\pklg)=3\ell-2k+g$ grows with $\ell$; the absence of a uniform per-field offset $\lvert \pklg\rvert =\pm\Ngh(\pklg)+\text{const}$ is **expected, not a defect** — it is the ordinary consequence of the maps being multilinear ($k$ inputs) AND multi-output ($\ell$ outputs), each slot carrying its own suspension unit, exactly the $L_\infty$ analogy (Hohm \& Zwiebach 2017, arXiv:1701.08824 §3.1–§3.2: $\deg b_n=-1$ constant in the suspended $b$-picture vs $\deg\ell_n=n-2$ growing in the $\ell$-picture). The per-slot dictionary gives every strand, input or output, the CFL degree $3-\Ngh$ (CFL's homological sign): $\lvert\pklg\rvert=3\ell-3k-\Ngh(\pklg)=-(k+g)$, which is $-2d(k+g-1)-1$ at $d=\tfrac12$ for every $(k,\ell,g)$ (026 §1.4). **Disclosure (critic S2, corrected 2026-10-03):** the CFL degree is matched exactly, not in $\ell$-independence only: with the strand degree $3-\Ngh$ on inputs and outputs (CFL's $C[1]$), $\lvert\pklg\rvert=-(k+g)$, which is CFL's degree at $d=\tfrac12$, the only value any affine per-strand regrading admits, outside CFL's integer-$d$ hypotheses ($\lvert\hbar\rvert=1$, odd). The former "no $d$" ($k$-slope $d=1$, $g$-slope $d=-\tfrac12$) shifted the output slots only and is withdrawn. Not matched by this regrading: the slot symmetries (inputs graded symmetric in $\varepsilon$, outputs in $\bar\varepsilon$, 026 §1.2 (iii)); the $g$-slope uses the $+g$ self-gluing convention. **Genus scope (critic C3):** the $+g$ coefficient is independently anchored only at $g=1$ (the (1,1,1) relation); $g\ge2$ is the uniform ghost-additive self-gluing convention (consistent extension, not an independent computation). Downstream users (R21/R22/R25/R26) invoke only the $g=0$ slice. **Carve-outs:** $d=\tfrac12$ (026 §1.4) is not CFL's $d=n-3$ for any contact target, and CFL's integer-$d$ results (homotopy theory, Weyl-algebra correspondence) do not apply as stated; finite-dim algebraic layer (degree bookkeeping is exact and convention-level, no CFT analytics needed).

**B7 — the ALL-ORDERS fixed-point theorem (algebraic layer: finite-dimensional, generic symmetric tensors). Within the jointly Gaussian, shared-parent ansatz with auxiliaries $\Sigma_k\in\operatorname{Sym}^k$, a cubic action matching a given nonpolynomial action exists at every order as a formal series in $g$ — the status the target itself has — with the Veronese diagonals of the parents forced and everything else presentation freedom. Outside the ansatz: non-Gaussian partitions at $n\ge8$ are not covered, and other cubic actions exist (R13, R24). The all-orders formulation, the next step of the programme.** **(1) Jacobi form (strengthens R25's sequential Gaussianity):** the scheme-B action `eq:Sdrallorders` is **jointly quadratic in the whole auxiliary tower** $(\Sigma_2,\dots,\Sigma_K)$ — block-tridiagonal (Jacobi) Hessian with diagonal blocks $a_k\hat M_k$, $\hat M_k=\tilde M_k-\tfrac{g}{2k+1}T_{2k+1}(\cdot,\cdot,\Psi)$, off-diagonal blocks the source maps $L_k(\Psi)$, sole inhomogeneity $\tilde u_2=V_4(\cdot,\Psi^{\otimes2})$. **(2) The effective action is ONE Gaussian with an operator-continued-fraction kernel:** $S(\text{saddles})=\tfrac12V_2+\tfrac g{3!}V_3+\tfrac{g^2}{4!}\tilde u_2^\top\hat H_2^{-1}\tilde u_2$, $\hat H_k=\hat M_k-g^2\tfrac{(2k)!}{(2k+2)!}L_{k+1}^\top\hat H_{k+1}^{-1}L_{k+1}$ (nested Schur; block solve == continued fraction == direct action at machine precision, real and complex $g$). **(3) Telescoping contact lemma:** level-$k$ data first enter at $g^{2k-2}$; at $g^{2k-2},g^{2k-1}$ only through the Veronese diagonals, linearly, coefficients exactly $1/(2k)!$, $1/(2k+1)!$ (rung factors telescope $(1/4!)\prod(2j{-}2)!/(2j)!=1/(2k)!$; collapse $\tilde u_j(P_{j-1})=\tilde M_jP_j$ automatic for ANY common parent — block composites and full polarizations equally). **(4) THEOREM:** the diagonal recursion $T_n=-n!\,\mathrm{Pol}(A_{n-2})$, $A_{n-2}=[g^{n-2}]$(previous tower's mismatch vs the full target), is always solvable ($A$ homogeneous degree $n$ by scaling ⇒ polarization exists), unique in the diagonal (linear response $\mathrm{diag}/n!$), and triangular (lower orders untouched) ⇒ `eq:closuredef` holds as a formal power series in $g$, exactly rational with $O(g^{2K})$ remainder at each truncation $K$ (matched through $O(g^{2K-1})$; the first unmatched order is the absent level $K{+}1$'s first entry $g^{2K}$ — critic fix S1, was mis-stated $O(g^{2K+2})$). All geometric $V_n$ enter ONLY through the forced diagonals; everything off-diagonal is presentation freedom that feeds forward and is re-absorbed (machine: canonical-block vs fully-polarized towers both close at 1e-15, same $d_6$, different tensors, different forced $d_7$). **(5) Gauge at all orders:** exact completed square $S=S(\text{saddles})+\sum_k(-a_k)\hat H_k(Y_k,Y_k)$ off-shell; kernel rotations $\delta Y_k=-\tfrac12\hat H_k^{-1}(\delta\hat H_k)Y_k$ at EVERY level give $\delta S=\delta[S(\text{saddles})]$ exactly (8.9e-16; falsification legs match); homogeneous, no independent parameters — R18/R21/R22/R25 pattern now a theorem for the tower. **(6) $O(g_s^6)$+$O(g_s^7)$ instance (018 §6 spec) settled en passant:** the $k=4$ pair $\{(4,4),(4,3,1)\}$ closes with forced diagonals (residual log-slope 8.015); the source $\tilde u_4(\Sigma_3)$ is forced (zeroing it leaves exactly the $A_6$ deficit) and **uses $\Sigma_3$** ($\partial\Sigma_4^*/\partial\Sigma_3=\hat H_4^{-1}L_4\neq0$) — **Harold's tower expectation $\Sigma_k\leftrightarrow(\tilde V_{2k},\tilde V_{2k+1})$ holds at $k=4$ and, by the theorem, at every $k$.** **Carve-outs:** CFT analytics of the nested $\hat H_k$ chain (all-orders auxiliarity; extends A2(iii)/T4); **convergence in $g$ of the infinite continued fraction NOT addressed** (order-by-order/formal only; exact rational at fixed truncation; R19 (g) perturbative-branch caveats; the target nonpolynomial action is itself a formal series in $g_s$, so order-by-order matching is the whole claim a perturbative reformulation makes — not registered as a hypothesis, and a real question only for non-perturbative use of the tower); non-Gaussian partition realisations at $n\ge8$ ((3,3,2)-type) conjectured free by the R25 pattern, NOT covered; canonical strand-local (running-composite) representatives of $T_8,T_9$ not constructed explicitly (script uses polarized parents); quantum/genus excluded (Harold). **Annotation 2026-09-14 (026 §7, B′c/026C):** presentation (b)'s products are the re-weighted $V_4$-slice of the brace *products* of $\hat{\mathfrak p}$ (026 (5.25), read on the doubled symbol algebra as bookkeeping: 026 §5′.5–§5′.7, no single graded-commutative polynomial operator host in the strand variables with even $\hbar$ and slot-uniform parity-homogeneous raising for the $\ell\ge2$ components; untested, not excluded: separate input/output generators, non-polynomial product, $\star$-calculus, odd parameter) and are not the Koszul brace structure of any operator (026 (5′.10), script W4, proven); what Theorem A′ (R31) guarantees for (b) is the $\min(k,\ell)=1$ relations, hence the composite gauge law; the $(2,2,0)$ relation plays no role in the HS theory or in the all-orders tower (026 §5.4, §5′.4). **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** The headline's "unique" was given the theorem's class (jointly Gaussian shared-parent ansatz, finite-dimensional generic tensors; 019 l.185–193), since R13 and R24 are cubic actions outside it, and the convergence hedge was marked as costing nothing against a target that is itself formal; 019 §6 l.272–274 ("the scheme question is closed at all orders", where "scheme C" is 018's scheme, not Route C) carries the same defect and should be read with this row.

**RS — the Riemann-surface / coloured-operad dictionary for the colour-2 (auxiliary-field) sector, superseding the obsolete bounded-neck picture of the 2026-06-28 Phase-6 note.** Answers the 2026-07-09 questions: what $m_2^{-1}$ means geometrically (Laplace/Schwinger form included), how a colour-2 edge glues two puncture-pairs without raising the *physical* genus, and how the OSFT/Witten analytic-solution technology maps across. **(1) Two kernels, sharply distinguished:** the ordinary worldsheet sewing kernel $\mathsf P=\int ds\,d\theta\,b_0^+b_0^-e^{-sL_0^++i\theta L_0^-}$ (a continuous 2-real-parameter surface family, 2 $b$-insertions) vs the colour-2 contraction $\mathsf S=(12/g_s^2)m_2^{-1}G_2^{-1}$ (no $q$, no $s$-integral, no $b$, no $L_0$ — the inverse of an *already-integrated* vertex). **(2) Frozen-neck sewing and chains (Definitions, this session):** a colour-2 edge = two simultaneous frozen-neck ($q{=}1$, unintegrated) contractions; $\chi$-additivity gives resolved genus $g=1$ for one edge, $g=k-1$ for a length-$k$ chain of vertex regions — but three independent physical gradings (coupling $g_s^{2}$ uniform, moduli/$b$-count $2k$ not $2(3k-2)$, $N_{\rm gh}=3\ell-2k+g$ via R23) all follow the *coloured* graph, not the resolved surface: the resolved genus is 't Hooft double-line bookkeeping that costs nothing, because the necks are never integrated. $\{m_2^n\}$ is a **discrete** semigroup of frozen-neck chains, contrasting the **continuous** semigroup $\{e^{-sL_0^+}\}$ — the precise content of "$m_2$ is a contact term, not a worldsheet element". **(3) $m_2^{-1}$, three representations:** (i) `excl. within local geometric kernels of bounded topology with standard node/ghost insertions (any genus, any continuous moduli), modulo the factorization input`: the bare Gram inverse is not such a kernel (upgraded to a THEOREM on that class — **R28**, RS5 closed 2026-07-14); outside the class: the chain sum (ii), whose ghost deficit is carried by its $(n{+}1)$ non-geometric $D^{-1}$ factors (020 l.231–233), not by the infinitude of the sum; (ii) **an affirmative surface definition of $m_2^{-1}$** — the Neumann chain series $m_2^{-1}=\sum_n(D^{-1}N)^nD^{-1}G_2$ *is* the honest surface representation, a convergent weighted sum over the discrete family of frozen-neck chains, conditional only on $\rho(D^{-1}N)<1$ on a stated momentum domain — so the definition is local in momentum space, away from the zeros of the truncated determinant, not a global representation (see PH21); (iii) the Laplace/Schwinger form $m_2^{-1}=\int dt\,e^{-tm_2}$ is spectral (conditional) / $\int dt\,m_2e^{-tm_2^2}$ (for real spectrum), $t$ Laplace-dual to $\operatorname{spec}(m_2)$, and does *not* commute with the chain expansion. **(4) The explicit $m_2^{-1}$-cancellation mechanism (this session, answers "how does $1/m_2$ cancel the moduli of the SECOND $V_4$"):** writing $P=\operatorname{Sym}(\Psi_1\Psi_2)$, $Q=\operatorname{Sym}(\Psi_3\Psi_4)$, each vertex's moduli integral, once performed, equals $G_2$ applied to $m_2$ of the bare external pair ($v_I=G_2(e_I,m_2P)$); the propagator sandwich collapses via the *algebraic* step $m_2^{-1}m_2=\mathrm{id}$ — not a geometric one — cancelling one whole vertex's $m_2$-factor and leaving $G_2(m_2P,Q)=V_4(P,Q)$, a single freshly-saturated $\mathcal V_{0,4}$ integral, not a leftover half of the two original ones; this is why $\dim_\C=1$ survives out of the naive $1+1=2$. **(5) The 3-channel covering is combinatorial, not geometric:** the three $\Sigma$-exchange matchings each reproduce the *whole* of $\mathcal V_{0,4}$ (from the vertex-region integrals inside each $V_4(\Sigma,\Psi\Psi)$), with the $3\times\tfrac13$ pure symmetry-factor bookkeeping (R14 prefactor arithmetic, hand-confirmed by the critic); NOT a tiling like the genuinely disjoint $s,t,u$ propagator regions, and NOT to be conflated with R2's unrelated $\mathcal V_3'$ twist-gluing construction. **(6) Coloured operad, made exact:** objects $C_k=\Sym^k\Hilb_0$, colour-$k$ edges = $G_k^{-1}$ contractions ($k$-strand cables); the resolution functor $\rho:C_2\hookrightarrow C_1^{\otimes2}$ is a vector-space map but *not* an operad map — boxed genus formula $g_{\rm res}=L_c+\sum_e(k_e-1)$ (connected coloured graph, genus-0 vertices), 3-line $\chi$-additivity proof, independently hand-checked by the critic on a double-bond example ($g=3$) beyond the note. **(7) OSFT/Witten dictionary (interpretive analogy, not a theorem):** $L_0^+\leftrightarrow K$ (geometric generators); wedge algebra $e^{-tK}$ (continuous) $\leftrightarrow$ $m_2$-chain monoid (discrete) as the central structural analogy; formal inverse $1/(1+K)$ (legal sandwiched) $\leftrightarrow$ bare $m_2^{-1}$ (legal sandwiched, R14); spectral-gap condition enabling Schnabl/Erler control $\leftrightarrow$ A2(iii) (open: positivity of $\operatorname{spec}(m_2)$). **(8) $\Sigma_k$-tower extension:** verbatim generalisation with colour set $\{C_k\}$; dressed vertices $\tilde V_{2k}$ = "amplitude minus colour-reducible exchanges" at the frozen-neck-kernel level (why R19/R25's subtraction carries no moduli bookkeeping); R26's continued fraction read as nested telescoping = the all-orders version of the channel identity. **Do-not-reintroduce traps (T1–T6, binding):** T1 the bounded-length plumbing-neck picture (Phase-6 note, WRONG — transposed R3's open-stub mechanism, which belongs to a different construction); T2 reading $m_2^{-1}$'s Schwinger $t$ as a worldsheet length (within R28's class: connected surfaces of bounded type with standard sewing insertions); T3 resolving colour-2 edges into moduli-carrying necks (within R28's class: connected surfaces of bounded type with standard sewing insertions); T4 expecting geometric tiling from the 3 Σ-channels; T5 conflating R2 with Σ-exchange; T6 expecting a pointwise map realising the Gram identity. **RS4 CLOSED (2026-07-09, same session): (1.a) VERIFIED WITH CORRECTION** against the primary closed-string sewing conventions (Erbin's book, live draft `Reviews/String theory/document/chapters/`, esp. `bsft_bv.tex`). $\omega_1^{-1}=b_0^-\delta_{L_0^-,0}$ — a colour-2 contraction retains the level-matching $b_0^-$ per strand; only the length-modulus factor $b_0^+/L_0^+$ is genuinely absent (propagator fixture $\mathsf P=(b_0^+/L_0^+)\,\omega_1^{-1}$ itself confirmed exact, prefactor included). Derived two independent ways (operator factorisation of $K\Delta=1$; explicit ghost zero-mode matrices), 27/27-PASS companion script; $\lvert q\rvert {=}1$ stubbed-coordinate convergence consistent-with the book's plumbing range via the stub's own length suppression. Wording-level patches (non-structural) applied to both 020 and the tex note; both recompile clean. **Carve-outs:** RS5 **CLOSED 2026-07-14** (promoted to **R28**, theorem modulo the standard factorization input); numerical spectrum of $m_2$ (A2(iii)) is the one remaining genuinely open geometric item — **RS6, deferred to a companion project**. **Annotation 2026-09-14 (031 §7, R33):** the resolved genus $g_{\rm res}=1$ of a colour-2 edge is the genus of a double cover; the physical surface is the quotient, a sphere of the *doubled* ($\mathbb Z_2$-orbifold) worldsheet theory with twist fields at the external punctures, and the coloured genus $0$ is the quotient's genus — 'bookkeeping, not geometry' made precise. The pair-state sum on a colour-2 edge is the doubled theory's untwisted-sector neck sum, which carries the cover's loop momentum. **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** item (3)(i)'s shorthand "not a surface or local moduli integral" was given the class it is proven on (020 l.223–231: bounded-topology kernels with standard node and ghost insertions, any genus, any continuous moduli), and representation (ii) was made visible as an affirmative surface definition of $m_2^{-1}$ rather than a footnote to a negative; its convergence condition is registered as PH21.

**RS5 — excl. within connected surfaces of bounded type carrying standard sewing ghost insertions: $m_2^{-1}$ admits no local geometric kernel (THEOREM, modulo the standard CFT factorization/sewing-completeness input). Open at the actual $V_4$: explicit-ghost dressings and mixed tube/slice kernels (R28$'$ below).** Ghost-grading obstruction (Angle 1, the proof): any local geometric kernel — any number of vertices of any valence, any genus, $\nu_f\ge0$ frozen (colour-2-type) nodes each carrying its standard sewing bivector $\omega_1^{-1}=b_0^-\delta_{L_0^-,0}$ (RS4/021), any smoothed handles $b_0^+b_0^-\delta/L_0^+$, any continuous moduli with $b$-ghost measure — has $N_{\rm gh}=2+\nu_f\ge2$ (leg/insertion telescoping, 022 eq. 2.2; smoothed handles and moduli exactly ghost-neutral, frozen nodes raise), while $N_{\rm gh}(m_2^{-1})=-2$ unconditionally (R23's $N_{\rm gh}(m_2)=+2$ + $m_2m_2^{-1}=\mathrm{id}$, 022 eq. 2.1); no stratum reaches $-2$, so no real measure over strata can — the compensating-ghost / "doubly-frozen"-node loophole is closed (all standard node insertions raise or preserve $N_{\rm gh}$). Independently confirmed by analyticity (Angle 2: a bounded-topology kernel is polynomial in $V_4$'s matrix elements, $m_2^{-1}=V_4^{-1}G_2$ is rational with a pole at $\det V_4=0$; also disarms unbounded node accumulation = the discrete Neumann tower in disguise) and by toy counterexample search (Angle 3: none found; Cayley–Hamilton floor on fixed-degree fits). Neumann series (R27 item 3.ii) reconciled term-by-term: the $n$-th term's geometric chain supplies $N_{\rm gh}=+2n$ and the $(n{+}1)$ non-geometric $D^{-1}$ factors supply $-2(n{+}1)$ — representing $m_2^{-1}$ *requires* at least one algebraic inverse within this class; "pure geometry of any topology cannot do it" is wider than the theorem — a dressing fine-tuned at the actual $V_4$ is not excluded (030 §3.3 l.688–691). The single modulo: factorization/sewing completeness (a degenerating node's only ghost insertions are $\omega_1^{-1}$ and its length dressing — standard, pinned for the closed string in RS4/021). Reuses R14, R15, R23, RS4. **Annotation 2026-09-08 (R28′, derivation 030 §5.6, Task 12 second look):** over the enlarged class of 030 §2 (disconnected products, half-frozen and diagonal tubes, non-sewing $b$ insertions, unsoaked antighosts, any $V_4$-independent moduli bounds, finite sums), no construction *uniform in the vertex data* (one that would also invert $tV_4$) equals $m_2^{-1}$ — the uniformity has a physical motivation (the family is the $g_s^2$ normalisation and continuous deformations of the vertex region, the local coordinates and the external momenta, 030 §3.3 l.671–674), it is not a class chosen for provability, though 030 l.1658 grants that $tV_4$ with fixed $V_3$ is not itself a consistent vertex — scaling, 030 §5.6 (script part 4, Vandermonde floor with break-on-purpose); at the actual $V_4$, product kernels $\hat k\otimes\hat k$ (030 Prop. 3.1), tube-only kernels of any measure (030 Prop. 3.2, commutant/level-diagonality) and the Laplace reading (030 Lemma 2.2) are excluded individually; explicit-ghost dressings (030 eq. 3.8) and mixed tube/slice kernels only as uniform constructions; not covered: $V_4$-dependent bounds (= the algebraic inverse in disguise), infinite sums (Neumann), and a change of kinetic term (→ R30). Two prose corrections to 022 upheld by the critic (022 §2.4 "annihilates" remark false — the $s=0$ integrand is a nonzero bivector; 022 §2.3 second sentence over-broad relative to its Definition); R28 itself unaffected. **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** The title and the "any topology" sentence were given the 022 class, and the motivation of R28$'$'s uniformity assumption was recorded, because 030 §3.3 leaves a dressing fine-tuned at the actual $V_4$ open.

- **Status**:
  - diagram enumeration, prefactors, and the telescoping collapse in the finite-dimensional model – Proven
  - off shell, $A_4^{\rm HS}$ = standard closed-SFT off-shell amplitude for the same vertex family – Conditional(PH1, PH3) – Proven
  - on shell, Virasoro–Shapiro – Conditional(PH1, PH3, PH4) – Proven; PH1 and PH3, as stated, are assumed on a real neighbourhood of the on-shell locus, which is what the on-shell limit needs (050 Addendum (3))
  - at level 0 (tachyon-pair block): the on-shell limit and its interchange with the eigen-truncation, with PH1 and PH3 replaced by R53 and R55 – Conditional(PH4, PH25, PH29, PH30) – Proven (050 Addendum C5–C5.3)

**The 4-point amplitude chain (amplitude-check plan Phase A): the cubic HS action reproduces the standard closed-SFT amplitude, conditional on named hypotheses (H1, H2 off shell; H1, H2, H3 on shell; H1 = PH1, H2 = PH3, H3 = PH4) — the paper's future §7.** Assembles the HS Feynman rules to 4-point in one place (extends R14 with the $V_3$ sector, amplitude- not action-normalized prefactors, and a mechanical Gaussian-moment cross-check of the diagram sum — genuinely independent of the hand enumeration): 3 cubic-exchange channels ($s,t,u$, identical to standard SFT diagram-by-diagram) + 1 Σ-exchange channel, which collapses via the telescoping identity to the standard quartic contact term with coefficient exactly 1 ($3\times\tfrac16\times12\times\tfrac16=1$). New enumeration lemma: at $O(g_s^2)$ with 4 external Ψ, counting ($g_s$-order + leg + Euler) forces tree topology and excludes tadpoles/loops/disconnected pieces over all topologies, not merely by inspection. Two CFT-level hypotheses are named exactly at their point of use and nowhere smuggled: **H1** — $m_2$ descends to an injective map on $\operatorname{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0/\ker V_4$ that is invertible there at each truncation level, so $m_2^{-1}$ is defined on the truncated quotient (used at the telescoping collapse, where existence is trivial, uniqueness is modulo the kernel, and the amplitude sees the solution only through $V_4(\cdot,X)$), **level uniformly**: the kernels of the level-truncated forms stabilise, so the quotient and the level-by-level propagator have a limit; and at the external momenta used, tachyon shell included, the truncated determinant on the quotient has no zero persisting with $L$ — falsifiers: a non-stabilising kernel, or a zero of $\det V_4^{(L)}(P^2)$ on the shell persisting with $L$ (PH18). *No auxiliary inner product and no bounded inverse are assumed* (restated 2026-09-22; the singular-value drift is a conditioning diagnostic only). **H2** — completeness of the $\operatorname{Sym}^2$ intermediate two-string state sum over the two strands' oscillator levels **and** the pair's relative momentum (R33/031 §4.3) (used at the resolution-of-identity step), in the form that permits the sum to be interchanged with $m_2^{-1}$ at the collapse: the scalar partial sums $s_N(P,Q)=\sum_{I\le N}V_4(P,m_2^{-1}\lambda_I)\,\langle\lambda_I,m_2Q\rangle$ converge to $V_4(P,Q)$ for the external data used — convergence of numbers, no norm on the string field space (the only analytic input of the telescoping step; moved here from H1 on 2026-09-22; scalar form 2026-09-22). Both hold automatically in the finite-dimensional model (levels only, relative momentum suppressed), where the collapse is exact matrix algebra; a level truncation of the actual theory does not truncate $\int d^Dk_a$, so whether a comparison at a fixed level truncation carries information is **open** — the one thing it could test is the convergence of the relative-momentum integral on $m_2P$ at fixed level. Both are genuine spectral hypotheses in the untruncated theory — the same two entries the paper's limitations table carries (H1↔A2(iii)/R27 kernel-quotient; H2↔the state-sum-completeness input). Consequences distinguished: **off-shell**, $A_4^{\rm HS}$ = standard closed-SFT off-shell amplitude for the same vertex family (stronger, scheme-relevant); **on-shell**, since the vertex family's Feynman regions cover $\mathcal M_{0,4}$ exactly once (**H3**; cited, not re-derived), $A_4^{\rm SFT}$ on tachyons = Virasoro–Shapiro (textbook, cited — Erbin 2301.01686 for covering/conventions, Polchinski I §6.6 for the VS formula by name, absent from Erbin's draft). Companion script demonstrates the "tautology trap" flagged in the amplitude plan (truncated telescoping PASSES identically on 5 deliberately corrupted vertices — vacuous at any finite level) and its one resolution: the Neumann/frozen-neck-chain representation of $m_2^{-1}$ (R27 item 3.ii) converges to the contact term iff $\rho(D^{-1}N)<1$ (model A, $\rho=0.74$: converges, rel. err. $1.7\times10^{-4}$ at $K=12$) and diverges otherwise (model B, $\rho=2.28$: error grows $\times215$) while the algebraic identity still telescopes exactly regardless — the only place the cubic reformulation's amplitude can differ from standard SFT is the representation theory of $m_2^{-1}$, exactly per the plan's §0 analysis. **Naming note:** this row's H1/H2 are the amplitude hypotheses, unrelated to the 2026-07-01 Annotation's "H2" (graded $S_4$ symmetry of $V_4$, already closed for every legitimate vertex family) — that claim is called "$S_4$-symmetry" throughout `agent/derivations/023-*`, never "H2". In record 023, "H2" is the completeness of the pair-state sum; the older "H2" of rows R7–R13 is the graded $S_4$ symmetry of $V_4$, and those rows' prose still uses "H2" in that older sense. Carve-outs: CFT-level truth of H1/H2 = Phase B (rides on the companion computation); region-covering property inherited from the vertex construction (R14(b)); off-shell claim states equality of amplitudes for the *same* vertex family, not a comparison across families. **Re-scoped 2026-09-21 (rigor audit):** the H1/H2 statements and the finite-truncation clause are brought in line with the revised record 023 (second fix pass; critic iter-2 major 3) — "automatic at finite truncation" holds in the finite-dimensional model only, H2 covers the relative momentum as well as the levels, and the on-shell statement is conditional on H1, H2, H3.

**Annotation (2026-09-26).** The on-shell-limit clause (listed as a gap in the rigor audit of 2026-09-21; distinct from this row's H3, which is the covering PH4) is supported at level 0 on the tachyon-pair block by 050 Addendum (A.1)–(A.7), from R51, R53, R55 (node check 2026-09-26: C5–C5.3 hold). In the resolved chain form $V_4\,m_2^{-1}V_4$, whose $\mu_I^{-1}$ cancels term by term at every real $s$, the $\Sigma$-exchange equals $C\,F(s,t,u)$ at every real external pair (Mercer, R55), off shell included, so it is the restriction of an entire function and its on-shell limit is its on-shell value. Eigen-truncations with threshold off the spectrum at $s_*$ are real-analytic near $s_*$ and converge uniformly on compacts of the Euclidean on-shell region $\alpha's<16$ (Dini, from R53's positivity), so truncating and going on shell commute, jointly. The propagator $m_2^{-1}$ alone has no pointwise value at the external data and does not enter; its complex-$s$ zeros are PH18's question. Levels $>0$ open: the analogue needs PH1 and PH3 at every point of a real neighbourhood of the on-shell locus (uniformity only for the truncation interchange); the level-0 positivity mechanism does not carry over ($G_2$ indefinite). Level-0 statement Conditional(PH25, PH29, PH30); recorded as the fourth Status line (which adds PH4 for the on-shell endpoint); the tag is unchanged.

**Annotation (2026-09-30).** In the SL(2,C) declared section, PH4 is supported numerically in the companion project (062 C1.5), and the on-shell SFT amplitude is proportional to the Virasoro–Shapiro integral, $C_4I_{\rm VS}$ (for general kinematics conditionally on PH4), the constant fixed by the cubic vertex and pairing through factorisation. The amplitude check itself belongs to the sister paper (Harold, 2026-09-26). The on-shell status of this row is unchanged: PH4 remains a hypothesis for general kinematics.

**Geometry of the auxiliary channel after a change of kinetic term (Task 12, derivation 030 §3.7, §4, §5; the Erbin–Fırat mechanism transplanted to the closed string).** Convention set and a 17-row $N_{\rm gh}$ table for every elementary kernel (030 §1.3; rule (1.5) ≡ 022 eq. 2.2; frozen limit → R27's node; open-string limit → Erbin–Fırat's counts). Counting lemma (030 Lemma 3.3): the factorisation $V_4=J\circ K^{-1}\circ J$ needs $2m_J+n_\mu(K)=2$ and $2n_b^J+n_b(K)=8$, so only a *rigid* $J=\mathcal W_3=\Omega_{V_4}(\mu_0)$ with either the bounded product tube on $\mathrm{Sym}^2\mathcal H_0$ or the diagonal tube $b_1b_2b_1^-b_2^-(1-e^{-\lambda L_{\rm tot}})/L_{\rm tot}$ on the pair-level-matched space $\mathcal S_{\rm pair}$ ($\gamma=-2$, 2 moduli) survives; the single-antighost fixture of the 2024 notes fails by parity, the fixture with both antighosts is exactly this (β2). **(β) double strand:** passes ghost, moduli, on-shell and Sonoda–Zwiebach counts, but integrating $\Sigma$ out gives a 2-parameter family of 4-punctured *tori* with the $\mathcal M_{1,4}$ antighost measure (the "missing six units" = six unintegrated antighosts), not $\mathcal V_{0,4}$ (genus, proven via 008 (B.2)); equality with $V_4$ is exactly the branched-double-cover question Q13, answered no within the classes checked (031, R33; see below) (030 §6.1; holomorphic involution swapping the necks with the four punctures as fixed points; Riemann–Hurwitz $b=4$; Lunin–Mathur covering-space dictionary), for which the loop-momentum content of the composite (the pair-state sum is $\int d^Dk_a$; 030 §3.7.3) gives an independent and cheaper route to a 'no'. **(α-rev) single strand** in $\mathcal H_0$ (or $\mathcal H$) with $\mathcal K=Q_BL_0^+/(e^{\sigma L_0^+}-1)$ (reversed range $\lvert q\rvert >1$) and rigid $\mathcal V_3'$: all counts pass with sphere topology, subject to a coordinate-extension precondition on $\mathcal V_3'$'s local discs (SZ-type discs are tangent at $\lvert q\rvert =1$; 030 §5.4); whether the neck family covers $\mathcal M'_\times$ is the computation (6.1)/(6.2) (030 §6.2 → Task 14), carried out in R37: covered once with an $S_3$ seam, never with constant $\sigma$ (numerical), (6.2) on shell Conditional(PH10) – Argued. **(α-neck)**, the faithful Erbin–Fırat transplant (030 §3.5), is consistent but leaves $\mathcal V_{0,4}$'s interior as an elementary quartic vertex and does not make the theory cubic. **(γ)** unchanged: no local geometric kernel for $1/m_2$ within R28's class (connected bounded-type surfaces, standard sewing ghost insertions) nor as a construction uniform in the vertex data (R28′); kernels tuned to the actual $V_4$ are untested (below); $V_4$ reproduced (R29). Toy (script part 3): every fixture the toy can build — disconnected $\mathcal W_3$, real-positive $g$, and a rank-limited $P$ that is not itself a geometric object — fails at its analytic floor (Eckart–Young / signature bounds); the toy is blind to a connected rigid $\mathcal W_3$ (Takagi). Q13 **closed 2026-09-14 (no within the classes checked; 031, R33)**. Not settled — (β): the correlator-level inequality for any $\mu_0$ is argued from the relative-momentum sum, not evaluated (PH9), and in the Möbius-disc model the quotient collar is numerically not $\mathcal V_{0,4}$ at sampled $(\beta,r)$; the ghost anomaly is consistent with the covering picture and is not the obstruction; after R33, (α-rev)/Task 14 is the most developed option — (β) is argued, not evaluated, to fail at correlator level (PH9), and kernels tuned to the actual $V_4$ (R28$'$; 030 l.1683–1686 lists three such cases) are untested. Still not settled: (6.1)/(6.2) beyond R37's numerical covering on one grid and its argued (6.2); BRST compatibility of the (β2)/(α-rev) kinetic operators on $\mathcal S_{\rm pair}$; the sister project's Gram-signature "sign fact" is uncited/unverified. **Annotation 2026-09-14 (032; R37):** (α-rev): covering exists with a $\theta$-dependent seam (032 §3), never with constant $\sigma$; on-shell identity holds (032 §4); seam coordinates continuous at first order, not at second (032 §5); off-shell gauge invariance of the strictly cubic version expected to fail on non-primary external states, not evaluated; the collar quartic term (5.2) is a sufficient cure. Does *not* by itself retire (α-rev) in favour of (γ).

### R31 — Theorem A′: non-augmented raised family satisfies genus-zero IBL relations iff cyclic $L_\infty$ [Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/026-linfty-to-ibl-repackaging.md` §3.4 (Theorem A′, (3.27)–(3.31)); §3.1–§3.2 (augmented relations, $B=2P+M$); §9 Prop. 9.1/Cor. 9.2 (not implied at any $k,\ell\ge2$ for the given products on the full graded space: one cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra violates all; proven and exact-checked; node check 2026-09-30)
- **Scope**: genus zero only (026 §3.6); finite-dimensional graded space with odd symplectic $\Omega_1=(-1)^{\epsilon(A)}\omega_1$ (C1; the plain $\omega_1$ is graded symmetric), the string field on the formal $\Delta$-contraction footing of R1–R15 (026 §3.4 commentary); degree bookkeeping needs $d=\tfrac12$, outside CFL's integer-$d$ hypotheses; the failure of the augmented relations is a not-implied statement within cyclic $L_\infty$ algebras, $P_{k,\ell}$ not evaluated on the closed-SFT algebra (026 §9.4)
