- **Origin**: a general instruction to proceed with all open tasks (2026-10-04); the recommendation on the model's vertex parameter is the record's; the theory's cubic vertex parameter is a one-line check, not a result
- **Used by**: PH31 (status decision), R80 (the three Conditional(PH31) clauses: model statements, their loss at $\alpha'_{\rm v}=0$ an artefact), R84 (scope: the rule is the $\mu=0$ case of the general law)

**Statement and the discussion paragraph.** The selection rule that refutes PH31 at $\alpha'_{\rm v}=0$ needs the local coordinates at the two punctures of a half to be the dilations about the pair (pole of each coordinate at the other puncture). The rigid halves at $\alpha'_{\rm v}=0$ have this for every $q$. The actual quartic vertex, being symmetric under relabelling, cannot have it throughout its region. It fails at least on an open set of moduli and, if the vertex's coordinates depend analytically on the modulus inside each of the twelve tiles of the vertex region – as the explicit section indicates –, at almost every point. On the boundary with the propagator region and over that region the coordinates are those of two glued copies of the theory's cubic vertex, which sits at $\alpha'_{\rm v}=\tfrac12$, so the rule fails there too. So for the real off-shell $p_{2,2}$ the rule fails on an open set of moduli (at almost every modulus under the same hypothesis) on inputs with ghost excitations (on inputs whose ghost part is $c_1\bar c_1$ it survives for any vertex). The refutation is a model artefact, and PH31 should be recorded as `refuted` for the model at $\alpha'_{\rm v}=0$ with the note that this value is not representative; for off-shell rank questions the recommendation is the model with $\alpha'_{\rm v}\ne0$ (constant $\mu=\tfrac1{2\beta}$, $\tfrac13$ at $\beta=\tfrac32$, at the theory's point standing in for the varying $\mu_i(q)\ne0$; on $|q|=1$ and over $\mathcal F_s$ the model at $\alpha'_{\rm v}=\tfrac12$, $\gamma'=\beta$ coincides with the actual vertex). What this does not settle is whether PH31's statement holds for $p_{2,2}$ itself — it is formulated for the model, and $p_{2,2}$'s circle states vary with $q$.

### R87 — The family raised with $\Omega^{-1}$ is the `eq:conv:sprod` family twisted by the input parity, $p^{\Omega}_{m,n}(B)=(-1)^{|B|}p_{m,n}(B)$; the IBL generating function holds for each family in its own form; the IBL theorem holds in the generating-function form under the paper's coefficient rule [mixed]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/088-ibl-generating-sign.md` C1–C4, the fix-pass section and *Transcription into the paper*; `codes/verify-ibl-generating-sign.py` (imports the engine of `codes/verify-paper-gap-7.py`); paper `paper/sections/07-ibl.tex` `eq:ibl:raised-family`, `eq:ibl:raised-vs-sprod`, `eq:ibl:generating`, the theorem after `eq:ibl:polarized-master`
- **Scope**: finite-dimensional graded space with the paper's pairings (appendix A: $n$-string pairing with one $c_0^-$ per strand and its Koszul sign; $\Omega(A,B)=(-1)^{|A|}\langle A,B\rangle$), graded-symmetric even vertices; the string field on the same formal footing as R31; genus zero; relations in the generating-function form (the operator-level signs of CFL Def. 2.3 not derived: which family CFL's conventions select at $\min(m,n)=1$ is open); C3 depends on the coefficient rule — under the paper's rule (coefficients moved to the left, passing only basis states; stated at 07-ibl after l. 153 since 2026-10-04) $R^{\Omega}=-E$; under a uniform Koszul evaluation the one-output relations of $p^{\Omega}$ differ from those of the $\ell_n$ by $2\ell_2(Q_Bx,y)$ at $(2,1)$ ($x$ even, $y$ odd; by hand, not machine-checked)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-04 – numerical check – `codes/verify-ibl-generating-sign.py` 20/20 PASS (producer), then 22/22 PASS after the fix pass with the new `C3.0`/`C3.0-break` (orchestrator run, `agent/scratch/orchestrator/verify-ibl-generating-sign-20261004T1637.log`), seed 20261004; exact Grassmann-polynomial model over $\mathbb Q$ with random graded-symmetric $\mathcal V_2,\mathcal V_3,\mathcal V_4$, both families, 25 bidegrees, a model satisfying the master equation ($\mathfrak{sl}_2$) and one violating it
  - 2026-10-04 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-088-20261004T155700.md` – REVISE (mechanical): C1 re-derived by hand at one and two outputs, C3/C3.1 by hand at bidegree $(0,2)$; 3 major on the replacement text (symbol clash with the paper's $\hat p_{m,n}$, the unrestricted $\Psi$, l. 205–206); fix pass `agent/reports/computator-088-fix-20261004T161300.md`
  - 2026-10-04 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-088-confirm-20261004T163300.md` – narrow confirmation: transcribe after #3 (a)–(c) (the coefficient rule stated before the theorem, the theorem and l. 170 qualified by the generating-function form) and #1; the $(2,1)$ rule-dependence computation re-done by hand; applied in the transcription, paper compiled (scratch copy, 0 errors)
- **Status**: C1 ($p^{\Omega}_{m,n}(B)=(-1)^{|B|}p_{m,n}(B)$, all $n$) – Proven (by hand, re-derived by two critics) and Verified (exact model); C2 (the generating function for each family in its own form) – Verified; C3 (the polarised master equation at $(m,n)$ equals minus the sum of the compositions of $p^{\Omega}$, configuration by configuration; with $\Delta$ on the contracted strand every relation vanishes identically) – Verified (25 bidegrees, generic data) and by hand at $(0,2)$; the rule dependence of C3 – Argued (by hand twice, not machine-checked)
- **Origin**: the record 086 critic's flag on the paper's l. 132 (2026-10-04)
- **Used by**: the paper's IBL section (the theorem and its definitions); R31 (the paper's statement of Theorem A′ is for $p^{\Omega}$)

The family raised by $\Omega^{-1}$ on every vertex slot, defined by $\mathcal V_{m+n}(A_1,\dots,A_n,B_1,\dots,B_m)=\Omega_n(A_1\otimes\cdots\otimes A_n,\,p^{\Omega}_{m,n}(B_1,\dots,B_m))$ with $\Omega_n(X,Y)=(-1)^{|X|}\langle X,Y\rangle_n$ (state parities, extended linearly), equals the family of `eq:conv:sprod` precomposed with $(-1)^{N_{\rm gh}}$ on the inputs: $p^{\Omega}_{m,n}(B)=(-1)^{|B_1|+\dots+|B_m|}p_{m,n}(B)$, at every number of outputs. The two families coincide on inputs of even total parity — the classical string field and the $\Sigma_k$ — and differ by a sign on odd inputs (a gauge parameter) already at one output; on fields of unrestricted ghost number they differ component by component. The paper's former statement "differs by an overall sign for one output, and by component-dependent signs from three outputs on" was wrong (with the opposite twist $\Omega'=-\Omega$ the factor would be $(-1)^n$). `eq:ibl:generating` holds verbatim for the `eq:conv:sprod` family (with $p_{1,1}=Q_B$) and for $p^{\Omega}$ read through $\Omega_n$, term by term equal to $\tfrac{g_s^{m+n-2}}{m!\,n!}\mathcal V_{m+n}(\Psi'^n,\Psi^m)$. Sorting the polarised master equation by degree gives, up to one overall sign common to all $(m,n)$, the genus-zero IBL relations of $p^{\Omega}$ in the generating-function form; read through the sprod family, the same relations join the $p_{m,n}$ by $\Omega^{-1}$; plain composition (raising the contracted strand with $\Delta$) gives relations that vanish identically, so the raising of the contracted strand is a relative, not an overall, sign. The theorem — $p^{\Omega}$ satisfies these relations at every $(m,n)$ iff the $\ell_n$ form a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra in the standard Koszul convention — holds as stated in the paper since 2026-10-04.

### R88 — One-leg universality selection rule: at zero momentum a genus-zero vertex vanishes when exactly one leg lies outside the matter identity module [Proven]

- **Source**: cited – Yang–Zwiebach, hep-th/0506077, §2.1 (eq. (2.3) and the paragraph after it), after Sen, hep-th/9911116; for the SL(2,C) family, `SL: R57`
- **Scope**: genus zero; zero momentum, on the $p=0$ Fock space (states polynomial in $x^\mu$, which enter the zero-momentum BRST cohomology, are not covered); any ghost part, Siegel gauge or not; $n=3,4$: the SL(2,C) cubic vertex and the quartic vertex in any admissible section (`SL: R57`); $n\ge5$: any vertex given as a moduli integral of a sphere correlator of conformally transformed states with $b$-insertions (the family-independent Ward argument of Yang–Zwiebach; no SL(2,C) $V_5$ exists, PH27). At $k\neq0$ the same couplings are $O(k^2)$; the rule does not license dropping outside-module exchanges in $k\to0$ limits taken through massless poles
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-06 – literature comparison – companion-project check (the identity module non-degenerate at $c=26$ on the indefinite Fock space by the Kac determinant, exact to level 8; explicit Ward induction; sharpness; machine checks) – cited, not re-derived here; the argument is the one PH28's motivation states (057 §4.1)

At zero momentum, a genus-zero string vertex $V_n$ vanishes when exactly one of its legs has its matter part outside the matter identity (universal) Virasoro module and all other legs have theirs inside it, whatever the ghost parts. The integrand vanishes at every point of the moduli integration, so the result holds for any local coordinates holomorphic in the puncture coordinate, with any dependence on the moduli. It covers $V_3(T,T,x)$, $V_4(T^3,x)$, $V_4(T^2D,x)$ for $x$ the matter trace $\eta_{\mu\nu}\alpha^\mu_{-1}\bar\alpha^\nu_{-1}\lvert0\rangle$, the traceless graviton, the $B$-field and every other level-(1,1) state with a matter oscillator. The rule is sharp: two outside legs couple ($V_3(T,x,x)\neq0$). The kinetic term obeys the same rule: $V_2(\mathcal U,\mathcal U^\perp)=\langle A\vert c_0^-Q_B\vert B\rangle=0$ for $A$ universal and $B$ outside, at every level and ghost number, Siegel gauge or not, since $Q_B$ contains the matter only through the matter Virasoro generators; at $k\neq0$ this mixing is linear in $k$. Discharges PH28.

### R89 — excl. within the level-(1,1) five-field truncation: no flat dilaton direction at $d^5$; the $d^5$ coefficient is the dropped $f_1$ exchange, and the cubic HS structure adds nothing at $d^5$ [Conditional(PH27, SL: PH5) – mixed]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/095-d5-five-field-truncation.md` C0–C5, Result and *Scope of the exclusion*; `codes/verify-d5-five-field.py`
- **Scope**: excl. within the level-(1,1) five-field truncation ($\Psi\in\mathrm{span}\{T,D\}$, $\Sigma\in\mathrm{Sym}^2\{T,D\}$, the quintic action $S_3$ of R61), zero momentum, tree level, formal Taylor coefficients, declared section, $\beta\in\{\tfrac32,2\}$; the class is that of record 059 (R64), the lowest level containing the dilaton. Outside it: the six-field truncation $\{T,D,f_1\}$ and every higher level truncation, flat through $d^5$ (cited: the sister's R63, R64, at $d^5$ under SL: PH5); other sections, where the value of $c_5$ changes and its non-vanishing is not established. Weakest relaxation that evades it: add the single field $f_1=c_{-1}\bar c_{-1}\lvert0\rangle$. PH27 includes here the normalisation factorisation $C_5^{\rm book}=(C_4^{\rm book})^2/C_3^{\rm book}$, so that $V_5$ converts from the sister's normalisation by $\lambda_5=\tfrac12$ (R43 covers $n\le4$); SL: PH5 is the sister's hypothesis that its five-point vertex extends the family consistently, cited, not registered here (`knowledge/decisions.md`, 2026-10-06). Sister inputs ($\{D^5\}^{\rm contact}$, $\{DDT\}$, $\{TD^3\}$, $\{DDf_1\}$, $\{f_1D^3\}$) cited from the companion project, not reproduced
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-06 – exact check – `codes/verify-d5-five-field.py` C1.1, C1.2, C2.1–C2.3 (exact elimination of $\Sigma$ and $t$ against the channel count of the same elimination, three seeded random rational data sets; breaks on the factor 4 or 6, the sign, the contact term doubled, and $g^3/4!$ in place of $g^3/5!$ in the action), seed 20261006
  - 2026-10-06 – numerical check – same script C3.1: $240c_5$ against $5\{DDf_1\}\{f_1D^3\}$ to $1.1\times10^{-14}$, $1.2\times10^{-14}$ of $\lvert\{D^5\}\rvert$ (the sister's residual, from its printed values); run 1: 55/55; after the fix pass 58 checks, 0 FAIL, of which 2 arithmetic only (`C4.1-arith-*`); orchestrator re-runs identical, exit 0 (`agent/scratch/orchestrator/095-rerun-20261006/`)
  - 2026-10-06 – critic review – `agent/reports/critic-095-20261006T185457.md` (Fable) – REVISE light: all nodes C0–C5 hold, §1–§3 algebra and numbers recomputed by hand; two majors (the factorisation rule missing from the hypotheses line and tag; the "normalisation test at $n=5$" restating the assumption) and six minors, mechanical → fix pass `agent/reports/computator-095-fix-20261006T190631.md` (sonnet), orchestrator grep-confirmation of every formulation (§4, C4, Result, proposed entry) and re-run; no second iteration (critic routing: none needed; the one new check, the action-level break, read and confirmed a genuine falsifier)
- **Status**: the closed form of $c_5$ and its factor $5$ and sign – Conditional(PH27) – Verified (exact, random data); no HS-specific term below degree 6 in $\Psi$, $V_3(T^2D)=0$ – Proven, Verified on random data; the $d^5$ coefficient is nonzero and its values – Conditional(PH27, SL: PH5) – Numerical; the normalisation agreement – a consistency check under PH27, not a test of $C_5$

Along the dilaton direction of the level-(1,1) five-field truncation of the quintic cubic HS action, the tree-level effective potential ($\mathcal V=-S$, book normalisation) contains $g^3c_5d^5$ with
$$c_5=-\frac{V_5(D^5)}{120}+\frac{V_3(TD^2)\,V_4(TD^3)}{12\,V_2(T,T)}=\frac{\{D^5\}^{\rm contact}+5\{DDT\}\{TD^3\}}{240},$$
the second form in the sister's brackets and normalisation. Only three structures can reach $d^5$: the contact term, the one-tachyon exchange, and a two-tachyon term through $V_3(T^2D)$, which vanishes by ghost number; the $t^2$ terms enter only as the propagator, and eliminating $\Sigma$ leaves nothing below degree 6 in $\Psi$ (the first HS-specific term is R61's $O(g^4\Psi^6)$ Schur term). With the sister's identity (its order-$d^5$ ghost-dilaton identity, closed under SL: PH5), $c_5=\tfrac1{48}\{DDf_1\}\{f_1D^3\}>0$ (values not reproduced here), declared section. The nonzero coefficient is the level-(2,2) exchange through $f_1$ that the truncation drops, as at $d^4$ (R64 (iv)); the truncated valley, lifted at $\delta^4$, is tilted at $\delta^5$, the two terms competing only at $\lvert\delta\rvert\sim1$, outside the regime of the expansion. The agreement of the factor $5$ and the signs with the sister's formula is a consistency check, under the factorisation rule, that R1's $\tfrac{2g^2}{4!}$ and R61's $\tfrac{g^3}{5!}$ agree with the Feynman rules and the sign conventions; it does not test $\lambda_5$ or $C_5$.

### R90 — The $n=3$ patch $\mathcal V'_4$: construction, two-patch map, 236-stratum census, necessary covering pieces [mixed]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/081-npatch-n3.md#C1` (C1, C1.1–C1.3, §1), `#C2` (C2, C2.1–C2.3, §2), `#C3` (C3, C3.1, §3); `codes/verify-npatch-n3.py`
- **Scope**: genus zero, tree level, at the level of surfaces (points of $\mathcal M_{0,N}$; no local coordinates of the ordinary legs, no off-shell content); statements for any centre $c(m)$ of the special coordinate, except the holomorphy of the two-patch map on a product tile and the complex-determinant form of local injectivity, which hold for a holomorphic centre only (the section's centre $-2c_3$ is real-analytic, not holomorphic; the real $6\times6$ Jacobian is the general test); numbers only with $\beta=\tfrac32$. Not claimed: that the two patches cover $\mathcal V_{0,6}$ once, nor any value for $\mathcal V'_4$ from the marches of the record's §4 (a run of the machinery on the falsified centroid surrogate for $c(m)$). Untested pieces of the decomposition: (i$'$) global injectivity, (ii b/c/d), (iii), (v) surjectivity; (iii) and (v) are blocked on a $\mathcal V_{0,5}$ with its section.
- **Origin**: C3.1 only: `agent/derivations/081-npatch-n3.md#C3.1`
- **Status**: C1, C1.1–C1.3, C2, C2.1–C2.3 – Proven (construction and algebra at the level of surfaces; (1.3$'$) agrees with the vertex section on all twelve tiles by record 090 C3, a numerical check); C3's census (236 strata) – Proven; C3's list of pieces – a list of necessary conditions on an unconstructed object, claimed as nothing more; C3.1 – Argued (degeneration argument; weakest step: the plumbing family as local coordinates at the divisor, a standard plumbing-fixture fact not re-proved)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-10 – exact check – `codes/verify-npatch-n3.py`, 15/15 PASS, seed 20261004 (fix pass 2 run, script as on disk): 5 of the 15 can fail on a wrong result (`C1.2-equivariance`, `C2.3-n2`, `C2.4-jacobian`, `C4-inversion-selfcheck`, `C4-march-rank`), the rest are transcription, identity or bookkeeping checks; the `C4-*` checks concern the surrogate run and support no statement of this entry; `C1.2-equivariance` tests the cocycle of the coded $a_g$ table, not that (1.3$'$) is the sister's transport
  - 2026-10-10 – critic iteration 2 (`agent/reports/critic-081-iter2-20261010T114942.md`, C1–C3.1 hold, re-derived by hand) and fix pass 2 (`agent/reports/computator-081-fix2-20261010T120207.md`); `codes/verify-npatch-n3.py` 15/15 PASS on the script as on disk; the open sub-tasks N2–N5 are later-project work (scope decision of 2026-10-10)

The candidate $3$-patch is $\mathcal V'_4(m)=(\{0,1,m,\infty\};\ w=\lambda(m)/(u-c(m)))$ for $m\in\mathcal V_{0,4}$: the symmetric quartic vertex's surface in the frame $z=0,1,m,\infty$ with the $\infty$ leg made the special leg, $w$ a Möbius coordinate with scale $\lambda$ and centre $c$. The scale is fixed, not chosen, by the requirement that the twist-only plumbing $|q|=1$ of two patches reproduce the propagator boundary of the $\mathcal V_4\mathcal V_4$ region: $|\lambda|$ is the $S_3$-equivariant transport of $\beta|m(m-1)|^{1/2}$ off the fundamental domain, $|\lambda(m)|=\beta|\tilde m(\tilde m-1)|^{1/2}/|a_g|$ with $\tilde m=g(m)\in\overline{\rm FD}$ and $a_g$ the affine coefficient of the relabelling $g$, continuous with a kink across three arcs (and their conjugates); the naive formula is not equivariant under $m\to1/m$ (factor $\vert m\vert^{-1/2}$), consistent with the sister project's statement that its section is the equivariant transport. The centre $c(m)$ is the section's centre, not a free constant; at $n=2$, $(\lambda,c)=(\beta,\tfrac12)$. Remaining freedom: the seam $\mathbb C^*$, the phase of $\lambda$, the choice of special leg.

The two-patch family glued by $w_Lw_R=q$ maps $(m_L,m_R,q)$ to the six points $\{0,1,m_L,\ c_L+\Lambda/(u_R-c_R):u_R\in\{0,1,m_R\}\}$, $\Lambda=\lambda_L\lambda_R/q$, three complex parameters $=\dim\mathcal M_{0,6}$; its three cross-ratios are Möbius and seam invariant; at $n=2$ it reduces to $Z_4^{(s)}=1/(1-\chi)$ (R2), and with one bare $\mathcal V_3'$ it is the $\mathcal V_4\mathcal V_3$ Feynman family of $\mathcal M_{0,5}$ for $|q|\le1$.

The census: the Feynman regions of $\mathcal M_{0,6}$ are labelled by the $236$ strata of $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,6}$ (trees with six labelled leaves, vertices of valence $\ge3$): $1$ open stratum, $15$ $\mathcal V_5\mathcal V_3$, and $220$ built from $\mathcal V_3,\mathcal V_4$: $10$ $\mathcal V_4\mathcal V_4$, $45$ $\mathcal V_3\mathcal V_4\mathcal V_3$, $105$ $\mathcal V_3^4$, $60$ $\mathcal V_4\mathcal V_3\mathcal V_3$ chains.

The covering condition of R82 C3.4 at $n=3$ has, as *necessary* pieces (not jointly sufficient): (i) local injectivity, $\det J_{\mathbb R}\ne0$ for the real $6\times6$ Jacobian; (i$'$) global injectivity on the vertex range, a global statement not obtained from the Jacobian (at $n=2$ the fold circle $|q|=4\beta^2$ is the fixed locus of $q\to16\beta^4/q$, where the Jacobian vanishes at two points only); (ii a–d) non-overlap with the $\mathcal V_4\mathcal V_4$ (ten channels, the own one included), $\mathcal V_3\mathcal V_4\mathcal V_3$, $\mathcal V_3^4$ and chain regions; (iii) the pieces blocked on $\mathcal V_5$; (iv) the cutoff $\sigma_V(m_L,m_R,\theta)$, finite on every ray; (v) surjectivity, image $\supseteq\mathcal V_{0,6}$. Argued (C3.1): as $|q|\to\infty$ every ray tends to the own $(3|3)$ divisor with component moduli $\mu_{L,R}=\chi(0,1,m_{L,R};c_{L,R})$, and ends in the own $\mathcal V_4\mathcal V_4$ region, a chain region or a $\mathcal V_3^4$ region according to whether two, one or none of the $\mu$'s lie in $\mathcal V_{0,4}$, so $\sigma_V<\infty$ on every ray.

### R91 — excl. within the exactly-rigid fixed-halves model: the rigid gluing is not seam-consistent with the cubic exchanges of the theory's vertex $\alpha'_{\rm v}=\tfrac12$, $\gamma'=\beta$ (first order, exact at $\theta=\pi$: $\ln\frac{4\beta^2-1}{4\beta}$; fold at second order), so $p^{\rm circ}_{2,2}$ is neither the theory's quartic vertex nor any consistent one off shell [mixed]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/opencode-p22-inverse-gluing.md` §4 ((4.0), (4.1), (4.3), (4.4), (4.6), (4.6b)); `agent/scratch/opencode-p22/verify-c4-offshell-family.py` 9/9 PASS, seed 20261003
- **Scope**: the class is the exactly-rigid fixed-halves model of R80 (A5: rigid $\mathcal V_3'$ halves glued on the $s$-circle over $Q_V$, fixed positions, the halves' own discs at the four external punctures) — the model in use for $p_{2,2}$; seam consistency means agreement of the germs modulo phase at every point of $\partial Q_V$, necessary for the geometric master equation off shell with the standard $b$-insertions and $\mathcal H_0$ externals. Outside the class: the true $p_{2,2}$ (the SL(2,C)-type section, germs moving with $q$, R86), about which nothing is claimed. Weakest relaxations that evade it: a collar term (sufficient under PH11, argued), a modified sewing map at the neck (open), $q$-dependent positions. Numbers at $\beta=\tfrac32$; the $\theta=\pi$ anchor for every $\beta>\beta_\circ=(1+\sqrt2)/2$; the first-order data independent of $(\alpha'_{\rm v},\gamma')$ at every $\beta$. At $\beta\ne\tfrac32$ the exclusion rests on the $\theta=\pi$ anchor alone (the other 11 seam points are computed at $\beta=\tfrac32$ only); $\beta\le\beta_\circ$ is not covered (the anchor vanishes at $\beta_\circ$, nothing else is computed there; for $\beta<\beta_\circ$ the $s$- and $u$-Feynman regions overlap at $\theta=\pi$, a separate inconsistency of the model). Hypotheses in force: none (A5 a model assumption)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-10 – exact/numerical check – `agent/scratch/opencode-p22/verify-c4-offshell-family.py`, 9/9 PASS, seed 20261003 (fix-pass run, 2026-10-10 by the computator after critic iteration 1; not re-run by the critic of iteration 2, which audited the script by reading; the three original checks at $\alpha'_{\rm v}=0$, $\gamma'=2$ and six at the theory's point, each with a break leg: $S_3$ symmetry of the half (exact), first-order $\Re m_i$ identical to the $\alpha'_{\rm v}=0$ run to $5\times10^{-16}$ at 12 seam points, the anchor $\tfrac43$ (exact), the anchor's closed form in $\beta$ at four rational $\beta$ and at $\beta_\circ$ (exact), second order at 12 seam points, the fold $|\Delta\kappa_i|=\tfrac16$)
  - 2026-10-10 – critics `agent/reports/critic-opencode-p22-20261010T111036.md` (iteration 1) and `agent/reports/critic-opencode-p22-iter2-20261010T120608.md` (iteration 2: the $\theta=\pi$ anchor, the pinch $\beta_\circ$ and the fold value re-derived by hand for general $\beta$); fix passes `agent/reports/computator-opencode-fix-20261010T114942.md`, `agent/reports/computator-opencode-fix2-20261010T121546.md`
- **Status**: the first-order failure at the $\theta=\pi$ seam point, $\Re m_i(\pi)=\ln\big((4\beta^2-1)/4\beta\big)\ne0$ at all four punctures for $\beta>\beta_\circ$ ($\ln\tfrac43$ at $\beta=\tfrac32$) – Proven (exact); the $(\alpha'_{\rm v},\gamma')$-independence of $Q_V$, $\sigma_V$, the fold arcs, $\hat f_m$, $h_*$ and of every first-order germ datum – Proven; the first-order failure at the other 11 sampled seam points ($|\Re m_i|\in[0.30,0.49]$) – Numerical ($\beta=\tfrac32$); the second-order failure at the 12 seam points at the theory's point ($\max_i|c_1\Delta\kappa_i|\in[0.28,0.33]$) – Numerical ($\beta=\tfrac32$; not needed for the exclusion); the fold clause, germ-consistent iff $\alpha'_{\rm v}=0$ with $|\Delta\kappa_i|=\alpha'_{\rm v}/2\beta$ at all four punctures ($\tfrac1{4\beta}$ at the theory's point, $|c_1\Delta\kappa_i|=1/\beta$) – Proven (Möbius algebra), checked at $\beta=\tfrac32$
- **Origin**: OpenCode study of the inverse of $p_{2,2}$ as a worldsheet gluing (complements R79/R80), Task 4; fix pass after `agent/reports/critic-opencode-p22-20261010T111036.md`

*In the exactly-rigid fixed-halves model of $p_{2,2}$, the gluing of two rigid $\mathcal V_3'$ halves is not seam-consistent with the cubic exchanges built from the same halves. The inner seam $|q|=1$ matches identically. On the outer seam $|q_s|=e^{\sigma_V(\theta)}$ (against the $t$/$u$ Feynman regions at unit modulus) the first-order conditions $|F_i^{(s)\prime}(0)|=|F_i^{(x)\prime}(0)|$ fail; they are independent of $(\alpha'_{\rm v},\gamma')$, and at $\theta=\pi$ the mismatch is $\ln\big((4\beta^2-1)/4\beta\big)$ at all four punctures, $\ln\tfrac43$ at $\beta=\tfrac32$, vanishing only at $\beta_\circ=(1+\sqrt2)/2$, where $Q_V$ pinches. The fold arcs are an internal seam where the two sheets of the plumbing covering meet; there the germs agree modulo phase iff $\alpha'_{\rm v}=0$, and at the theory's vertex $\alpha'_{\rm v}=\tfrac12$, $\gamma'=\beta$ they differ at second order by $|\Delta\kappa_i|=\tfrac1{4\beta}$ at all four punctures. Hence, at the theory's vertex, $p^{\rm circ}_{2,2}$ (equal to $p_{2,2}$ on shell) is not the theory's quartic vertex off shell.*

*Given a section $f_i(\cdot;Z)$ of a quartic vertex, read its germs at $Z(q)$ in the frame of two glued R2 halves, $F_i^{(u)}=N_q^{-1}\circ f_i$; split $F_i^{(u)}=h_i\circ g_i(q)$ with $h_i$ the fixed half coordinate; then $\mathcal V_4$ on its vertex region is (3.2): two circle states of the fixed half on rescaled/reparametrised inputs $U_{g_i(q)}A_i$, paired through $\mathcal S(q)$ with the Schiffer antighosts $-b_0^++\sum_iB_i$, $\mathrm ib_0^-+\sum_iB_i$, the $B_i=b^{(i)}[\partial g_i/g_i']$ acting on the inputs before $U_{g_i}$. Forced: the frame, the germs, the total antighost insertions. Chosen: the half coordinates (canonically the theory's vertex, for which the input operators are trivial on $\mathcal H_0$ at $|q|=1$; any other choice describes the same surface states with a $q$-independent factor moved into the plumbing operator), hence the input operators, and the placement of the inner contours (inputs, or neck level by level). For the SL(2,C) section $g_i=d_\kappa\circ m_{\kappa\mu}$ relative to the $\lambda=0$ half (relative to the canonical half $\lambda_0=\frac12$, $\mu\to\mu-\frac1{2\beta}$ in the $L_1$ exponent), one $(\lambda=\mu\gamma',\kappa)$ at all four legs, so the two halves at $q$ are the same member $\lambda(q)$ of the family with inputs rescaled by $|\kappa|^{L_0^+}$ on $\mathcal H_0$ (R92), and the dressings are $(\partial\ln|\kappa|)b_0^{+(i)}-\kappa\partial\mu\,b_1^{(i)}-\mathrm{c.c.}$ The dressing is never a coordinate change at the special puncture (an operator of another form is left open; a Möbius map fixing three points is trivial; in the Möbius case the two input fields are $\sigma$-related global quadratic fields and never coincide); it equals a neck insertion with creation modes of order $\le2L+2$ on level-$\le L$ inputs. On four matter primaries the whole off-shell content is $|\kappa(q)|^{2\sum h_i}$ times the fixed-halves integrand; on shell this is R80.*

### R94 — The fixed-level kinetic operator $p_{2,2}^{(L)}(P)$: entire Hilbert–Schmidt family, self-adjoint structure at real $P^2$, exact Schur reduction over the level-0 block [Conditional(PH25, PH29, PH30) – Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/104-kernel-analytic-structure.md#C1`, `#C2`, `#C3`
- **Scope**: every mass level $L$ per strand, every $p\in\mathbb C$ for C2 (entire), every real $P^2$ for the self-adjoint structure and the Schur reduction; vertex family with P1 (PH34 for the section); Gaussian rate $\beta>\sqrt2$ under PH29, PH30; the range condition $\mathcal D_L=\mathcal H_{>0}$ and the range clause for $p_{3,2}$ are open (C1.5, C3.2); the indefiniteness of the full symmetric form near $P^2=0$ at level $1$ is at the declared section (R68, with R68's scope)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-18 – toy-model checks – `codes/verify-kernel-analytic-structure.py` – 19/19 PASS after the fix pass (C1.4-structure, C1.6-range with the Mehler window, C2.4-HS, C3.1-schur, C3.3-variational, C3.3-variational-realp, C3.3-inertia and their break legs)

At fixed level $L$ and total momentum $P=p\,n$, $p_{2,2}^{(L)}(P)$ is the integral operator (1.2) on $\mathcal H^{(L)}=\{\sigma\in L^2(\mathbb R^{26};W_L):\tau\sigma(-q)=\sigma(q)\}$; its kernel is the basis-free space of null functions; $p\mapsto K^{(L)}_p$ is an entire Hilbert–Schmidt-operator-valued function whose kernel is entire in $(p,q,q')$ and bounded by $(1+|q|+|q'|)^{8L}e^{c(|q|+|q'|)}e^{-a_*(q^2+q'^2)}$, Hilbert–Schmidt if $a_*>0$ ($\beta>\sqrt2$; iff at $L=0$, R51), the level-0 condition unchanged by the level; $K_p^{\!\top}=K_{-p}=UK_pU$ with $U$ the reflection $n\to-n$, so $K_pU$ is real self-adjoint at real $p$ and $K_p$ self-adjoint at imaginary $p$; $\ker K^{(L)}_p\cong\ker(S_L|_{\mathcal D_L})$, $S_L=K_{LL}-K_{L0}K_{00}^{-1}K_{0L}$, $\mathcal D_L=\{\sigma_1:K_{0L}\sigma_1\in\mathrm{ran}\,K_{00}\}$, exactly; the level-0 positivity mechanism does not extend, and the full symmetric form at level $1$ ($K_0$ at $P=0$, $K_pU$ at real $p$, $K_p$ at imaginary $p$) is indefinite on a real neighbourhood of $P^2=0$, so that definiteness cannot give D2 at all real $P^2$ above level $0$.

### R95 — excl. within entire Hilbert–Schmidt families: discreteness of the singular set (D1) is not a consequence of analyticity and compactness; under PH35 the dichotomy "kernel everywhere or discrete" and D1 from one reference point [Conditional(PH35, PH25, PH29, PH30) – Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/104-kernel-analytic-structure.md#C4`
- **Scope**: the exclusion covers every family with C2's properties (the counterexample (4.1), with geometric weights, is in the class: entire in $(x,y)$ of order 2 with Gaussian decay); the conditional statement holds on the neighbourhood $\Omega_L$ of PH35, for each $L$; the reference point at $L\ge1$ is open (C4.6); $L=0$ is R53
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-18 – toy-model checks – `codes/verify-kernel-analytic-structure.py` – C4.1-counterexample (repaired family, falsifiable with BREAK-C4.1-shift), C4.2-relative-unbounded, C4.5-fredholm (matrix determinant lemma only) and break legs PASS, 19/19 after the fix pass

Entire Hilbert–Schmidt families with a singular set accumulating at a finite point exist (4.1): D1 needs an input beyond C2. The level-diagonal split (PH21) and the Schur complement do not provide a compact perturbation of the identity in the toy scaling (4.2) – there, polynomial-weighted Gaussian operators are relatively unbounded with respect to the unweighted one, $K_0^{-1}aK_0=ra$, $K_0^{-1}a^\dagger K_0=r^{-1}a^\dagger$. Under PH35 (Fredholm form of $\mathcal T_L=\mathbb K_0^{-1/2}K^{(L)}\mathbb K_0^{-1/2}$, $\mathbb K_0$ the level-0 block at a fixed reference momentum): for each $L$, either $\ker K^{(L)}_p\ne0$ for all $p\in\Omega_L$ or $Z_L\cap\Omega_L$ is discrete; one $p_0$ with $\ker K^{(L)}_{p_0}=0$ gives D1 at level $L$, with a locally finite set of exceptional real $P^2$.

### R96 — The stub change of the fixed-level kinetic operator: Gaussian congruence up to the domain, plus the shell of single-string exchanges where the mass sum acts [Conditional(PH25) – Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/106-kernel-heuristic-mass-sum.md#C0`
- **Scope**: every level $L$, every complex $p$; the one-sided stub family $[s_0,s_0+\varepsilon)$ obtained from the project's quartic vertex by adding the strip $\sum_{\rm ch}\mathcal V_3\Delta_{[s_0,s]}\mathcal V_3$ (a geometric BV solution for any base vertex; no statement about the project's section is used), with A4's convergence of the intermediate sum; the congruence is the rewriting of that family in rescaled local coordinates
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-18 – by reading – (0.1) from the $L_0^+$ eigenvalue (C9) summed over a pair, (0.4) two-line operator algebra in both directions; no machine check (no numerical claim)

$K^{(L),B}_P[s_0+\sigma]=\mathrm e^{-\alpha'\sigma p^2/4}\,\Lambda_{\sigma}\bigl(K^{(L)}_P[s_0]+\int_{s_0}^{s_0+\sigma}\mathcal E^{(L)}_P\bigr)\Lambda_{\sigma}$ with $\Lambda_{\sigma}=\mathrm e^{-\alpha'\sigma q^2/2}\mathrm e^{-\sigma(\hat l-2)}$ bounded, positive, injective, of unbounded inverse, and $\mathcal E^{(L)}_P[s]$ the sewn two-cubic-vertex surface summed over the three channels, a convergent sum over intermediate mass levels, finite-rank per level in the $s$-channel with exponents $h_n(P)=\tfrac{\alpha'}{2}P^2+2(n-1)$; $\ker(\Lambda K\Lambda)=\Lambda^{-1}(\ker K)\cong\ker K\cap\mathrm{ran}\,\Lambda$: the level factor moves no kernel; at fixed $K$ the Gaussian factor's kernel decreases with $\sigma$, null vectors with Gaussian decay at every rate kept, those of a fixed rate $\mathrm e^{-aq^2}$ lost once $\alpha'\sigma/2\ge a$ (Schwartz decay is not enough); the stub change as a whole also moves $K$ by the strip and can create or remove null vectors. The mass-sum suppression $\lambda^{-h}$ is, at fixed external level, the level factor and says nothing about the kernel through the congruence.

**The exchange at real momenta (record 105).** $\mathcal C$ is the limit of the truncations $\mathcal C^{(L)}=\langle u^{\le L},(K^{\le L}_P)^{-1}w^{\le L}\rangle$ of the level-mixing pair block of $V_4$ between the two $\mathcal V_5$ slices, on the Euclidean relative-momentum contour with real external momenta — not a sum of level-diagonal block inverses (035 §4.3 item 1) — defined off the real singular sets of the truncated blocks (none at level 0; discrete above, under Track D's conditional D1; whether empty is open) and, as argued, the reviews' prescription with an empty deformation step; on on-shell external tachyons every momentum-independent region integral of the standard measure is $\mathcal N\int e^{\langle\lambda,x\rangle}d\nu_R$ with $\nu_R\ge0$ of compact support (only the translation modes of the Schiffer vectors survive on a tachyon, on or off shell), so (a), (a′) and (b) imply: T1 constant ratio $\mathcal C/\mathcal V_6$ (a non-constant ratio refutes (b); a constant one is consistent with it; (b) on tachyons follows only from constancy on an open set); T2 $0\le\mathcal C/\mathcal V_6\le1$ under (a), $\le\tfrac56$ under (a′), the two distinct mathematical statements and exclusive unless $\mathcal C\equiv0$, T2 measuring $\alpha$ under (b); T3 log-convexity of $\lvert\mathcal C\rvert$ in the invariants (midpoint form, points and midpoints in the real domain), necessary for (a), (a′) and (b) with $\alpha\ne0$; T4 growth exponents bounded by the extrema of $\alpha'\ln\lvert z_{ij}\rvert$ on $\overline{\mathcal V_{0,6}}$; T3⁺ exponential convexity of $\mathcal C$ ((b): iff $\alpha\ge0$) and of the complement $W_6$ for (a′) or $\mathcal V_6-\mathcal C$ for (a) ((b): iff $\alpha\le\tfrac56$, resp. $\alpha\le1$), with (a′) $\iff$ the inverse transform of $\tfrac65\mathcal C/\mathcal N$ is the conjugation-symmetrised $\tfrac12(\mathbf 1_{R'}+\mathbf 1_{\bar R'})\mu_0$ pushed forward by the nine $\log\lvert$cross-ratio$\rvert$, injective up to $z\mapsto\bar z$ — a $\{0,\tfrac12,1\}$-valued density (likewise (a) with $\mathcal C$). The tests are necessary conditions: they can refute the target (a′), not prove it; the only sufficient direction is that exact inverse transform. A genuine pole of the continuation refutes (a)/(a′)/(b) as a corollary (identity theorem), valid wherever the fixed-contour form is meromorphic (H-C4) and not for the frozen-relative-momentum model; the tests do not need it, and the pole cancels in the amplitude.

### R100 — Calibration of T3/T3⁺/T4 on the fixed-halves slab model: positive against signed weight [mixed]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/105-exchange-real-momenta.md#C5`, eqs. (5.1)–(5.2)
- **Scope**: R80's model (A5, A5′, PH31; with PH31, R85's restriction to $\mathcal C_{\rm sector}$ at $\alpha'_{\rm v}=0$), sector $m=0$, constant cutoff $\tau$, slab widths $a,b$, on the convergence half-line $h>0$; no $\mathcal V_6$ in the model, so T1/T2 are not calibrated; nothing about the actual vertices
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-18 – exact/numerical check – `codes/verify-exchange-real-momenta.py`: `C5-single`, `C5-multiple` (disjoint case $b<\tau$ only; the overlap clause is derived, not checked), `C5-generic`, `C5-break` (fires), `C3.5-expconvex` PASS (re-run 2026-10-18 after the last fix pass, `agent/runs/computator-c-105-fix2-20261018T152542/verify-run.log`, identical); `C5-generic-wide`, `C5-single-ratio` informative; "T3⁺ fails for every generic pair" is proven (uniqueness of the representing measure), not machine-resolved at $(0.7,0.7,0.75)$ — hence `[mixed]`

**Calibration (record 105 §5).** $\hat\mu_{\mathcal C}=\hat f_a\hat f_b\,h/(e^{\tau h}-1)$; single slab ($a=\tau$) and compact support ($a=k\tau$) are positive measures — log- and exponentially convex, T4 saturated by the support — and are **not** separated from each other by the tests; the compact case is a $\{0,1\}$ density (a union of $k$ bounded plumbing regions) iff $b\le\tau$, an overlapping density otherwise; the generic case has $(\log\hat\mu_{\mathcal C})''(0^+)=(a^2+b^2-\tau^2)/12$, so T3 fails at small $h$ iff $a^2+b^2<\tau^2$ (074's widths: $-1/192$), and T3⁺ fails for every generic pair by uniqueness of the representing measure, with a numerical visibility set by the size of the signed weight (dipoles of width $\lvert a-\tau\rvert$ invisible to $4\times4$ matrices at $10^{-10}$ for $(0.7,0.7,0.75)$). The poles at $2\pi ik/\tau$ are a property of the closed form, not the reason.

*On the tachyon-pair block the whole off-shell content of $p_{2,2}$ is the weight $|\kappa(q)|^{\sum_ih^+_i}$ on the rigid gluing (R93). Writing $\sum_ih_i^+=\alpha'(\tfrac{P^2}2+\ell^2+\ell'^2)-8$ and using that the rigid four-tachyon integrand depends on the relative momenta only through $\ell\!\cdot\!\ell'$, a sewing form of the level-summed inverse, on a class large enough to represent the tachyon-block functionals, gives by the forward identity $p=(\pi_W\Phi)^\dagger M^{-1}(\pi_W\Phi)$ a factorised tachyon block whenever the kernel acts trivially on the matter sl(2) multiplicities. Equating would force $\Lambda\mapsto\int_{Q_V}|\kappa|^{\Lambda}\rho$ to be an exponential, i.e. $\ln|\kappa|$ constant on $Q_V$, while it varies from $0$ at the propagator seam to $\ln\tfrac34$ at the outer seam. This excludes the CKV pair, ghost dressings and Möbius-moving cut frames (for halves with Möbius cut coordinate), already at first order of the local-coordinate expansion. Non-Möbius moving cut frames break the factorisation and are not excluded: the chain-level route through a sewing with $q$-independent or Möbius cut frames stops here, while a sewing with non-Möbius cut frames — the form $p_{2,2}$ would have if R93 C3.3's level-uniform clause holds ($p_{2,2}$ itself has the leg-dressed form), which even then would say nothing about the inverse — is the open step before Gate G2; its first necessary test is at second order in the non-Möbius amplitude. The model's chain reading additionally needs 074 A5′ for $\mathcal V_5$, which fails on an open subset of each face of $\mathcal V_{0,5}$ bordering the $\mathcal V_3\!-\!P\!-\!\mathcal V_4$ Feynman regions, because there $\mathcal V_5$'s $\Sigma$-leg coordinates are $\mathcal V_4$'s, whose mapping radii vary over $\mathcal V_{0,4}$.*

### R102 — excl. within field-independent zero-mode D's: candidate C1 at quadratic order is, for D = c_0^- f(L_0^-) symmetrized, in every sector where f(m_1) + f(m_2) ≠ 0, the projector form on ker(b_0^-(1) + b_0^-(2)) (the c_0^- b_0^- words: sector types numerical, table rows DN, Dgen, Dgen0); the per-strand slice is never a gauge slice; no D of the class acts on H_0^∧2 [Proven]

- **Scope**: field-independent $D$ of ghost number $+1$ built from $c_0^-$, $b_0^-$ and functions of $L_0^-$ on two strands (row D1 of record 102), $B_2 = b_0^-$ symmetrized, $q$ any symmetric form with $q|_{\mathcal H_0^{\wedge 2}} = p$, source term extended by zero, quadratic order; structural statements for arbitrary strand spaces, dimensions in the strand model of record 108 (model M at $m = 0$).
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-18 `codes/verify-gauge-unfixing-c1.py` C2.1-raise, C2.3-count, C2.2-brute, C2.2-family, C3-table, C4-prediction, seeds 20261018 and 20261019, 10/10 PASS (computator, record 108).
  - 2026-10-22: Fable check of the nodes changed after the last review (agent/reports/critic-gu-check-108-20261022T193154.md): A8, C5 hold, the C3 clause narrowed to generic coupling blocks (c' > dim ker p in every row); fix pass agent/reports/computator-gu-108-fix3-20261022T194958.md; codes/verify-gauge-unfixing-c1.py rerun 10/10 PASS (seed 20261018).

Every such $D$ raises the $c_0^-$ count by one, so $\operatorname{im} D$ never meets $\mathcal H_0^{\wedge 2}$: $\Lambda_2$ does not act on the present components of $\Sigma_2$ and no $D$ mixes $\Lambda_2$ with $\Lambda \otimes \Psi$. Relative to $\ker B_2$ a sector $\{m_1, m_2\}$ is transverse iff $\{D, B_2\} = f(m_1) + f(m_2)$ is invertible (for $D = c_0^- f(L_0^-)$); there $q = P^{\mathsf T} (q|_S) P$. The slice $\ker B_2$ is always larger than $\mathcal H_0^{\wedge 2}$ (five $c_0^-$ states in the level-matched sector of the model, all $b_0^-$-annihilated states with $L_0^- \neq 0$), and the per-strand slice is never complemented by $\operatorname{im} D$ ($\dim E^{(1)} = 2 \dim L^{(0)}$). The non-transverse sectors carry E1b directions (no gauge symmetry acts), or, for odd $f$, residual gauge directions inside the slice at total $L_0^- = 0$ off level matching ($f(m_1) = - f(m_2) \neq 0$), which never reach $\mathcal H_0^{\wedge 2}$. Neither yes/no rule discriminates: criterion (i) with the decoupled family, and the slice-relative rule, both give "yes" on $D = 0$; the literal "yes" rests on a coupling block between $\mathcal H_0^{\wedge 2}$ and the added directions which, with the source term extended by zero, generically breaks the elimination to $\mathcal V_4(\Psi^4)$ ($c' > \dim\ker p$ in every row). The only field-independent route to $\mathcal H_0^{\wedge 2}$ within record 102's block list is rows D1 + D2, and it requires the vertex on non-level-matched inputs to output states outside $\ker b_0^-$.

### R103 — Grading of the genus-zero IBL Maurer–Cartan equation: with CFL's weight the second component has ghost number 5, with the output-counting weight it is $\Sigma_2$ at ghost number 4 [Verified]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/103-ibl-mc-conventions.md`, claim-tree nodes C2–C7, blocks E, H1, Λ, R
- **Scope**: genus zero and $k,\ell\ge1$ (CFL's range), $k+\ell\le4$, $\mathfrak m$ with one- and two-string components; gradings only; $d=\tfrac12$ dictionary of R23 (forced at genus zero); $\hbar$ even, central, its degree a $\mathbb Z$-label; two homogeneity criteria (whole component, order by order in $\hbar$); gauge transformation taken of the form $[\hat{\mathfrak p},\lambda]$ for the grading of $\Lambda_n$ only; the conjugated form puts $\Sigma_2$ at genus label $-1$ in CFL's expansion, outside CFL (9.1)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-18 – exact check – `codes/verify-ibl-mc-conventions.py` C1, C2-C3 (+break), C4 (+break), C5, C5.3, C6, C7.1 (+break), TW; 18/18 PASS at seeds 20261018 and 20261019; support-constrained model ($\dim8$, ghosts $0$–$5$), both R87 families, measured ghost numbers
  - 2026-10-18 – exact check (fix pass) – same script, NV (non-vacuity), C5-ord (+break), C2-C3 with a perturbed-rule break leg; 42/42 PASS with the 19b checks at both seeds
  - 2026-10-18 – exact check (final fix pass) – same script, C5-ord-n (+break): dependence of the order-by-order criterion on the dressing $\hbar^{-1-n}$, whole-component value $5-n$; 46/46 PASS at both seeds
  - 2026-10-22: Fable check of the nodes changed after the last review (agent/reports/critic-gu-check-103a-20261022T193154.md): C5.4, A9 hold, dependents stand; closing sentence narrowed to its class and the S4 qualifiers added (fix passes agent/reports/computator-gu-103-fix3-20261022T195114.md, agent/reports/computator-gu-103-fix4-20261022T195734.md); codes/verify-ibl-mc-conventions.py rerun 46/46 PASS (seeds 20261018, 20261019).

For $\mathfrak m=\hbar^{-1}(\Psi_1+\Psi_2)$ (CFL Def. 9.1) and the genus-zero raised family, the $E_1$ component has terms $Q_B\Psi_1,\tfrac12\ell_2(\Psi_1^2),\tfrac16\ell_3(\Psi_1^3)$ at $\hbar^{-1}$ and $\ell_2(\Psi_2),\ell_3(\Psi_1,\Psi_2)$ at $\hbar^0$; the $E_2$ component has the lifts of $p_{1,1}$, $\ell_2(\Psi_1,\cdot)$, $\tfrac12\ell_3(\Psi_1^2,\cdot)$ on one strand of $\Psi_2$, $p_{1,2}(\Psi_1)$, $\tfrac12p_{2,2}(\Psi_1^2)$ at $\hbar^{-1}$ and $p_{2,2}(\Psi_2)$, the lift of $\ell_3(\Psi_2,\cdot)$ on a second $\Psi_2$ at $\hbar^0$; $g_s=\tau$ weights $g_s^{k+\ell-2}$. In CFL degree ($\lvert\hbar\rvert=1$) homogeneity requires $N_{\rm gh}(\Psi_2)=5$, $N_{\rm gh}(\Lambda_2)=4$. In physical ghost number with $\hbar$ and $g_s$ ghost-neutral: no value makes the whole components homogeneous ($E_1$ requires $4$; at $4$ every term of $E_L$ has $N_{\rm gh}=2L+\ell_p$, so $E_2$ splits into the one-output sector at $5$ and the two-output sector at $6$); order by order in $\hbar$ the unique value is $5$ (with $\Lambda_2$ at $4$), for $\Psi_2=\mathfrak m_{2,0}$; the order-by-order criterion depends on the dressing of the two-string component and fixes nothing for $\mathfrak m_{2,g\ge1}$ within the genus-zero truncation, while across orders $N_{\rm gh}(\mathfrak m_{2,g})=5+g$ with $N_{\rm gh}(\hbar)=-1$. The unique $N_{\rm gh}(\hbar)$ making each component homogeneous across $\hbar$-orders is $-1$; order by order every value works. Conjugating by $\hbar^{-L}$ on $E_LC$ turns CFL's weight $\hbar^{k+g-1}$ into $\hbar^{\ell+g-1}$, in which $(\Psi,\Sigma_2;\Lambda_1,\Lambda_2)$ at $(2,4;1,3)$ is homogeneous order by order for any $N_{\rm gh}(\hbar)$ and across orders for $N_{\rm gh}(\hbar)=-1$, and at genus zero the $\hbar$-order of a term is the number of outputs of its product minus one. Either way $N_{\rm gh}(\mathfrak m_{2,0})=4$ is excluded; as $\mathfrak m_{2,g\ge1}$ the value $4$ is excluded across orders ($5+g$) and unconstrained order by order within the genus-zero truncation. The rescaling and suspension caveat was checked among dictionaries that treat input and output strands alike and changes nothing. The obstruction is a property of the raised family: in Münster–Sachs' structure, whose two-output datum is $\omega_c^{-1}$, a two-string element at 4 is homogeneous with a neutral $\hbar$.

### R104 — excl. within local redefinitions $f=\alpha\Sigma_2+\beta\Psi^2$: the quartic-order equations of motion are not the components of CFL's Maurer–Cartan expression $e^{-\mathfrak m'}\hat{\mathfrak p}'(e^{\mathfrak m'})$ of the raised family; the components with coefficients, and the mismatch list with the baseline separated [mixed]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/103-ibl-mc-conventions.md#C8`, blocks MC, H2, V19b
- **Scope**: genus zero, $k,\ell\ge1$, $k+\ell\le4$ (through $O(g_s^2)$), components $E_1$–$E_4$; output-counting form of record 103 block R ($\mathfrak m'=\Psi+\Psi_2$, weight $\hbar^{\ell-1}g_s^{k+\ell-2}$, $N_{\rm gh}(\Psi,\Psi_2)=(2,4)$); field strands at ghost number $2$ ($\Sigma_2$ with $(2,2)$ strands); strand parity C6; ansatz A10 ($f$ linear in $\Sigma_2$, degree $\le2$ in $\Psi$, built from the family, power series in $g_s$, $\hbar$-independent; equivalently $f=\alpha\Sigma_2+\beta\Psi^2$); reading (I) checked, reading (III) checked at first order in the amplitude of a free solution, reading (II) argued; the exclusion uses no hypothesis; the reading of the components as a gauge-covariant Maurer–Cartan equation is conditional on PH42 (H-par, as a single operator on $EC$) and on the double-contraction relations, an open condition expected to fail for the genus-zero truncation unless genus-one terms $p_{k,\ell,1}$ are added; coefficients of $E_3$, $E_4$ by hand
- **Verification**:

  - 2026-10-18 – exact check (fix pass) – same script, C2-C3-E34 (+break): $E_3$, $E_4$ term list, powers and ghost numbers; 42/42 PASS at both seeds

- **Status**: reading (I) and the components (MC.5)–(MC.7) with coefficients – Verified (C8.1, C8.2 with break ladders); reading (III) at first order on a free solution – Verified for M-b (C8.2-shell), Argued for M-r0; reading (II) – Argued (degree-one counting of block H2); $E_3$, $E_4$ components (MC.8)–(MC.9) – term list and gradings Verified (C2-C3-E34), coefficients Argued (by hand); the baseline for any local $\Psi_2(\Psi)$ – Argued (counting); the reading of the components as a gauge-covariant Maurer–Cartan equation – Conditional on PH42 (H-par) and on the double-contraction relations, expected to fail at genus zero

The components of $e^{-\mathfrak m'}\hat{\mathfrak p}'(e^{\mathfrak m'})=0$ through $O(g_s^2)$ are $\mathcal M_1=Q_B\Psi+\tfrac{g_s}2\ell_2(\Psi^2)+\tfrac{g_s^2}{3!}\ell_3(\Psi^3)+g_s\ell_2(\Psi_2)+g_s^2\ell_3(\Psi,\Psi_2)$ (ghost $3$), $\mathcal M_2^{(5)}=\hat Q\Psi_2+g_s\widehat{\ell_2(\Psi,\cdot)}\Psi_2+\tfrac{g_s^2}2\widehat{\ell_3(\Psi,\Psi,\cdot)}\Psi_2+g_s^2\widehat{\ell_3(\Psi_2,\cdot)}\Psi_2$ (ghost $5$, $\hbar^0$; $\hat Q$ the lift of $p_{1,1}$, derived), $\mathcal M_2^{(6)}=g_sp_{1,2}(\Psi)+\tfrac{g_s^2}2p_{2,2}(\Psi^2)+g_s^2p_{2,2}(\Psi_2)$ (ghost $6$, $\hbar^1$), and the $E_3$, $E_4$ components (MC.8)–(MC.9), headed by $g_s^2\hbar^2p_{1,3}(\Psi)$ (ghost $9$); every relative coefficient $+$, given the operations of the raised family and C6 (no Koszul sign arises, all letters even; the signs of the operations are R87's). $\mathcal M_1$ and $\mathcal M_2^{(6)}$ are the Euler–Lagrange equations of $S_{\le4}(\Psi)+g_s\mathcal V_3(\Psi_2,\Psi)+\tfrac{g_s^2}2\mathcal V_4(\Psi_2,\Psi^2)+\tfrac{g_s^2}2\mathcal V_4(\Psi_2,\Psi_2)$. For no $\Psi_2=\alpha\Sigma_2+\beta\Psi^2$ ($\alpha,\beta$ power series in $g_s$) are $\mathcal E_\Psi$, $\mathcal E_\Sigma$ of `eq:hs4:eom` these components: $E_1$ needs $\alpha_0=0$ and $\beta_0=0$ (from $g_s\ell_2(\Psi_2)$) against $\alpha_0=\tfrac16$, $\beta_0=-\tfrac16$; the ghost-6 sector contains the $f$-independent $g_sp_{1,2}(\Psi)$, absent from $\mathcal E_\Sigma$, and without it requires $\alpha+\beta=-\tfrac12$ against $0$; the ghost-5 sector vanishes identically only for $f=0$; the components are not invariant under $\Sigma_2\to\Sigma_2+N$, $N\in\ker p_{2,2}$, for $\alpha\neq0$; $E_3$, $E_4$ have no counterpart. $g_sp_{1,2}(\Psi)$ and $g_s^2p_{1,3}(\Psi)$ are independent of $f$ and present at $\Psi_2=0$ (CFL Lemma 9.2): with $\Psi_2=0$, or any $\Psi_2(\Psi)$ built locally from the family, the original quartic closed SFT is not a Maurer–Cartan element of the raised family either; a $\Psi_2(\Psi)$ containing $p_{2,2}^{-1}$ removes M-b at the price of a modified kinetic operator of $\Psi$ (case (i)), and these baseline items measure the distance between the raised family and any tree-level closed SFT; the items specific to the Hubbard–Stratonovich form are the $\alpha$-part of $g_s\ell_2(\Psi_2)$, the coefficient mismatch, the ghost-5 sector, the $f$-dependent $E_3$, $E_4$ terms, the kernel non-invariance and the action-level sign. At first order in the amplitude of a free solution the Maurer–Cartan equation requires $p_{1,2}(\Psi_0)=0$, for any such $f$. In CFL's single-parity $E_2C$ at $d=\tfrac12$ the plain symmetric $\Sigma_2$ is zero; the comparison exists only in the C6 grading. These components are forced by CFL (2.1) on even words and are the Maurer–Cartan equation of any IBL reading of the raised family in the C6 grading; the exclusion does not depend on whether such a reading closes. Read as a gauge-covariant Maurer–Cartan equation, these components are conditional on PH42 (H-par, as a single operator on $EC$) and on the double-contraction relations, which fail for the genus-zero truncation unless genus-one terms $p_{k,\ell,1}$ are added. Exclusion scope: record 103, block H2, *Scope of the exclusion* (outside: $f$ with the propagator $p_{2,2}^{-1}$ or the $\Psi$ propagator, which remove $p_{1,2}$ of all or of the non-harmonic part of $\Psi$; a different family).

The former proposed entry "Parity of the strands in the symmetric algebra of the raised family" is withdrawn: it duplicated the registered convention C6 (critic 21b #3). Suggested addition to C6's *Checked on* line, for the orchestrator: record 103 check C8.1-par (raised outputs of $p_{1,2}(\Psi)$ graded-symmetric in the shifted parity, not the physical one; the physical quotient kills its $(3,3)$-strand part, the shifted quotient kills $\Sigma_2$).

### R105 — excl. within the linear transverse class: L1, the projector form $P^{\mathsf T} p P$ is the unique symmetric solution of the gauge-unfixing problem [Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/102-gauge-unfixing-structure.md#C1`
- **Scope**: the linear transverse class – $D$ field-independent, $\Lambda_2$ not acting on $\Psi$, $E = S \oplus \operatorname{im} D$, $q$ graded symmetric and homogeneous; $\ker p$ arbitrary; finite or infinite dimension with the algebraic direct sum; outside it: failures of transversality ($S \cap \operatorname{im} D \neq 0$, E1a; extra directions $W$ outside $S \oplus \operatorname{im} D$, E1b), field-dependent $D$ (E2); E3 does not evade it at a transverse locus with field-independent $D$
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-18 – exact check – `codes/verify-gauge-unfixing-structure.py` C1-search, seeds 20261018 and 7: 10 instances (even, $p$ degenerate and not; graded 3/4/5 with Koszul signs; $p = p_{2,2}$ of model M with $D$ random, $\dim \ker p = 1$ and 5), nullity 0, $P^{\mathsf T} p P$ an exact solution, $\ker q = \ker p \oplus \operatorname{im} D$, wrong-complement probe rejected
  - 2026-10-18 – exact check – same script, C1-break-sym (nullity $\dim S \cdot \operatorname{rank} D$) and C1-break-transverse (codimension: nullity 4; intersection: inconsistent unless $S \cap \operatorname{im} D \subseteq \ker p$, then nullity 0), FAIL as expected
  - 2026-10-18 – exact check – same script, C1.4-E1b-source: not a verification of any statement of this entry (its (L1.11) legs draw $\Psi^2$ as a free vector of $S$; check 102a #5). The non-transverse case with the source term (route E1b) is open.
  - 2026-10-22: Fable check (agent/reports/critic-gu-check-102a-20261022T193154.md): the E1b clause of C1.4 fails at the second condition of (L1.11) and is held out (the non-transverse case with the source term is open); the check C1.4-E1b-source does not support it; fix pass agent/reports/computator-gu-102-fix3-20261022T195114.md; codes/verify-gauge-unfixing-structure.py rerun 10/10 PASS.

Let $E = S \oplus \operatorname{im} D$, $D$ of ghost number $+1$ acting on the two-string parameter, $p$ a graded-symmetric form on the slice $S$ with $\ker p$ arbitrary, and $q$ a graded-symmetric form on $E$ with $q \circ D = 0$ and $q|_S = p$. Then $q(x, y) = p(Px, Py)$, $P$ the projector onto $S$ along $\operatorname{im} D$, and $\ker q = \ker p \oplus \operatorname{im} D$; conversely $P^{\mathsf T} p P$ satisfies the hypotheses. The enlarged quadratic term depends on $\Sigma_2$ only through $P\Sigma_2$, and the enlargement is the kernel shift of 012 §2 with kernel $\ker p \oplus \operatorname{im} D$. Proof: symmetry moves $q \circ D = 0$ to the second slot, so $q(x, y) = q(Px, Py)$. Each hypothesis is needed for the uniqueness of $q$: invariance of the quadratic term constrains only the symmetric part of $q$, to which L1 applies, and a non-symmetric first-slot solution differs by $f(Px, (1-P)y)$, an affine space of dimension $\dim S \cdot \operatorname{rank} D$, which cannot enter a gauge-invariant action (it drops from $q(\Sigma_2, \Psi^2)$, and invariance of $q(\Psi^2, \Sigma_2)$ forces $f(\Psi^2, \operatorname{im} D) = 0$); if $S \cap \operatorname{im} D \ne 0$, a solution exists iff the intersection lies in $\ker p$, so the gauge-fixed form degenerates there; if $S + \operatorname{im} D \ne E$, $q$ is free on the extra directions $W$. The non-transverse case with the source term (route E1b) is open. These relaxations change the value of $q$ and the gauge-fixed theory reproduced; none makes $\Lambda_2$ non-trivial (next entry). A worldsheet-defined $q$ (E3) gives back $P^{\mathsf T} p P$ wherever the slice is transverse and $D$ field-independent.

**Annotation (2026-10-26).** 2026-10-26: Harold's decision (knowledge/decisions.md, 2026-10-26, Workflow B follow-up): $p_{2,2}$ is the totally symmetric reading of $\mathcal V_4$, and a component of $q$ on two-string states that vanishes on every square $\Psi^2$ is data beyond $\mathcal V_4$ (route E3), not a redefinition of $p_{2,2}$. For a $q$ without such data the step that failed in record 102 ($q_{SS}=p$ on $S_\Psi$) holds by this definition, so the form on the slice is $p_{2,2}$; the open part of the non-transverse case with the source term is a $q$ carrying data beyond $\mathcal V_4$ (knowledge/open-problems.md, later project after workflow B, route 6).

### R106 — within field-independent $D$ and $\Lambda_2$ not acting on $\Psi$, every two-string parameter is a shift, transverse or not [Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/102-gauge-unfixing-structure.md#C1.5`
- **Scope**: $D$ linear and field-independent, $\Lambda_2$ field-independent, $\delta_{\Lambda_2} \Psi = 0$, $q$ graded symmetric with $q \circ D = 0$, any $E \supseteq S$ (transverse or not), source $q(\Sigma_2, \Psi^2)$, the composite $\Lambda$-law of $\Sigma_2$ or any $\Lambda$-law of the form composite $+ D\kappa$; outside: field-dependent $D$ or $\Lambda_2$ acting on $\Psi$ (E2)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-10-18 – none: two-line argument, eqs. (L1.8)–(L1.9) of record 102 (no machine check applies)
  - 2026-10-22: Fable check (agent/reports/critic-gu-check-102a-20261022T193154.md): C1.5 holds; scope narrowed (Λ₂ field-independent; the non-composite Λ-law is not a route within A3).

Within this class $\operatorname{im} D \subseteq \ker q$ on any $E$, by graded symmetry alone, so $\delta_{\Lambda_2} \Sigma_2 = D\Lambda_2$ is an exact symmetry of the enlarged quadratic and source terms, and with the composite law $\delta_\Lambda \Sigma_2 = 2 \, \delta_\Lambda \Psi \otimes \Psi$ the commutator $[\delta_\Lambda, \delta_{\Lambda_2}]$ vanishes on $\Psi$ and $\Sigma_2$: $\Lambda_2$ commutes with everything and is a shift, an enlargement of the kernel shifts. Failures of transversality (E1a, E1b) and a worldsheet origin of $q$ (E3) leave this intact. A non-trivial two-string parameter needs a field-dependent $D$ or $\Lambda_2$ acting on $\Psi$ (E2); a $\Lambda$-law of $\Sigma_2$ differing from the composite law by $D\kappa$ gives a commutator $-D\kappa(\Lambda, \Psi, D\Lambda_2)$ valued in $\operatorname{im} D$ and leaves $\Lambda_2$ a shift.

  - 2026-10-18 – exact check – same script, C3-break: symmetric $\mu$ and one-sided $\delta\Psi \propto \mathcal E_{\Sigma_2}$ are not symmetries; without the mixed $\mu^{\Psi\Sigma_2}$ entries the span has rank 4 (44) instead of 18 (54); without kernel shifts 18 instead of 54 (degenerate); FAIL as expected
  - 2026-10-18 – exact check – same script, C3.3-restriction: $S_{\rm el} = S_{\le4}$, a random solution restricted to the saddle is a symmetry of $S_{\le4}$ at weight $\le 4$ while not preserving $\Sigma_2 = \Psi^2$; on $\Sigma_2 = 2\Psi^2$ it is not (FAIL as expected)

In model M at $O(g_s^2)$, with the filtration weights 1 for $\Psi$ and $\Lambda$ and 2 for $\Sigma_2$ and $\Lambda_2$ (closed at total weight 4: every term of a degree-0 $\delta$ has non-negative shift, so the equations see $S_{\le 4}$ only), the solution space of $\delta\tilde S_{\le4}=0$ is $G + K + T + U$: the composite law $\delta\Sigma_2 = 2\,\delta\Psi\otimes\Psi$ of $\delta\Psi = \Lambda'(\chi + M\Psi)$ with a field-dependent $\Lambda'$, the kernel shifts $\delta\Sigma_2 \in \ker p$, the trivial symmetries $\mu^{ij}\mathcal E_j$ with $\mu$ antisymmetric over both equations of motion (including $\delta\Psi\propto\mathcal E_{\Sigma_2}$ with partner $\delta\Sigma_2\propto\mathcal E_\Psi$), and the coefficients $U$ the $O(g_s^2)$ action does not constrain. The $\Lambda_2$-dependent part of the solution space in the determined coefficients consists of kernel shifts, and $\delta\Psi = g_s\ell_2(\Lambda_2)$ admits no completion at weight $\le 4$ (it fails at weight 4). A symmetry restricted to the $\Sigma_2$ saddle is a symmetry of the eliminated action because $\partial_{\Sigma_2}\tilde S = 0$ there, whatever $\delta\Sigma_2$ does to the diagonal. The field-dependent gauge parameters are in $U$ because M is on-shell, and every $\delta\Sigma_2$ of shift $\ge 1$ because $\Sigma_2$ enters only the quartic term (as in closed SFT), so the entry checks the combined statement modulo $U$ and does not test the completeness step of the L2-SFT entry. Scope of the exclusion: the class is the present theory at the order where $\Sigma_2$ enters, in an on-shell finite model; outside it are the enlarged spaces of criterion 3a, $O(g_s^3)$, where M's unseen coefficients become constrained, and the generic-momentum regime; the weakest relaxation that evades it is a larger space for $\Sigma_2$.

### R108 — excl. within the present field content at $O(g_s^2)$: the $\Lambda_2$ sector, every two-string symmetry the action constrains is gauge, kernel shift or trivial; criterion 3a necessary at $O(g_s^2)$ [Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/102-gauge-unfixing-structure.md#C4.θ` (and C6)
- **Scope**: closed SFT at the algebraic layer, $O(g_s^2)$, present field content (Harold's decision: $\Sigma_2 \in (\mathcal H_2)^{\wedge 2}/\ker p_{2,2}$, level-matched; $q = p$, present source term); the $\Lambda_2$-linear part of symmetries polynomial in the fields, filtration weights 1 for $\Psi, \Lambda$ and 2 for $\Sigma_2, \Lambda_2$, equations at total weight $\le 4$; generic momentum: the total momentum of $\Lambda_2$'s strands and the momenta of the $\Psi$ legs, and their sums, off every mass shell; modulo $U_{\rm SFT}$ (every $\delta\Sigma_2$ of shift $\ge 1$, hence every level-matched $D$); no covariance restriction; outside: enlarged spaces (3a), $O(g_s^3)$ (E2), on-shell momenta

  - 2026-10-18 – exact check – `codes/verify-gauge-unfixing-structure.py` C4.theta-core, seeds 20261018 and 7: in an acyclic graded model, the solutions of $\langle A, Q\Psi\rangle = 0$ are $\operatorname{im} Q$ and the solutions of $\langle b\Psi, Q\Psi\rangle = 0$ are $\operatorname{im} Q$-valued maps plus $\mu\circ Q$ with $\mu$ antisymmetric (13 = 13); with a cohomology direction both fail (FAIL as expected); the linear-algebra core only
  - 2026-10-22: Fable check (agent/reports/critic-gu-check-102b-20261022T193154.md): C4.θ re-derived independently, C6 holds as a visibility statement.

In closed SFT at generic momentum at $O(g_s^2)$, with the present field content, the $\Lambda_2$-linear part of any symmetry is, modulo the components the $O(g_s^2)$ action does not see, a gauge transformation with a parameter built from $\Lambda_2$ (field-independent, $b_0^+ (L_0^+)^{-1}$ applied to the field-independent $\delta\Psi$, or linear in $\Psi$), plus a kernel shift, plus a trivial symmetry $\hat\mu(\Lambda_2) Q\Psi$ with $\hat\mu$ antisymmetric. Proof at total weight $\le 4$: weight 3 gives $Q\,\delta\Psi^{[1]} = 0$, so $\delta\Psi^{[1]} \in \operatorname{im} Q$ (no cohomology at generic momentum); the $\Lambda_2\Sigma_2$ monomials at weight 4 force the field-independent $\delta\Sigma_2$ into $\ker p$; the rest at weight 4 is $\langle \delta\Psi^{[2]}, Q\Psi\rangle = 0$, whose solutions are $\operatorname{im} Q$-valued maps plus $\mu\circ Q$ with $\mu$ antisymmetric, because $\langle\cdot, Q\cdot\rangle$ is non-degenerate on the Siegel complement of $\operatorname{im} Q$. No invertibility of $p$, no properness, no regularity hypothesis and no completeness theorem is used, and the per-strand grading is not used either. Hence a non-trivial two-string parameter needs a larger space for $\Sigma_2$ (criterion 3a) or content at $O(g_s^3)$ on the present space; this is a visibility statement (the present space exposes its $\Lambda_2$ content at this order only through $\delta\Psi$ at shifts 1–2 and a field-independent $\delta\Sigma_2$, while a larger space exposes a field-independent $D$ at weight 4), not evidence against the present space at $O(g_s^3)$. Scope of the exclusion: the class is the present field content at the order where $\Sigma_2$ enters, at generic momentum; outside are enlarged spaces, $O(g_s^3)$ and on-shell momenta; the weakest relaxation that evades it is a larger space for $\Sigma_2$.

In closed SFT at generic momentum at $O(g_s^2)$ with the present field content, a symmetry with a ghost-number-one single-string parameter built from $\Lambda_2$ and the fields restricts on the $\Sigma_2$ saddle to a symmetry of Zwiebach's truncated action; by the completeness of its gauge generators (properness of the master action, R62 at $k=2$, the regularity hypothesis H1, and the Henneaux–Teitelboim theorem) that restriction is a field-dependent gauge transformation plus a trivial symmetry; lifting back, a symmetry whose $\delta\Psi$ vanishes on the saddle is a trivial symmetry ($\delta\Psi\propto\mathcal E_{\Sigma_2}$, $\Sigma_2 - \Sigma_2^* = -\tfrac{4!}{2g_s^2}p^{-1}\mathcal E_{\Sigma_2}$, $p$ invertible on the quotient) plus a symmetry of the $\Sigma_2$ sector alone, which is trivial up to kernel shifts by Koszul acyclicity of the linear sequence $p(\Sigma_2 - \Psi^2)$, level by level. ### R111 — Kinetic operators of the multi-string fields have ghost number $n$; CFL Thm 6.1 puts the canonical model on $\mathrm{Sym}\,H(Q_B)$ with non-auxiliary multi-string directions; $\Sigma_2$ is auxiliary by an $L_\infty$ transfer (R41) and R73, not by Thm 6.1 [Conditional(PH1, PH5, PH14, PH27) – mixed]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/114-ibl-remarks-check.md`, nodes C2.1, C4.1–C4.5
- **Scope**: genus zero, ghost-neutral $g_s$; CFL Thm 6.1 as stated in `literature/reviews/RPEPEC4L.md` (PDF not cached); the IBL$_\infty$ side assumes CFL's axioms (integer $d$), which the raised family does not satisfy (R31), so (b) describes IBL$_\infty$ algebras, not the cubic action; (c) is $\Sigma_2$ only (quartic order for the classical complex, $k \in \{2,3\}$ for the BRST spectrum); the higher $\Sigma_n$ are expected to behave alike, not examined
- **Hypotheses**: (a), (b) none; (c) classical complex: PH1 through R41 (finite-dimensional model); BRST spectrum: PH1, PH5, PH14 [$O(g_s^4)$], PH27 through R73 and R62
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-11-07 – exact check – `codes/verify-ibl-remarks.py`, check C4.1 (Künneth for the derivation extension on $\mathrm{Sym}^2$, against $\mathrm{Sym}^2 H$ computed separately) with break leg (Koszul sign dropped), PASS, seed 20261107
(a) An $n$-string field of total ghost number $2n$, with a ghost-neutral coupling, has a kinetic operator of ghost number $n = N_{\rm gh}(p_{n,n})$, from $2N + N_{\rm gh}(K) = 5n$; an operator of ghost number one acting strand by strand (the extension $\hat Q_B$) is possible only at $n = 1$.
(b) By CFL Thm 6.1 (characteristic zero) and Künneth, the canonical model of an IBL$_\infty$ algebra with $\mathfrak p_{1,1,0} = Q_B$ lives on the Fock space $\mathrm{Sym}\,H(Q_B)$: only $\mathfrak p_{1,1}$ determines the one-particle states, but the multi-string directions carry $\hat Q_B$ and the sectors $\mathrm{Sym}^k H(Q_B)$, so they are not auxiliary.
(c) For $\Sigma_2$: under PH1, on the classical complex at quartic order, the pair ($\Sigma_2$, its equation of motion) is acyclic and its elimination is the $L_\infty$ homotopy transfer of R41, the inverse of the strictification; under PH1, PH5, PH14 [$O(g_s^4)$], PH27, the BRST cohomology of the cubic theory around a background equals that of the original theory (R73), so $\Sigma_2$ carries no physical state.
Harold's reading of Thm 6.1 (2026-11-07) holds in the form (b) for "only $Q_B$ carries physical states", and in the form (a)+(c) for "the other kinetic terms define auxiliary fields" for $\Sigma_2$, the latter not as a consequence of Thm 6.1.

### R114 — Composite vertices enter every reduced vertex with a minus sign and a positive coefficient in the diagram basis of the cubic theory; the sign depends on the diagram only after re-expansion in elementary vertices (first positive term at $n = 7$) [Proven]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/118-composite-vertex-sign.md`, claim tree C1–C4, eqs. (T.5), (R.1)–(R.3)
- **Scope**: tree level, formal power series in $g_s$, kernel conditions of section 3 (A1); leading-order values $\mathcal W_n(\Psi^n)$ for all $n \ge 6$; coefficients of the paper's displays taken as given (A3)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-11-09 – symbolic check – sympy one-liner (no companion script, brief `Resources: none`) – rational identities $6!/600$, $7!/1800$, $7!/3000$ and chain net $+\tfrac{42}{25}$ PASS; two-level toy elimination: all stationary-value monomials positive to $O(g^3)$ PASS, break leg ($W_7 \to -W_7$) fires

**Statement.** $\mathcal W_n(\Psi^n) = \mathcal V_n(\Psi^n) - \sum_T n!\,c_T\,T$, the sum over tree diagrams of $\widetilde S_{\le n-1}$ at order $g_s^{n-2}$ with the reduced vertices at their nodes and $\Sigma_k$ propagators (the exchange diagrams are subtracted only in this reading: with elementary vertices at the nodes the "composite vertex" $\mathcal V_{5\cdot5\cdot5}$ is added, below), $c_T > 0$ for every $T$: the sign is that of the auxiliary quadratic forms (negative), which makes every propagator of the eliminated action positive, while the odd couplings are positive (the sign of the sources drops out); reality and parity signs do not enter. In the elementary basis the coefficient is an inclusion–exclusion sum, and the chain $\mathcal V_{5\cdot5\cdot5}$ in $\mathcal W_7$ carries $+\tfrac{42}{25} = -\tfrac{42}{25} + \tfrac{14}{5}\cdot\tfrac65$, the first positive elementary composite term; $\mathcal V_{6\cdot5}$ carries $-\tfrac{14}{5}$. Consistent with R25 (2)–(3): the induced action $\tfrac{1}{1800} S_2 - \tfrac{1}{3000} S_1$ and the forced triple $(1, \tfrac{42}{25}, -\tfrac{14}{5})$.

### R115 — Auxiliary fields and physical states: no re-split within the cubic action's gauge symmetry, nor on the present field content at $O(g_s^2)$; the ghost-number argument for $p_{n,n}$ is gauge-independent; a re-split needs a $2$-string parameter acting on $\Psi$ on an enlarged content [Conditional(PH1, PH5, PH14 [$O(g_s^4)$], PH27, PH43) – Argued]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/119-aux-physical-dof-conjecture.md#C1`, `#C2`, `#C3`, `#C4`
- **Scope**: genus zero, cubic action at quartic order ($\Psi$, $\Sigma_2$), statements on $\Sigma_n$ by the same counting; "gauge transformation" read as ghost-number-preserving field redefinitions, BV canonical transformations, and the gauge fixing or un-fixing of a proper BV action (119 A3); C2 is Proven (arithmetic), C1 and C4 conditional through R73 and R111, C3 through R109 (PH43) and R110; the $\hat Q$-based statements of R18 are used because the question concerns the form with $Q_B$ on each strand (R18's annotation of 2026-10-17 stands)
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-11-09 – consistency check – ghost-number arithmetic and ledger cross-reading, record 119 *Checks*, by hand, PASS

Within the cubic action's own gauge symmetry (parameter $\Lambda\in\mathcal H$) no state moves out of $\Psi$: the flat fluctuation is inert (R60) and the orbits lie in the $\Psi$ sector; a representative with a $\hat\sigma_2$ component differs by an exact term (C1). The counting $N_{\rm gh}(K)=n$ for the kinetic operator of $\Sigma_n$ is invariant under every gauge transformation or ghost-number-preserving redefinition, so a strand-wise $Q_B$ enters only with a partner of ghost number $3n-1$ or in CFL's half-integer grading (C2). A parent in which the spectrum is re-split between $\Psi$ and $\Sigma_2$ must have a two-string parameter acting on $\Psi$ by an operator $D$ of ghost number $-1$, $g_s\ell_2(\Lambda_2)+\dots$ being the candidate fixed by the IBL law (R110); R106's class has $D=0$ and re-splits nothing, and R108 shows that, within the present field content at $O(g_s^2)$ and generic momentum, a $\Lambda_2$ acting on $\Psi$ moves nothing out of the $\Lambda$-orbits ($D(\Lambda_2)\in\operatorname{im}Q_B$), so a re-split needs the enlarged content (ghost-$5$ partner, enlarged $\Sigma_2$ sectors), $O(g_s^3)$ or on-shell momenta; the classes a $\Psi$-gauge would move, if any, are non-generic (C3). The IBL formulation is not a gauge-unfixed member of the family: a proper parent and its gauge fixing share their BRST cohomology, and its multi-string sectors carry $\mathrm{Sym}^kH(Q_B)$, not a re-split of $H(Q^{(k)}_{\Psi_0})$ (C4). The paragraph `07-ibl.tex` l. 70–75 compares field contents, not gauges, and stands.

### R116 — Degree dictionary paper / CFL / Münster–Sachs for the $(m,n)$ products: CFL's affine fit is $3 - N_{\rm gh}$ with $d = \tfrac12$, Münster–Sachs use $d = 0$ via $A_c = s^{-2}\tilde A_c$ [Verified]

- **Source**: `agent/derivations/120-muenster-degree-dictionary.md#C2`, `#C4`, `#C5`, `#C6`, `#C7`
- **Scope**: genus zero, products read from the fundamental vertices with inputs at ghost number 2 and outputs at 3 (physical ghost numbers, C1 pairing); strand gradings that are functions of the ghost number (only $N = 2, 3$ enter, so "affine" is no restriction); degrees only, not the IBL relations; the Münster–Sachs grading is cited (MS2013 App. A.2, sec. IV.B, V.A; MS2014 l. 66, footnote 2, App. A.2), not machine-checked; $\omega_c = \pm\Omega$ assumes their bpz form differs from C1's by a sign depending on the parities only, and the overall sign is not fixed by their text
- **Verification**:
  - 2026-11-09 – exact check – `codes/verify-muenster-degree.py` C1, C2, C2-numeric, C4, C4-cfl, C5, C6, C7-sign, C7-tensor, 9/9 PASS; C2-break (integer $d$, `--perturb`) FAILS as required; C7-tensor recoded after review (the first version was tautological: both sides coded by the same expression): the Koszul sign of $\Omega^{\otimes n}$ is now computed by reordering the $2n$ factors and compared with the exponent of `eq:conv:pairing-n`, $n = 2, 3, 4$, with the transposed exponent required to disagree

With a strand degree $\delta = \alpha N_{\rm gh} + \beta$, an $(m,n)$ product has degree $n(3\alpha+\beta) - m(2\alpha+\beta)$ ($3n - 2m$ in the paper). CFL's $|\mathfrak p_{k,\ell,g}| = -2d(k+g-1) - 1$ (R5) is reproduced at genus zero by exactly one strand grading that is a function of the ghost number, $\delta = 3 - N_{\rm gh}$ (a $C[1]$-degree), with $d = \tfrac12$; no integer $d$ admits a fit, and no such grading makes the degree independent of both $m$ and $n$. Münster–Sachs (arXiv:1109.4101 App. A.2, sec. IV.B, V.A; arXiv:1208.5626 App. A.2, footnote 2) grade $A_c = s^{-2}\tilde A_c$ (strand degree $N_{\rm gh} - 2$, string field of degree 0, $\omega_c$ of degree $-1$, no degree on $\hbar$): every structure map $l_n^g$ and $\omega_c^{-1}$ has degree $+1$, i.e. CFL's $d = 0$ with the cohomological sign, in the filtered extension ($k = 0$ operations). The paper's $p_{m,n}$ have degree $n$ in their grading, the degree of $l_{m+n-1}\circ(\omega_c^{-1})^{n-1}$, and are not structure maps of the loop homotopy algebra. Their even shift carries no Koszul sign; $\omega_c = \pm\Omega$ (up to the parity-sign reading above) with $\Omega(A,B) = (-1)^{|A|}\langle A,B\rangle$ (conventions C1), so their raising corresponds to the paper's $p^{\Omega}_{m,n}$ family, equal to $p_{m,n}$ on even inputs.
